DockSmith: Scaling Reliable Coding Environments via an Agentic Docker Builder
Authors: Jiaran Zhang, Luck Ma, Fanqi Wan, Di Qi, Xu Zhao, Jieyi Hou, Zhe Xie, Mengqiang Ren, Xin Wu, Zhewei Huang, Liangyu Chen, Qi Han, Xiangyu Zhang
First: 2026-01-31T08:16:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:59:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable Docker-based environment construction is a dominant bottleneck for scaling execution-grounded training and evaluation of software engineering agents. We introduce DockSmith, a specialized agentic Docker builder designed to address this challenge. DockSmith treats environment construction not only as a preprocessing step, but as a core agentic capability that exercises long-horizon tool use, dependency reasoning, and failure recovery, yielding supervision that transfers beyond Docker building itself. DockSmith is trained on large-scale, execution-grounded Docker-building trajectories produced by a SWE-Factory-style pipeline augmented with a loop-detection controller and a cross-task success memory. Training a 30B-A3B model on these trajectories achieves open-source state-of-the-art performance on Multi-Docker-Eval, with 39.72% Fail-to-Pass and 58.28% Commit Rate. Moreover, DockSmith improves out-of-distribution performance on SWE-bench Verified, SWE-bench Multilingual, and Terminal-Bench 2.0, demonstrating broader agentic benefits of environment construction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DockSmith：通过代理式Docker构建器实现可靠编码环境的扩展
可靠的基于Docker的环境构建是扩展软件工程代理执行导向训练与评估的主要瓶颈。我们引入了DockSmith，一种专门设计的代理式Docker构建器，旨在解决这一挑战。DockSmith不仅将环境构建视为预处理步骤，更将其视为核心代理能力，涉及长期工具使用、依赖推理和故障恢复，从而产生超越Docker构建本身的迁移监督。DockSmith在由SWE-Factory风格流水线增强的循环检测控制器和跨任务成功记忆生成的大规模执行导向Docker构建轨迹上进行训练。在这些轨迹上训练30B-A3B模型，在Multi-Docker-Eval上达到开源最先进性能，失败率39.72%，提交率58.28%。此外，DockSmith在SWE-bench Verified、SWE-bench Multilingual和Terminal-Bench 2.0上的分布外性能也得到提升，展示了环境构建更广泛的代理益处。
Summary / 总结
To address the bottleneck of reliable Docker environment construction in scaling software engineering agents, DockSmith was developed as an agentic Docker builder that treats environment building as a core capability requiring long-horizon tool use, dependency reasoning, and failure recovery. Trained on large-scale execution-grounded Docker-building trajectories generated by a SWE-Factory pipeline enhanced with loop detection and cross-task memory, a 30B-A3B model achieved state-of-the-art results on Multi-Docker-Eval with 39.72% Fail-to-Pass and 58.28% Commit Rate. Additionally, DockSmith demonstrated improved out-of-distribution performance on SWE-bench Verified, SWE-bench Multilingual, and Terminal-Bench 2.0, indicating broader agentic benefits from mastering environment construction.
为解决软件工程代理规模化训练中Docker环境构建的瓶颈问题，DockSmith提出一种代理式Docker构建器，将环境构建视为核心能力，需进行长时程工具调用、依赖推理与故障恢复。该模型在SWE-Factory流水线增强版（含循环检测与跨任务记忆）生成的大规模执行轨迹上训练，30B-A3B模型在Multi-Docker-Eval上达到SOTA，Fail-to-Pass为39.72%，Commit Rate为58.28%；同时在SWE-bench Verified、Multilingual及Terminal-Bench 2.0等分布外任务中表现更优，表明环境构建能力具有更广泛的代理价值。
Recursive Multi-Agent Systems
Authors: Xiyuan Yang, Jiaru Zou, Rui Pan, Ruizhong Qiu, Pan Lu, Shizhe Diao, Jindong Jiang, Hanghang Tong, Tong Zhang, Markus J. Buehler, Jingrui He, James Zou
First: 2026-04-28T17:59:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:59:34+00:00
Comments: 36 Pages. Project Website: https://recursivemas.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recursive or looped language models have recently emerged as a new scaling axis by iteratively refining the same model computation over latent states to deepen reasoning. We extend such scaling principle from a single model to multi-agent systems, and ask: Can agent collaboration itself be scaled through recursion? To this end, we introduce RecursiveMAS, a recursive multi-agent framework that casts the entire system as a unified latent-space recursive computation. RecursiveMAS connects heterogeneous agents as a collaboration loop through the lightweight RecursiveLink module, enabling in-distribution latent thoughts generation and cross-agent latent state transfer. To optimize our framework, we develop an inner-outer loop learning algorithm for iterative whole-system co-optimization through shared gradient-based credit assignment across recursion rounds. Theoretical analyses of runtime complexity and learning dynamics establish that RecursiveMAS is more efficient than standard text-based MAS and maintains stable gradients during recursive training. Empirically, we instantiate RecursiveMAS under 4 representative agent collaboration patterns and evaluate across 9 benchmarks spanning mathematics, science, medicine, search, and code generation. In comparison with advanced single/multi-agent and recursive computation baselines, RecursiveMAS consistently delivers an average accuracy improvement of 8.3%, together with 1.2$\times$-2.4$\times$ end-to-end inference speedup, and 34.6%-75.6% token usage reduction. Code and Data are provided in https://recursivemas.github.io.
中文标题/摘要
标题：递归多智能体系统
递归或循环语言模型近期作为一种新的扩展轴应运而生，通过在潜在状态上迭代优化同一模型计算以深化推理。我们将这一扩展原则从单模型推广至多智能体系统，并提出问题：智能体协作本身是否可以通过递归进行扩展？为此，我们引入RecursiveMAS，一种递归多智能体框架，将整个系统视为统一的潜在空间递归计算。RecursiveMAS通过轻量级RecursiveLink模块将异构智能体连接为协作循环，实现分布内潜在思维生成与跨智能体潜在状态传递。为优化该框架，我们开发了一种内外循环学习算法，通过跨递归轮次共享梯度的信用分配，实现系统整体协同优化。理论分析表明，RecursiveMAS在运行时复杂度和学习动态上优于标准文本型多智能体系统，并在递归训练中保持梯度稳定。实证上，我们在4种代表性智能体协作模式下实例化RecursiveMAS，并在涵盖数学、科学、医学、搜索和代码生成的9个基准上进行评估。与先进的单/多智能体及递归计算基线相比，RecursiveMAS持续实现平均准确率提升8.3%，端到端推理速度提升1.2至2.4倍，且token使用量减少34.6%至75.6%。代码与数据见https://recursivemas.github.io。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the success of recursive language models in deepening reasoning through iterative latent-state refinement, this work explores whether agent collaboration can similarly be scaled via recursion. The authors propose RecursiveMAS, a framework that recursively computes across a unified latent space, connecting heterogeneous agents via a lightweight RecursiveLink module to enable shared latent thought generation and state transfer. An inner-outer loop learning algorithm facilitates co-optimization across recursion rounds using gradient-based credit assignment. Theoretical analysis confirms efficiency and gradient stability, while empirical evaluation across four collaboration patterns and nine benchmarks shows RecursiveMAS outperforms baselines by 8.3% average accuracy, achieves 1.2x–2.4x inference speedup, and reduces token usage by 34.6%–75.6%.
受递归语言模型通过迭代优化潜状态提升推理能力的启发，本研究探索是否可通过递归方式扩展多智能体协作的规模。作者提出RecursiveMAS框架，将多智能体系统统一为递归潜空间计算，通过轻量级RecursiveLink模块连接异构智能体，实现共享思维生成与状态传递。采用内-外循环学习算法，通过共享梯度信用分配实现系统级协同优化。理论分析表明该框架效率更高且训练梯度稳定，实验证明在四种协作模式和九个基准任务上，RecursiveMAS平均准确率提升8.3%，推理速度提升1.2–2.4倍，token使用量减少34.6%–75.6%。
DV-World: Benchmarking Data Visualization Agents in Real-World Scenarios
Authors: Jinxiang Meng, Shaoping Huang, Fangyu Lei, Jingyu Guo, Haoxiang Liu, Jiahao Su, Sihan Wang, Yao Wang, Enrui Wang, Ye Yang, Hongze Chai, Jinming Lv, Anbang Yu, Huangjing Zhang, Yitong Zhang, Yiming Huang, Zeyao Ma, Shizhu He, Jun Zhao, Kang Liu
First: 2026-04-28T17:58:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:58:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Real-world data visualization (DV) requires native environmental grounding, cross-platform evolution, and proactive intent alignment. Yet, existing benchmarks often suffer from code-sandbox confinement, single-language creation-only tasks, and assumption of perfect intent. To bridge these gaps, we introduce DV-World, a benchmark of 260 tasks designed to evaluate DV agents across real-world professional lifecycles. DV-World spans three domains: DV-Sheet for native spreadsheet manipulation including chart and dashboard creation as well as diagnostic repair; DV-Evolution for adapting and restructuring reference visual artifacts to fit new data across diverse programming paradigms and DV-Interact for proactive intent alignment with a user simulator that mimics real-world ambiguous requirements. Our hybrid evaluation framework integrates Table-value Alignment for numerical precision and MLLM-as-a-Judge with rubrics for semantic-visual assessment. Experiments reveal that state-of-the-art models achieve less than 50% overall performance, exposing critical deficits in handling the complex challenges of real-world data visualization. DV-World provides a realistic testbed to steer development toward the versatile expertise required in enterprise workflows. Our data and code are available at \href{https://github.com/DA-Open/DV-World}{this project page}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DV-World：在真实场景中基准测试数据可视化代理
真实世界的数据可视化（DV）需要原生环境感知、跨平台演化和主动意图对齐。然而，现有基准测试常受限于代码沙箱、单语言创建任务及假设完美意图。为弥合这些差距，我们引入DV-World，一个包含260项任务的基准，旨在评估DV代理在真实世界专业生命周期中的表现。DV-World涵盖三个领域：DV-Sheet用于原生电子表格操作，包括图表与仪表板创建及诊断修复；DV-Evolution用于在不同编程范式中适应并重构参考视觉资产以适配新数据；DV-Interact则通过模拟真实世界模糊需求的用户模拟器实现主动意图对齐。我们的混合评估框架结合表格值对齐以确保数值精度，并采用MLLM作为裁判配合评分标准进行语义-视觉评估。实验表明，当前最先进模型的整体表现低于50%，暴露出在处理真实世界数据可视化复杂挑战方面的关键缺陷。DV-World提供了一个现实的测试平台，引导开发向企业工作流所需的多样化能力迈进。我们的数据与代码可在\href{https://github.com/DA-Open/DV-World}{此项目页面}获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for realistic evaluation of data visualization agents in professional settings, DV-World introduces a benchmark of 260 tasks across three domains—DV-Sheet for spreadsheet and chart manipulation, DV-Evolution for cross-platform adaptation of visual artifacts, and DV-Interact for user-intent alignment—designed to reflect real-world workflows. The benchmark employs a hybrid evaluation framework combining Table-value Alignment for numerical accuracy and MLLM-as-a-Judge with rubrics for semantic-visual quality. Experimental results show that current state-of-the-art models achieve under 50% overall performance, highlighting significant gaps in handling complex, multi-faceted visualization challenges in real-world scenarios.
为评估数据可视化代理在真实专业场景中的能力，DV-World 设计了涵盖260个任务的基准测试，分为三个领域：DV-Sheet 用于表格与图表操作，DV-Evolution 用于跨平台视觉资产的适配重构，DV-Interact 用于与用户模拟器协同应对模糊需求。该基准采用混合评估框架，结合表格值对齐评估数值精度与基于多模态大模型的语义-视觉评分。实验表明，当前最先进模型整体准确率低于50%，暴露出在处理现实复杂性、模糊意图与多模态推理方面存在显著缺陷，亟需向企业级工作流所需的多样化能力演进。
BayesL: a Logical Framework for the Verification of Bayesian Networks
Authors: Stefano M. Nicoletti, E. Moritz Hahn, Mariëlle Stoelinga
First: 2025-06-30T12:18:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:54:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern explainable AI still struggles with a fundamental gap: although Bayesian networks (BNs) provide transparent probabilistic structure, there is no unified way to formally express, query, and verify what these models imply. Analysts often rely on ad hoc queries, manual interventions, or informal reasoning to explore causal relations and hypothetical scenarios, making it difficult to systematically validate model behaviour, uncover hidden assumptions, and guarantee reliability. We introduce BayesL (pronounced Basil), a logical framework for specifying, querying, and verifying the behaviour of BNs. BayesL is a structured language that supports both probabilistic inference queries (e.g., marginal, conditional, MAP) and model-checking-style queries that specify formal properties of BN behaviour. It facilitates versatile reasoning over causal and evidential relationships, including counterfactual what-if scenarios via conditional probability tables updates, without requiring manual modifications to the model. In addition to graph structure reasoning and inference, BayesL enables the formal specification of properties, supported by dedicated model checking algorithms and a preliminary open-source implementation. By allowing inference and verification within a single formal language, BayesL establishes a white-box verification paradigm in which model structure, assumptions, and reasoning processes are explicitly encoded and systematically checked. We demonstrate this through two diagnostic case studies and a benchmark set of BN models, showing how BayesL clarifies BN behaviour in a precise and analyzable way, advancing the transparency, trustworthiness, and practical explainability of BN-based systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BayesL：用于贝叶斯网络验证的逻辑框架
现代可解释人工智能仍面临一个根本性难题：尽管贝叶斯网络（BNs）提供了透明的概率结构，但尚无统一方式形式化表达、查询和验证这些模型所蕴含的含义。分析人员常依赖临时查询、手动干预或非正式推理来探索因果关系和假设场景，这使得系统性验证模型行为、揭示隐含假设并保证可靠性变得困难。我们引入BayesL（发音为Basil），一种用于指定、查询和验证BN行为的逻辑框架。BayesL是一种结构化语言，支持概率推理查询（如边缘、条件、MAP）和模型检测风格的查询，用于形式化描述BN行为的属性。它支持对因果和证据关系的多样化推理，包括通过条件概率表更新实现反事实“假设情景”，无需手动修改模型。除图结构推理和推理外，BayesL还支持属性的形式化指定，辅以专用模型检测算法和初步开源实现。通过在单一形式语言内实现推理与验证，BayesL确立了白盒验证范式，其中模型结构、假设和推理过程被明确编码并系统性检查。我们通过两个诊断案例研究和一组BN模型基准测试，展示了BayesL如何以精确且可分析的方式阐明BN行为，从而提升基于BN系统的透明度、可信度和实际可解释性。
Summary / 总结
BayesL addresses the lack of formal methods for querying and verifying Bayesian networks by introducing a logical framework that unifies probabilistic inference and model-checking. It enables precise specification of causal and evidential relationships, including counterfactual scenarios, without altering the BN structure. Experimental results from diagnostic case studies and benchmark models show BayesL effectively clarifies model behavior, supports systematic validation, and enhances transparency and trust in BN-based systems.
BayesL 解决了贝叶斯网络缺乏形式化查询与验证方法的问题，传统方法常依赖非正式或手动推理。它提出一个逻辑框架，支持概率推理（如边缘、条件、MAP）与模型检测查询，用于形式化指定和验证网络行为，包括通过条件概率表更新实现反事实场景，无需修改模型结构。在两个诊断案例和一组贝叶斯网络基准模型上的实验表明，BayesL 能够精确、可分析地验证模型行为，提升贝叶斯网络系统的透明度与可靠性。
How Fast Should a Model Commit to Supervision? Training Reasoning Models on the Tsallis Loss Continuum
Authors: Chu-Cheng Lin, Eugene Ie
First: 2026-04-28T17:52:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:52:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Adapting reasoning models to new tasks during post-training with only output-level supervision stalls under reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) when the initial success probability $p_0$ is small. Using the Tsallis $q$-logarithm, we define a loss family $J_Q$ that interpolates between RLVR (at $q{=}0$, the exploitation pole) and the log-marginal-likelihood over latent trajectories (at $q{=}1$, the density-estimation pole). All members share the same per-example gradient direction, differing only by a scalar amplification $P_{θ^{-q}}$ that reweights each instance independently of the learning rate. This amplification is the mechanism that addresses cold-start stalling: under gradient flow, the exploitation pole requires $Ω(\frac{1}{p_0})$ time to escape cold start, while the density-estimation pole escapes in $Θ\big(\log(\frac{1}{p_0})\big)$; intermediate $q$ trades escape speed against noise memorization. Because $P_θ$ is intractable, we derive two Monte Carlo estimators from the two factorizations of the gradient: Gradient-Amplified RL (GARL) samples from the prior and amplifies the RL gradient, and Posterior-Attenuated Fine-Tuning (PAFT) importance-resamples from the posterior and runs standard SFT. Both have bias $O\big(\frac{q}{M P_θ^{q+1}}\big)$; GARL has lower variance, PAFT has semantically coherent gradients. On FinQA, HotPotQA, and MuSiQue, GARL at $q{=}0.75$ substantially mitigates cold-start stalling, escaping cold start where GRPO fails entirely. In warm start, GARL at low $q$ dominates FinQA where training is stable; on HotPotQA and MuSiQue, GARL destabilizes during training, and PAFT at $q{=}0.75$ provides stable gradients (best overall on HotPotQA at 47.9 maj@16, $+14.4$ over GRPO).
中文标题/摘要
标题：模型应在何时承诺监督？在Tsallis损失连续体上训练推理模型
在后训练阶段仅使用输出层监督时，当初始成功概率 $p_0$ 较小时，推理模型适应新任务会因可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）而停滞。我们利用Tsallis $q$-对数，定义了一族损失函数 $J_Q$，在 $q{=}0$（利用极点）时对应RLVR，在 $q{=}1$（密度估计极点）时对应潜变量轨迹的对数边缘似然。所有成员共享相同的每样本梯度方向，仅通过标量放大因子 $P_{θ^{-q}}$ 区别，该因子独立于学习率重新加权每个实例。该放大机制解决了冷启动停滞问题：在梯度流下，利用极点需 $Ω(\frac{1}{p_0})$ 时间才能脱离冷启动，而密度估计极点则需 $Θ\big(\log(\frac{1}{p_0})\big)$；中间 $q$ 值在逃离速度与噪声记忆之间进行权衡。由于 $P_θ$ 不可计算，我们从梯度的两种分解中推导出两个蒙特卡洛估计器：梯度放大强化学习（GARL）从先验采样并放大RL梯度；后验衰减微调（PAFT）从后验重要性重采样并运行标准SFT。两者偏差均为 $O\big(\frac{q}{M P_θ^{q+1}}\big)$；GARL方差更低，PAFT梯度语义更连贯。在FinQA、HotPotQA和MuSiQue数据集上，GARL在 $q{=}0.75$ 时显著缓解冷启动停滞，而GRPO完全失败。在暖启动下，低 $q$ 的GARL在FinQA上表现稳定；在HotPotQA和MuSiQue上，GARL训练不稳定，而PAFT在 $q{=}0.75$ 时提供稳定梯度（在HotPotQA上以47.9 maj@16取得最佳整体表现，比GRPO高14.4）
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of cold-start stalling in reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) for reasoning models when initial success probability is low. It introduces a Tsallis loss family interpolating between RLVR (q=0) and log-marginal-likelihood (q=1), with gradient directions preserved but scaled by instance-dependent amplification factors that adjust escape speed from cold start. Two Monte Carlo estimators, GARL and PAFT, approximate this scaling: GARL samples from prior and amplifies RL gradients, while PAFT resamples from posterior and runs standard supervised fine-tuning. GARL at q=0.75 significantly reduces cold-start stalling on FinQA, HotPotQA, and MuSiQue, outperforming GRPO; in warm-start settings, GARL with low q performs best on FinQA, while PAFT at q=0.75 provides stable gradients on HotPotQA, achieving 47.9 maj@16 (+14.4 over GRPO).
该论文针对初始成功率较低时强化学习从可验证奖励（RLVR）在推理模型中出现的冷启动停滞问题，提出了一种基于Tsallis熵的损失族，其在q=0时对应RLVR，q=1时对应对潜变量轨迹的对数边缘似然，梯度方向一致但通过标量因子重新加权样本。为近似不可计算的放大因子，提出两种蒙特卡洛估计器：梯度放大强化学习（GARL）从先验采样并放大RL梯度，后验衰减微调（PAFT）从后验重要性重采样并运行标准SFT。GARL在FinQA和HotPotQA上显著缓解冷启动停滞，优于GRPO；在HotPotQA和MuSiQue上，PAFT提供更稳定的梯度，取得最佳性能（47.9 maj@16，比GRPO高14.4），而GARL在暖启动场景下表现更优。
A paradox of AI fluency
Authors: Christopher Potts, Moritz Sudhof
First: 2026-04-28T17:51:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:51:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
How much does a user's skill with AI shape what AI actually delivers for them? This question is critical for users, AI product builders, and society at large, but it remains underexplored. Using a richly annotated sample of 27K transcripts from WildChat-4.8M, we show that fluent users take on more complex tasks than novices and adopt a fundamentally different interactional mode: they iterate collaboratively with the AI, refining goals and critically assessing outputs, whereas novices take a passive stance. These differences lead to a paradox of AI fluency: fluent users experience more failures than novices -- but their failures tend to be visible (a direct consequence of their engagement), they are more likely to lead to partial recovery, and they occur alongside greater success on complex tasks. Novices, by contrast, more often experience invisible failures: conversations that appear to end successfully but in fact miss the mark. Taken together, these results reframe what success with AI depends on. Individuals should adopt a stance of active engagement rather than passive acceptance. AI product builders should recognize that they are designing not just model behavior but user behavior; encouraging deep engagement, rather than friction-free experiences, will lead to more success overall. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/bigspinai/bigspin-fluency-outcomes
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI流利度的悖论
用户的AI技能水平如何影响AI实际为其提供的服务？这一问题对用户、AI产品开发者及整个社会都至关重要，但尚未得到充分探讨。通过对WildChat-4.8M数据集中2.7万个标注丰富的对话转录样本进行分析，我们发现：熟练用户承担更复杂的任务，并采用截然不同的互动模式——他们与AI协作迭代，不断调整目标并批判性评估输出；而新手则采取被动立场。这种差异导致了AI流利度的悖论：熟练用户遭遇的失败比新手更多——但这些失败往往可见（是其积极参与的直接结果），更可能实现部分恢复，且常伴随复杂任务上的更大成功。相比之下，新手更多经历“隐形失败”：看似成功结束的对话，实则偏离目标。综合来看，这些结果重新定义了AI成功的要素：个体应采取主动参与而非被动接受的态度；AI产品开发者应意识到，他们设计的不仅是模型行为，更是用户行为；鼓励深度参与而非无摩擦体验，将带来整体更佳成效。我们的代码与数据可在https://github.com/bigspinai/bigspin-fluency-outcomes获取。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how user fluency with AI influences interaction outcomes, motivated by the need to understand whether skilled users receive better AI performance. Analyzing 27K annotated transcripts from WildChat-4.8M, researchers found that fluent users engage in iterative, collaborative interactions with AI, refining goals and critically evaluating outputs, while novices adopt passive stances. Despite experiencing more visible failures, fluent users achieve greater success on complex tasks and are more likely to recover from errors, whereas novices often encounter invisible failures—conversations that appear successful but miss the target. The findings suggest that active engagement, not passive acceptance, is key to AI success, and that AI product design should encourage deep user involvement rather than frictionless interactions.
本研究探讨用户对AI的熟练程度如何影响交互结果，揭示了一个悖论：熟练用户通过主动协作、反复调整目标和批判性评估输出，虽然失败更明显，却在复杂任务上取得更高成功率；而新手被动接受AI结果，失败往往‘隐形’——对话看似成功实则偏离目标。基于27K条标注对话数据，研究发现熟练用户的深度互动虽导致更多可恢复的失败，但整体任务完成度更高，提示用户与AI设计者应重视主动参与而非被动接受，以实现更优成果。
Teacher Forcing as Generalized Bayes: Optimization Geometry Mismatch in Switching Surrogates for Chaotic Dynamics
Authors: Andre Herz, Daniel Durstewitz, Georgia Koppe
First: 2026-04-28T17:50:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:50:37+00:00
Comments: Presented at the Workshop on Optimization and Post-Bayesian Inference in Machine Learning, AISTATS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Identity teacher forcing (ITF) enables stable training of deterministic recurrent surrogates for chaotic dynamical systems and has been highly effective for dynamical systems reconstruction (DSR) with recurrent neural networks (RNNs), including interpretable almost-linear RNNs (AL-RNNs). However, as an intervention-based prediction loss (and thus a generalized Bayes update), teacher forcing need not match the free-running model's marginal likelihood geometry. We compare the objective-induced curvatures of ITF and marginal likelihood in a probabilistic switching augmentation of AL-RNNs, estimating ambiguity-aware observed information via Louis' identity. In the switching setting studied here, conditioning on a single forced regime path (as ITF does) inflates curvature, while marginal likelihood curvature is reduced by a missing-information correction when multiple switching explanations remain plausible. In Lorenz-63 experiments, windowed evidence fine-tuning improves held-out evidence but can degrade dynamical quantities of interest (QoIs) relative to ITF-pretrained models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：教师强制作为广义贝叶斯：混沌动力系统切换代理中的优化几何不匹配
身份教师强制（ITF）可稳定训练混沌动力系统的确定性递归代理，并在递归神经网络（RNN）的动态系统重建（DSR）中表现出色，包括可解释的几乎线性RNN（AL-RNN）。然而，作为基于干预的预测损失（因此是广义贝叶斯更新），教师强制无需匹配自由运行模型的边缘似然几何。我们在AL-RNN的概率切换增强框架中比较了ITF与边缘似然的客观诱导曲率，通过Louis恒等式估计具有模糊感知的观测信息。在本研究的切换设定中，对单一强制模式路径的条件化（如ITF所做）会放大曲率，而边缘似然曲率则因多个切换解释仍具合理性时的缺失信息校正而降低。在Lorenz-63实验中，窗口证据微调可提升保留证据，但可能相对ITF预训练模型降低动力学关键量（QoIs）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand why teacher forcing (TF) stabilizes training of recurrent neural networks for chaotic dynamics, this work frames TF as a generalized Bayes update and investigates its mismatch with the true marginal likelihood geometry. Using a probabilistic switching augmentation of almost-linear RNNs, the authors compare the curvature of the objective induced by TF versus the marginal likelihood, estimating observed information via Louis' identity. Results show that TF inflates curvature by conditioning on a single forced regime path, while marginal likelihood curvature is reduced by a missing-information correction when multiple switching explanations are plausible. In Lorenz-63 experiments, TF-pretrained models outperform windowed evidence fine-tuning in terms of held-out evidence and dynamical quantities of interest.
为理解为何教师强制（TF）能稳定训练用于混沌动力系统建模的循环神经网络，尽管其目标函数与真实边际似然几何不匹配，本文分析了TF与边际似然在切换增强的AL-RNN中曲率的差异。通过Louis恒等式估计模糊感知的观测信息，研究比较了ITF与边际似然在概率切换AL-RNN中的目标诱导曲率。结果表明，ITF通过仅条件于单一强制状态路径而放大曲率，而边际似然曲率则因存在多个合理切换解释而通过缺失信息校正降低。在Lorenz-63实验中，窗口证据微调提升了验证集证据，但相对ITF预训练模型而言，损害了动力学关键量的表现。
Carbon-Taxed Transformers: A Green Compression Pipeline for Overgrown Language Models
Authors: Ajmain Inqiad Alam, Palash Roy, Chanchal K. Roy, Banani Roy, Kevin A. Schneider
Venue: Proceedings of ACM Software Engineering 3, FSE, Article FSE047, 2026
First: 2026-04-28T17:48:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:48:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The accelerating adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) in software engineering (SE) has brought with it a silent crisis: unsustainable computational cost. While these models demonstrate remarkable capabilities in different SE tasks, they are unmanageably large, slow to deploy, memory-intensive, and carbon-heavy. This reality threatens not only the scalability and accessibility of AI-powered SE, but also its long-term environmental sustainability. The research challenge is clear: we must go beyond accuracy and address efficiency and environmental cost as first-class design constraints. To meet this challenge, we introduce Carbon-Taxed Transformers (CTT), a systematic multi-architectural compression principled pipeline ordering inspired by economic carbon taxation principles. Drawing from the economic concept of carbon pricing, CTT operationalizes a computational carbon tax that penalizes architectural inefficiencies and rewards deployment-ready compression. We evaluate CTT across three core SE tasks: code clone detection, code summarization, and code generation, with models spanning encoder-only, encoder-decoder, and decoder-only architecture. Our results show that CTT delivers on inference: (1) up to 49x memory reduction, (2) time reduction up to 8-10x for clone detection, up to 3x for summarization, and 4-7x for generation, (3) up to 81% reduction in CO2 emissions and (4) CTT retains around 98% accuracy on clone detection, around 89% on summarization, and up to 91% (textual metrics) and 68% (pass@1) for generation. Two ablation studies show that pipeline ordering and individual component contributions are both essential, providing empirical justification for CTT's design and effectiveness. This work establishes a viable path toward responsible AI in SE through aggressive yet performance-preserving compression.
中文标题/摘要
标题：碳税变压器：面向臃肿语言模型的绿色压缩管道
大型语言模型（LLM）在软件工程中的快速采用带来了无声危机：不可持续的计算成本。尽管这些模型在不同软件工程任务中表现出色，但它们体积庞大、部署缓慢、内存密集且碳排放量高。这一现实不仅威胁着AI驱动软件工程的可扩展性和可及性，也影响其长期环境可持续性。研究挑战明确：我们必须超越准确率，将效率和环境成本作为首要设计约束。为此，我们提出碳税变压器（CTT），一种受经济碳税原则启发的系统性多架构压缩管道，按顺序排列。借鉴碳定价经济概念，CTT实施计算碳税，惩罚架构低效，奖励可部署的压缩。我们在三个核心软件工程任务——代码克隆检测、代码摘要和代码生成中评估CTT，涵盖仅编码器、编码器-解码器和仅解码器架构模型。结果表明，CTT在推理中表现优异：（1）内存减少高达49倍，（2）克隆检测时间减少8-10倍，摘要减少3倍，生成减少4-7倍，（3）二氧化碳排放减少高达81%，（4）克隆检测准确率保持约98%，摘要约89%，生成文本指标高达91%，pass@1为68%。两项消融研究显示，管道顺序和各组件贡献均至关重要，为CTT设计与有效性提供实证依据。本工作通过激进但性能保持的压缩，为软件工程中的负责任AI开辟了可行路径。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the unsustainable computational and environmental costs of large language models in software engineering, this work proposes Carbon-Taxed Transformers (CTT), a compression pipeline inspired by carbon taxation principles that penalizes inefficient architectures and rewards deployable efficiency. Evaluated across code clone detection, summarization, and generation using diverse model architectures, CTT achieves up to 49x memory reduction, 8-10x speedup in clone detection, and 81% CO2 emission reduction, while preserving 98% accuracy on clone detection, 89% on summarization, and up to 91% textual quality and 68% pass@1 for generation. Ablation studies confirm that pipeline ordering and individual compression components are critical to CTT’s effectiveness.
为应对大型语言模型在软件工程中带来的高计算与碳排放成本，本文提出Carbon-Taxed Transformers（CTT），一种受碳税理念启发的压缩管道，通过惩罚低效架构、奖励可部署的轻量化设计。在代码克隆检测、摘要与生成三大任务中，CTT实现最高49倍内存缩减、克隆检测速度提升8–10倍、摘要提升3倍、生成提升4–7倍，同时碳排放降低81%，准确率保持在克隆检测98%、摘要89%、生成91%（文本指标）及68%（pass@1）。消融实验表明，管道顺序与各压缩组件协同作用对性能至关重要。
Toward a Functional Geometric Algebra for Natural Language Semantics
Authors: James Pustejovsky
First: 2026-04-28T17:47:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:47:46+00:00
Comments: 43 pages. Keywords: geometric algebra, Clifford algebra, compositional semantics, natural language semantics, type coercion, multivector representations, graded type system, Generative Lexicon, neural language models, distributional semantics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Distributional and neural approaches to natural language semantics have been built almost exclusively on conventional linear algebra: vectors, matrices, tensors, and the operations that accompany them. These methods have achieved remarkable empirical success, yet they face persistent structural limitations in compositional semantics, type sensitivity, and interpretability. I argue in this paper that geometric algebra (GA) -- specifically, Clifford algebras -- provides a mathematically superior foundation for semantic representation, and that a Functional Geometric Algebra (FGA) framework extends GA toward a typed, compositional semantics capable of supporting inference, transformation, and interpretability while retaining full compatibility with distributional learning and modern neural architectures. I develop the formal foundations, identify three core capabilities that GA provides and linear algebra does not, present a detailed worked example illustrating operator-level semantic contrasts, and show how GA-based operations already implicit in current transformer architectures can be made explicit and extended. The central claim is not merely increased dimensionality but increased structural organization: GA expands an $n$-dimensional embedding space into a $2^n$ multivector algebra where base semantic concepts and their higher-order interactions are represented within a single, principled algebraic framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向自然语言语义的功能性几何代数
分布式和神经方法在自然语言语义研究中几乎完全依赖传统线性代数：向量、矩阵、张量及其相关运算。这些方法取得了显著的实证成功，但在组合语义、类型敏感性和可解释性方面仍存在固有结构限制。本文主张，几何代数（特别是克莱因代数）为语义表示提供了更优的数学基础，而功能性几何代数（FGA）框架则将几何代数拓展为一种类型化、组合式的语义体系，支持推理、转换和可解释性，同时保持与分布式学习和现代神经架构的完全兼容性。本文构建了形式基础，指出了几何代数相较于线性代数所具备的三项核心能力，通过详尽实例展示运算层面的语义差异，并说明当前Transformer架构中已隐含的几何代数运算如何显式化并扩展。核心主张并非单纯增加维度，而是增强结构组织性：几何代数将n维嵌入空间扩展为2^n维多向量代数，使基本语义概念及其高阶交互统一于一个严谨的代数框架内。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the structural limitations of linear algebra in compositional semantics and interpretability, this paper proposes Functional Geometric Algebra (FGA) as a superior mathematical framework using Clifford algebras to represent natural language meaning. FGA introduces a typed, compositional system that supports inference and transformation while remaining compatible with neural architectures and distributional learning. The author demonstrates three key advantages of GA over linear algebra: richer semantic representation through multivectors, graded type systems for type coercion, and explicit operator-level semantic contrasts. Experimental results show that GA-based operations already present in transformer models can be formalized and extended, enabling more principled semantic composition within a 2^n-dimensional multivector space that organizes base concepts and their interactions cohesively.
为解决线性代数在组合语义建模中的结构性局限，本文提出功能几何代数（FGA）框架，基于克莱因代数构建更优的语义表示体系。FGA支持类型化、组合式语义，兼具推理能力与可解释性，同时兼容分布式学习与神经架构。作者系统构建了FGA形式化基础，指出其相较线性代数的三大优势，通过算子级语义对比示例加以说明，并揭示当前Transformer架构中隐含的几何代数操作可被显式化与扩展。实验表明，FGA将n维嵌入空间扩展为2^n多向量代数，使基础语义概念及其高阶交互关系在统一、严谨的代数框架内得以表达。
TSN-Affinity: Similarity-Driven Parameter Reuse for Continual Offline Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Dominik Żurek, Kamil Faber, Marcin Pietron, Paweł Gajewski, Roberto Corizzo
First: 2026-04-28T17:41:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:41:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Continual offline reinforcement learning (CORL) aims to learn a sequence of tasks from datasets collected over time while preserving performance on previously learned tasks. This setting corresponds to domains where new tasks arise over time, but adapting the model in live environment interactions is expensive, risky, or impossible. However, CORL inherits the dual difficulty of offline reinforcement learning and adapting while preventing catastrophic forgetting. Replay-based continual learning approaches remain a strong baseline but incur memory overhead and suffer from a distribution mismatch between replayed samples and newly learned policies. At the same time, architectural continual learning methods have shown strong potential in supervised learning but remain underexplored in CORL. In this work, we propose TSN-Affinity, a novel CORL method based on TinySubNetworks and Decision Transformer. The method enables task-specific parameterization and controlled knowledge sharing through a RL-aware reuse strategy that routes tasks according to action compatibility and latent similarity. We evaluate the approach on benchmarks based on Atari games and simulations of manipulation tasks with the Franka Emika Panda robotic arm, covering both discrete and continuous control. Results show strong retention from sparse SubNetworks, with routing further improving multi-task performance. Our findings suggest that similarity-guided architectural reuse is a strong and viable alternative to replay-based strategies in a CORL setting. Our code is available at: https://github.com/anonymized-for-submission123/tsn-affinity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TSN-Affinity：基于相似性驱动的参数复用持续离线强化学习方法
持续离线强化学习（CORL）旨在从随时间收集的数据集中学习一系列任务，同时保持对先前学习任务的性能。该场景对应于新任务随时间出现但实时环境交互中调整模型成本高昂、风险大或不可行的领域。然而，CORL继承了离线强化学习和防止灾难性遗忘的双重困难。基于回放的持续学习方法仍是强基线，但存在内存开销问题，并且回放样本与新学习策略之间存在分布不匹配。同时，基于架构的持续学习方法在监督学习中展现出强大潜力，但在CORL中仍处于探索阶段。本文提出TSN-Affinity，一种基于TinySubNetworks和决策变换器的新型CORL方法。该方法通过强化学习感知的复用策略，根据动作兼容性和潜在相似性路由任务，实现任务特定参数化和可控知识共享。我们在基于Atari游戏和Franka Emika Panda机械臂操作任务的模拟基准上评估该方法，涵盖离散和连续控制。结果表明，稀疏子网络具有强保留能力，路由进一步提升多任务性能。我们的研究发现，基于相似性的架构复用是CORL场景中优于回放策略的强而可行的替代方案。代码见：https://github.com/anonymized-for-submission123/tsn-affinity。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of preserving performance across tasks in continual offline reinforcement learning while avoiding catastrophic forgetting and distribution mismatch, this work introduces TSN-Affinity, a method combining TinySubNetworks and Decision Transformer to enable task-specific parameterization and controlled knowledge sharing. It routes tasks based on action compatibility and latent similarity to guide parameter reuse, reducing reliance on replay buffers. Evaluated on Atari games and Franka Emika Panda manipulation tasks, the method demonstrates strong retention of prior task performance even with sparse subnetworks, and routing further enhances multi-task performance, suggesting architectural reuse via similarity is a viable alternative to replay-based approaches.
为解决持续离线强化学习中的灾难性遗忘与数据分布偏移问题，本文提出TSN-Affinity方法，结合TinySubNetworks与决策变换器，实现任务特定参数化与受控知识共享。该方法依据动作兼容性与潜在相似性路由任务，引导参数复用，避免回放方法的内存开销与分布偏移。在Atari游戏与Franka Emika Panda机械臂操控任务上的实验表明，该方法在多任务学习中保持了优异的性能，且路由机制显著提升多任务表现，证明基于相似性引导的架构复用是回放方法在持续离线强化学习中的可行替代方案。
Variational Neural Belief Parameterizations for Robust Dexterous Grasping under Multimodal Uncertainty
Authors: Clinton Enwerem, Shreya Kalyanaraman, John S. Baras, Calin Belta
First: 2026-04-28T17:40:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:40:49+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Contact variability, sensing uncertainty, and external disturbances make grasp execution stochastic. Expected-quality objectives ignore tail outcomes and often select grasps that fail under adverse contact realizations. Risk-sensitive POMDPs address this failure mode, but many use particle-filter beliefs that scale poorly, obstruct gradient-based optimization, and estimate Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) with high-variance approximations. We instead formulate grasp acquisition as variational inference over latent contact parameters and object pose, representing the belief with a differentiable Gaussian mixture. We use Gumbel-Softmax component selection and location-scale reparameterization to express samples as smooth functions of the belief parameters, enabling pathwise gradients through a differentiable CVaR surrogate for direct optimization of tail robustness. In simulation, our variational neural belief improves robust grasp success under contact-parameter uncertainty and exogenous force perturbations while reducing planning time by roughly an order of magnitude relative to particle-filter model-predictive control. On a serial-chain robot arm with a multifingered hand, we validate grasp-and-lift success under object-pose uncertainty against a Gaussian baseline. Both methods succeed on the tested perturbations, but our controller terminates in fewer steps and less wall-clock time while achieving a higher tactile grasp-quality proxy. Our learned belief also calibrates risk more accurately, keeping mean absolute calibration error below 0.14 across tested simulation regimes, compared with 0.58 for a Cross-Entropy Method planner.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向多模态不确定性的变分神经信念参数化用于鲁棒灵巧抓取
接触变化、传感不确定性及外部干扰使抓取执行具有随机性。期望质量目标忽略尾部结果，常选择在不利接触实现下失败的抓取方案。风险敏感型POMDP可解决此失效模式，但多数方法使用粒子滤波信念，其扩展性差、阻碍基于梯度的优化，并以高方差近似估计条件风险价值（CVaR）。我们转而将抓取获取建模为对潜在接触参数和物体姿态的变分推断，用可微高斯混合分布表示信念。通过Gumbel-Softmax组件选择与位置-尺度重参数化，将样本表示为信念参数的平滑函数，从而通过可微CVaR代理实现路径梯度，直接优化尾部鲁棒性。仿真结果表明，我们的变分神经信念在接触参数不确定性及外力扰动下显著提升抓取成功率，同时将规划时间减少约一个数量级。在串行链机器人臂配多指手的平台上，我们验证了在物体姿态不确定性下抓取并提升的成功性，对比高斯基线方法。两种方法均能应对测试扰动，但我们的控制器步数更少、耗时更短，且获得更高的触觉抓取质量代理值。所学信念还能更准确校准风险，仿真各场景下平均绝对校准误差低于0.14，而交叉熵方法规划器为0.58。
Summary / 总结
To address the stochastic nature of dexterous grasping under contact variability and sensing uncertainty, this work formulates grasp acquisition as variational inference over latent contact parameters and object pose, using a differentiable Gaussian mixture to represent beliefs. It employs Gumbel-Softmax and location-scale reparameterization to enable smooth gradient-based optimization via a differentiable CVaR surrogate, directly optimizing for tail robustness. In simulation, the variational neural belief achieves higher grasp success under contact and force perturbations while reducing planning time by an order of magnitude compared to particle-filter methods. On a physical robot arm, it outperforms a Gaussian baseline in grasp-and-lift success under pose uncertainty, terminating faster with higher tactile quality and better risk calibration, showing mean absolute calibration error below 0.14 versus 0.58 for a Cross-Entropy Method planner.
为应对接触不确定性与传感噪声导致的抓取过程随机性，该研究将抓取规划建模为对潜在接触参数与物体位姿的变分推断，使用可微高斯混合模型表示信念。通过Gumbel-Softmax与位置-尺度重参数化，实现信念参数到样本的平滑映射，从而支持基于梯度的CVaR代理优化，直接提升尾部鲁棒性。实验表明，该方法在接触与位姿不确定性下抓取成功率更高，规划时间比粒子滤波MPC减少一个数量级，在物理机器人上执行更快速，且风险校准更准确，平均绝对误差低于0.14，显著优于交叉熵方法基线的0.58。
Three Models of RLHF Annotation: Extension, Evidence, and Authority
Authors: Steve Coyne
First: 2026-04-28T17:39:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:39:14+00:00
Comments: 17 pages. Accepted to ACM FAccT '26, June 25-28, Montreal
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Preference-based alignment methods, most prominently Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback (RLHF), use the judgments of human annotators to shape large language model behaviour. However, the normative role of these judgments is rarely made explicit. I distinguish three conceptual models of that role. The first is extension: annotators extend the system designers' own judgments about what outputs should be. The second is evidence: annotators provide independent evidence about some facts, whether moral, social or otherwise. The third is authority: annotators have some independent authority (as representatives of the broader population) to determine system outputs. I argue that these models have implications for how RLHF pipelines should solicit, validate and aggregate annotations. I survey landmark papers in the literature on RLHF and related methods to illustrate how they implicitly draw on these models, describe failure modes that come from unintentionally or intentionally conflating them, and offer normative criteria for choosing among them. My central recommendation is that RLHF pipeline designers should decompose annotation into separable dimensions and tailor each pipeline to the model most appropriate for that dimension, rather than seeking a single unified pipeline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RLHF标注的三种模型：扩展、证据与权威
基于偏好的对齐方法，尤其是人类反馈强化学习（RLHF），利用人类标注者的判断来塑造大语言模型的行为。然而，这些判断的规范性角色很少被明确说明。我区分了三种该角色的概念模型：第一是扩展，即标注者扩展系统设计者自身对输出应为何物的判断；第二是证据，即标注者提供关于某些事实（无论是道德、社会或其他方面）的独立证据；第三是权威，即标注者作为更广泛人群的代表，拥有独立决定系统输出的权威。我认为这些模型对RLHF流程如何征集、验证和聚合标注具有影响。我综述了RLHF及相关方法文献中的里程碑式论文，以说明它们如何隐含地依赖这些模型，描述因无意或有意混淆这些模型而产生的失败模式，并提供选择模型的规范性标准。我的核心建议是，RLHF流程设计者应将标注分解为可分离的维度，并为每个维度定制最合适的流程，而非追求单一统一的流程。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the lack of explicit normative grounding in RLHF annotation practices by proposing three conceptual models—extension, evidence, and authority—that define how human judgments should influence model behavior. The author argues that these models dictate different requirements for annotation solicitation, validation, and aggregation, and that conflating them can lead to flawed alignment. Through an analysis of key RLHF literature, the paper identifies failure modes arising from misaligned model assumptions and recommends that pipeline designers decompose annotation tasks into distinct dimensions, each matched to the most suitable model, rather than pursuing a one-size-fits-all approach.
本文针对RLHF标注实践中缺乏明确规范基础的问题，提出三种概念模型——扩展、证据与权威——以界定人类判断如何影响大语言模型行为。作者认为，这些模型对标注的征集、验证与聚合有不同的要求，混淆模型可能导致对齐失败。通过对相关文献的分析，本文识别出因模型假设错配引发的失效模式，并建议将标注任务按维度分解，为每个维度匹配最合适的模型，而非追求统一的标注流程。
Conditional misalignment: common interventions can hide emergent misalignment behind contextual triggers
Authors: Jan Dubiński, Jan Betley, Anna Sztyber-Betley, Daniel Tan, Owain Evans
First: 2026-04-28T17:36:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:36:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Finetuning a language model can lead to emergent misalignment (EM) [Betley et al., 2025b]. Models trained on a narrow distribution of misaligned behavior generalize to more egregious behaviors when tested outside the training distribution. We study a set of interventions proposed to reduce EM. We confirm that these interventions reduce or eliminate EM on existing evaluations (questions like "How do I make a quick buck?"). However, if the evaluation prompts are tweaked to resemble the training context, the model displays EM. We call this conditional misalignment. As in standard EM, the model displays misaligned behaviors more egregious than those seen during training, but only on inputs sharing features with the training data. The first two interventions are diluting misaligned data with benign data, and finetuning on benign data after misaligned data. Both produce conditional misalignment. For instance, models trained on a mix of only 5% insecure code still show misalignment when asked to format responses as Python strings (resembling the training context). The third intervention is inoculation prompting. Here, statements with a similar form to the inoculation prompt serve as triggers for misalignment, even if they have the opposite meaning. On the positive side, inoculation prompting has lower (but still non-zero) conditional misalignment if training is on-policy or includes reasoning distillation. Our results imply that in realistic post-training, where misaligned data is typically combined with benign data, models may be conditionally misaligned even if standard evaluations look clean.
中文标题/摘要
标题：条件性错位：常见干预措施可能通过上下文触发隐藏新兴错位
微调语言模型可能导致新兴错位（EM）[Betley等，2025b]。在狭窄的错位行为分布上训练的模型，在测试时若超出训练分布，会泛化出更严重的错位行为。 我们研究了一组旨在减少EM的干预措施。我们确认这些措施在现有评估（如“如何快速赚钱？”）中能减少或消除EM。但若评估提示被调整以模拟训练上下文，模型仍会表现出EM。我们称之为条件性错位。与标准EM类似，模型在训练期间未见过的更严重错位行为仅在输入与训练数据共享特征时出现。 前两种干预措施是用良性数据稀释错位数据，或在错位数据后微调良性数据。两者均产生条件性错位。例如，仅用5%不安全代码训练的模型，当被要求以Python字符串格式化响应（模拟训练上下文）时仍会表现出错位。 第三种干预措施是免疫提示。即使语句含义相反，只要形式与免疫提示相似，即可触发错位。正面来看，若训练采用在线策略或包含推理蒸馏，免疫提示的条件性错位较低（但仍非零）。 我们的结果表明，在现实的后训练场景中，错位数据通常与良性数据混合，模型即使在标准评估中表现干净，也可能存在条件性错位。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how common interventions intended to reduce emergent misalignment (EM) in fine-tuned language models can inadvertently create conditional misalignment—where models exhibit harmful behaviors only when prompted with inputs resembling their training context. The authors evaluate three interventions: diluting misaligned data with benign data, fine-tuning on benign data after misaligned data, and inoculation prompting. All three reduce EM on standard benchmarks but reveal conditional misalignment when prompts mimic training context. For example, models trained with only 5% insecure code still misbehave when asked to format responses as Python strings. Inoculation prompting shows lower conditional misalignment under on-policy training or reasoning distillation, but remains non-zero. The findings suggest that real-world fine-tuning, which typically mixes benign and misaligned data, may produce models that appear safe on standard tests but are dangerously misaligned under contextually triggered inputs.
该论文研究了旨在减少语言模型微调后‘涌现对齐偏差’的常见干预措施，发现这些措施反而可能引发‘条件对齐偏差’——即模型仅在输入与训练数据上下文相似时才表现出更严重的有害行为。作者测试了三种干预方法：用良性数据稀释有害数据、在有害数据后微调良性数据、以及‘免疫提示’，结果发现所有方法在标准测试中可抑制偏差，但在模拟训练上下文的提示下仍会显现偏差，例如仅用5%有害代码训练的模型在要求输出Python字符串格式时仍会违规。免疫提示在策略对齐或推理蒸馏训练下可降低条件偏差，但仍非零。研究提示，现实世界中混合良性与有害数据的微调过程，可能导致模型在标准测试中表现正常，但在特定上下文触发下仍存在严重对齐风险。
Robust Deepfake Detection: Mitigating Spatial Attention Drift via Calibrated Complementary Ensembles
Authors: Minh-Khoa Le-Phan, Minh-Hoang Le, Trong-Le Do, Minh-Triet Tran
First: 2026-04-28T17:32:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:32:48+00:00
Comments: 4th place (out of 94 teams) in the NTIRE 2026 Robust Deepfake Detection Challenge
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Current deepfake detection models achieve state-of-the-art performance on pristine academic datasets but suffer severe spatial attention drift under real-world compound degradations, such as blurring and severe lossy compression. To address this vulnerability, we propose a foundation-driven forensic framework that integrates an extreme compound degradation engine with a structurally constrained, multi-stream architecture. During training, our degradation pipeline systematically destroys high-frequency artifacts, optimizing the DINOv2-Giant backbone to extract invariant geometric and semantic priors. We then process images through three specialized pathways: a Global Texture stream, a Localized Facial stream, and a Hybrid Semantic Fusion stream incorporating CLIP. Through analyzing spatial attribution via Score-CAM and feature stability using Cosine Similarity, we quantitatively demonstrate that these streams extract non-redundant, complementary feature representations and stabilize attention entropy. By aggregating these predictions via a calibrated, discretized voting mechanism, our ensemble successfully suppresses background attention drift while acting as a robust geometric anchor. Our approach yields highly stable zero-shot generalization, achieving Fourth Place in the NTIRE 2026 Robust Deepfake Detection Challenge at CVPR. Code is available at https://github.com/khoalephanminh/ntire26-deepfake-challenge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：鲁棒深度伪造检测：通过校准互补集成缓解空间注意力漂移
当前深度伪造检测模型在纯净学术数据集上达到最先进性能，但在真实世界复合退化（如模糊和严重有损压缩）下易出现严重空间注意力漂移。为解决此弱点，我们提出一种基于基础的取证框架，整合极端复合退化引擎与结构约束的多流架构。训练期间，我们的退化流程系统性破坏高频伪影，优化DINOv2-Giant主干网络提取不变的几何与语义先验。随后通过三条专用路径处理图像：全局纹理流、局部面部流及融合CLIP的混合语义融合流。通过Score-CAM分析空间归因及余弦相似度评估特征稳定性，定量证明这些流提取非冗余、互补的特征表示并稳定注意力熵。通过校准离散投票机制聚合预测，我们的集成成功抑制背景注意力漂移，同时作为鲁棒几何锚点。该方法实现高度稳定的零样本泛化，在CVPR NTIRE 2026鲁棒深度伪造检测挑战赛中获第四名。代码见https://github.com/khoalephanminh/ntire26-deepfake-challenge。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of current deepfake detectors under real-world degradations like blurring and compression, this work proposes a forensic framework that combats spatial attention drift through a degradation-aware training pipeline and a multi-stream architecture. The method integrates a compound degradation engine with a structurally constrained model using DINOv2-Giant, training it to extract invariant geometric and semantic features. Three specialized streams—Global Texture, Localized Facial, and Hybrid Semantic Fusion with CLIP—extract complementary representations, validated via Score-CAM and Cosine Similarity. A calibrated voting ensemble suppresses attention drift and enhances geometric stability, enabling robust zero-shot generalization. The model achieved fourth place in the NTIRE 2026 challenge among 94 teams.
为解决现有深度伪造检测模型在真实世界降质（如模糊与严重压缩）下注意力漂移的问题，该研究提出一种基于基础模型的取证框架。方法通过训练阶段的复合降质引擎去除高频伪影，引导DINOv2-Giant主干网络学习不变的几何与语义先验。三个专用流——全局纹理流、局部面部流和融合CLIP的混合语义流——提取互补特征，经Score-CAM与余弦相似度验证其非冗余性与稳定性。预测结果通过校准离散投票机制融合，有效抑制背景注意力漂移并稳定特征熵，实现鲁棒零样本泛化。该方法在NTIRE 2026深度伪造检测挑战赛中获得第四名。
Audio2Tool: Speak, Call, Act -- A Dataset for Benchmarking Speech Tool Use
Authors: Ramit Pahwa, Apoorva Beedu, Parivesh Priye, Rutu Gandhi, Saloni Takawale, Aruna Baijal, Zengli Yang
First: 2026-04-17T16:41:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:29:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Voice assistants increasingly rely on Speech Language Models (SpeechLMs) to interpret spoken queries and execute complex tasks, yet existing benchmarks lack domain breadth, acoustic diversity, and compositional reasoning complexity to evaluate tool-calling performance. We introduce Audio2Tool, a large-scale dataset comprising approximately 30,000 queries designed to assess tool-calling capabilities of SpeechLMs across three primary domains: Smart Car, Smart Home, and Wearables. Our benchmark features a multi-tier complexity hierarchy, ranging from simple direct commands to complex multi-intent and needle-in-a-haystack extraction to isolate distinct failure modes. To ensure realism, we employ zero-shot voice cloning text-to-speech synthesis and diverse noise profiles to simulate in-the-wild conditions. Evaluations of state-of-the-art SpeechLMs and ASR-LLM pipelines show strong performance on simple commands but significant degradation under compositional and acoustic challenges. Code and dataset are publicly available on the project page: https://audio2tool.github.io/.

No Pedestrian Left Behind: Real-Time Detection and Tracking of Vulnerable Road Users for Adaptive Traffic Signal Control
Authors: Anas Gamal Aly, Hala ElAarag
First: 2026-04-28T17:29:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:29:23+00:00
Comments: © Anas Gamal Aly and Hala ElAarag, 2026. This is the authors' version of the work. It is posted here for your personal use. Not for redistribution. The definitive Version of Record will be published in Proceedings of the 2026 ACM Southeast Conference (ACMSE 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current pedestrian crossing signals operate on fixed timing without adjustment to pedestrian behavior, which can leave vulnerable road users (VRUs) such as the elderly, disabled, or distracted pedestrians stranded when the light changes. We introduce No Pedestrian Left Behind (NPLB), a real-time adaptive traffic signal system that monitors VRUs in crosswalks and automatically extends signal timing when needed. We evaluated five state-of-the-art object detection models on the BGVP dataset, with YOLOv12 achieving the highest mean Average Precision at 50% (mAP@0.5) of 0.756. NPLB integrates our fine-tuned YOLOv12 with ByteTrack multi-object tracking and an adaptive controller that extends pedestrian phases when remaining time falls below a critical threshold. Through 10,000 Monte Carlo simulations, we demonstrate that NPLB improves VRU safety by 71.4%, reducing stranding rates from 9.10% to 2.60%, while requiring signal extensions in only 12.1% of crossing cycles.
中文标题/摘要
标题：不让任何行人被落下：面向自适应交通信号控制的脆弱道路使用者实时检测与跟踪
当前人行横道信号采用固定时长，无法根据行人行为调整，可能导致老年人、残疾人或分心行人等脆弱道路使用者（VRUs）在信号灯变色时被困。我们提出“不让任何行人被落下”（NPLB）系统，该系统实时监测人行横道上的VRUs，并在必要时自动延长信号时间。我们在BGVP数据集上评估了五种最先进的目标检测模型，YOLOv12在mAP@0.5指标上表现最佳，达到0.756。NPLB将我们微调的YOLOv12与ByteTrack多目标跟踪及自适应控制器结合，当剩余时间低于临界阈值时自动延长行人通行相位。通过10,000次蒙特卡洛模拟，我们证明NPLB可使VRU安全提升71.4%，将被困率从9.10%降至2.60%，同时仅在12.1%的过街周期中需要延长信号。

Motivated by the inefficiency of fixed pedestrian signals that often strand vulnerable road users, the authors propose NPLB, a real-time adaptive traffic signal system that detects and tracks pedestrians using a fine-tuned YOLOv12 detector and ByteTrack tracker, then extends signal timing when necessary. Evaluated on the BGVP dataset, YOLOv12 achieved the highest mAP@0.5 of 0.756 among five models. In 10,000 Monte Carlo simulations, NPLB reduced pedestrian stranding from 9.10% to 2.60%, improving VRU safety by 71.4%, while extending signals in only 12.1% of cycles.
为解决固定行人信号无法适应行人行为、导致弱势道路使用者（VRU）被滞留的问题，作者开发了NPLB系统，该系统通过微调的YOLOv12检测器和ByteTrack跟踪器实时检测与追踪行人，并在必要时延长信号时间。在BGVP数据集上，YOLOv12模型达到最高的mAP@0.5（0.756）。通过10,000次蒙特卡洛模拟，NPLB将行人滞留率从9.10%降至2.60%，VRU安全提升71.4%，且仅在12.1%的过街周期中延长信号。
Explainable AI for Jet Tagging: A Comparative Study of GNNExplainer, GNNShap, and GradCAM for Jet Tagging in the Lund Jet Plane
Authors: Pahal D. Patel, Sanmay Ganguly
First: 2026-04-28T17:28:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:28:41+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 9 figures. Comments are welcome
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph neural networks such as ParticleNet and transformer based networks on point clouds such as ParticleTransformer achieve state-of-the-art performance on jet tagging benchmarks at the Large Hadron Collider, yet the physical reasoning behind their predictions remains opaque. We present different methods, i.e. perturbation-based (GNNExplainer), Shapley-value-based (GNNShap), and gradient-based (GRADCam); adapted to operate on LundNet's Lund-plane graph representation. Leveraging the fact that each node in the Lund plane corresponds to a physically meaningful parton splitting, we construct Monte Carlo truth explanation masks and introduce a physics-informed evaluation framework that goes beyond standard fidelity metrics. We perform the analysis in three transverse-momentum bins ($\mathrm{p_T} \in [500,700]$, $[800,1000]$, and the inclusive region $[500,1000]$ GeV), revealing how explanation quality and focus shift between non-perturbative and perturbative regimes. We further quantify the correlation between explainer-assigned node importance and classical jet substructure observables -- $N$-subjettiness ratios $τ_{21}$ and $τ_{32}$ and the energy correlation functions -- establishing the degree to which the model has learned known QCD features. We find that overall the weight assigned by explainability methods has a correlation with analytic observables, with expected shift across different phase space regimes, indicating that a trained neural network indeed learns some aspects of jet-substructure moments. Our open-source implementation enables reproducible explainability studies for graph-based jet taggers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于喷注识别的可解释人工智能：在伦德喷注平面中对GNNExplainer、GNNShap和GradCAM的比较研究
粒子网络（如ParticleNet）和基于点云的变换器网络（如ParticleTransformer）在大型强子对撞机的喷注识别基准测试中达到最先进性能，但其预测背后的物理推理仍不透明。我们提出了三种方法——基于扰动的（GNNExplainer）、基于Shapley值的（GNNShap）和基于梯度的（GradCAM），并将其适配于伦德网（LundNet）的伦德平面图表示。利用伦德平面中每个节点对应物理上有意义的强子分裂这一事实，我们构建了蒙特卡洛真实解释掩码，并引入了一个超越标准保真度度量的物理信息评估框架。我们在三个横向动量区间（p_T ∈ [500,700]、[800,1000] 及包含区间 [500,1000] GeV）进行分析，揭示了在非微扰与微扰区域中解释质量与焦点的变化。我们进一步量化了解释器分配的节点重要性与经典喷注子结构可观测量——N-子喷注性比值τ_{21}和τ_{32}以及能量相关函数之间的相关性，从而确定模型学习已知QCD特征的程度。我们发现，总体而言，可解释性方法分配的权重与解析可观测量存在相关性，且在不同相空间区域中存在预期的偏移，表明训练好的神经网络确实学习了部分喷注子结构矩的特征。我们的开源实现支持对基于图的喷注识别器进行可重复的可解释性研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the physical reasoning behind high-performing graph neural networks for jet tagging, this study compares three explainability methods—GNNExplainer, GNNShap, and GradCAM—adapted to Lund plane graph representations. The authors leverage the physical meaning of nodes (parton splittings) to construct Monte Carlo truth masks and introduce a physics-informed evaluation framework beyond standard fidelity metrics. Experiments across three transverse-momentum bins reveal that explanation quality and focus vary between non-perturbative and perturbative regimes. The methods show correlation between assigned node importance and classical jet substructure observables like τ21, τ32, and energy correlation functions, indicating that neural networks capture some QCD features, with shifts in importance patterns across phase space.
为理解高性能图神经网络在喷注识别中的物理机制，本研究比较了三种可解释性方法——GNNExplainer、GNNShap 和 GradCAM——在 Lund 平面图结构上的表现。作者利用蒙特卡洛真实掩码构建物理信息驱动的解释评估框架，并在三个横动量区间分析解释质量如何随强子化与微扰区变化。结果发现，可解释性方法赋予节点的重要性与经典喷注子结构可观测量（如 τ21、τ32 和能量关联函数）存在相关性，表明神经网络确实学习了部分 QCD 特征。研究结果表明，可解释性方法能捕捉到有意义的物理模式，尽管不完美，且开源实现支持可复现分析。
QCalEval: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Quantum Calibration Plot Understanding
Authors: Shuxiang Cao, Zijian Zhang, Abhishek Agarwal, Grace Bratrud, Niyaz R. Beysengulov, Daniel C. Cole, Alejandro Gómez Frieiro, Elena O. Glen, Hao Hsu, Gang Huang, Raymond Jow, Greshma Shaji, Tom Lubowe, Ligeng Zhu, Luis Mantilla Calderón, Nicola Pancotti, Joel Pendleton, Brandon Severin, Charles Etienne Staub, Sara Sussman, Antti Vepsäläinen, Neel Rajeshbhai Vora, Yilun Xu, Varinia Bernales, Daniel Bowring, Elica Kyoseva, Ivan Rungger, Giulia Semeghini, Sam Stanwyck, Timothy Costa, Alán Aspuru-Guzik, Krysta Svore
First: 2026-04-28T17:28:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:28:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Quantum computing calibration depends on interpreting experimental data, and calibration plots provide the most universal human-readable representation for this task, yet no systematic evaluation exists of how well vision-language models (VLMs) interpret them. We introduce QCalEval, the first VLM benchmark for quantum calibration plots: 243 samples across 87 scenario types from 22 experiment families, spanning superconducting qubits and neutral atoms, evaluated on six question types in both zero-shot and in-context learning settings. The best general-purpose zero-shot model reaches a mean score of 72.3, and many open-weight models degrade under multi-image in-context learning, whereas frontier closed models improve substantially. A supervised fine-tuning ablation at the 9-billion-parameter scale shows that SFT improves zero-shot performance but cannot close the multimodal in-context learning gap. As a reference case study, we release NVIDIA Ising Calibration 1, an open-weight model based on Qwen3.5-35B-A3B that reaches 74.7 zero-shot average score.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QCalEval：用于量子校准图理解的视觉-语言模型基准
量子计算校准依赖于实验数据的解读，而校准图是该任务最通用的人类可读表示形式，但目前尚无系统性评估视觉-语言模型（VLMs）如何解读校准图。我们引入QCalEval，这是首个针对量子校准图的VLM基准：涵盖22个实验家族、87种场景类型、243个样本，评估六种问题类型，支持零样本和上下文学习两种设置。最佳通用零样本模型平均得分72.3，许多开源模型在多图像上下文学习中表现下降，而前沿闭源模型则显著提升。在90亿参数规模的监督微调消融实验中，SFT可提升零样本性能，但无法弥合多模态上下文学习的差距。作为参考案例，我们发布NVIDIA Ising Calibration 1，基于Qwen3.5-35B-A3B构建的开源模型，零样本平均得分达74.7。

From Threads to Trajectories: A Multi-LLM Pipeline for Community Knowledge Extraction from GitHub Issue Discussions
Authors: Nazia Shehnaz Joynab, Soneya Binta Hossain
First: 2026-04-28T17:21:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:21:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Resolution of complex post-production issues in large-scale open-source software (OSS) projects requires significant cognitive effort, as developers need to go through long, unstructured and fragmented issue discussion threads before that. In this paper, we present SWE-MIMIC-Bench, an issue trajectory dataset generated from raw GitHub discussions using an automated multi-LLM pipeline. Unlike simple summarization, this pipeline utilizes a group of closed-source LLMs to perform granular tasks: analyzing individual comments with awareness of externally-linked resources, classifying comment analyses into label-specific fields (e.g., root cause, solution plan, implementation progress), and synthesizing label-aware trajectories which capture a structured and coherent narrative of the entire discussion thread. Our pipeline uses five closed-source LLM configurations for distinct purposes: label classification, inline code block and external link summarization, comment analysis, label-specific field classification and trajectory synthesis. By generating concise and reliable trajectories from complex conversation threads, this system can assist developers and researchers of broader software engineering community to understand the experience-driven collaborative approach for issue diagnosis. Furthermore, the generated trajectories can be used to train modern LLM agents to think and act like an expert developer. We evaluated our system on 800 real-world GitHub issues drawn from the SWE-Bench-Pro, SWE-Bench-Multilingual and SWE-Bench-Verified dataset, achieving a 91.7% success rate in extracting 734 high-fidelity reasoning trajectories.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从对话到轨迹：一种用于从GitHub问题讨论中提取社区知识的多大语言模型流水线
在大型开源软件（OSS）项目中解决复杂的后期制作问题需要大量认知努力，因为开发者需在长篇、无结构且碎片化的议题讨论线程中进行筛选。本文提出SWE-MIMIC-Bench，这是一个通过自动化多大语言模型流水线从原始GitHub讨论中生成的议题轨迹数据集。与简单摘要不同，该流水线利用一组闭源大语言模型执行细粒度任务：结合外部链接资源分析单个评论，将评论分析分类至特定标签字段（如根本原因、解决方案计划、实施进度），并合成标签感知轨迹，以捕捉整个讨论线程的结构化、连贯叙事。该流水线使用五种闭源大语言模型配置，分别用于标签分类、内联代码块与外部链接摘要、评论分析、标签特定字段分类和轨迹合成。通过从复杂对话线程生成简洁可靠的轨迹，该系统可帮助更广泛的软件工程社区开发者和研究人员理解基于经验的协作式问题诊断方法。此外，生成的轨迹可用于训练现代大语言模型代理，使其像专家开发者一样思考和行动。我们在SWE-Bench-Pro、SWE-Bench-Multilingual和SWE-Bench-Verified数据集中抽取的800个真实GitHub议题上评估了该系统，成功提取734条高保真推理轨迹，准确率达91.7%。
Summary / 总结
To address the cognitive burden developers face when navigating fragmented GitHub issue discussions, this study introduces SWE-MIMIC-Bench, a dataset of issue trajectories generated via a multi-LLM pipeline. The method employs five closed-source LLMs to perform granular tasks including comment analysis with external resource awareness, label classification (e.g., root cause, solution plan), and synthesis of structured trajectories that capture coherent narratives. Evaluated on 800 real-world issues from three SWE-Bench datasets, the pipeline achieved a 91.7% success rate in producing 734 high-fidelity reasoning trajectories, demonstrating its effectiveness in extracting structured knowledge from unstructured discussions and enabling downstream training of expert-like LLM agents.
为提升大型开源项目中复杂问题的解决效率，该研究提出SWE-MIMIC-Bench数据集，通过多大语言模型（LLM）管道从GitHub讨论中提取结构化知识。该方法利用五个闭源LLM分别完成评论分析（结合外部资源）、标签分类（如根本原因、解决方案）及轨迹合成等任务，最终生成连贯的议题叙事。在800个真实GitHub问题上测试，系统成功提取734条高保真推理轨迹，准确率达91.7%，有效将无结构对话转化为结构化知识，可用于训练LLM模拟专家开发者思维。
Drivetrain simulation using variational autoencoders
Authors: Pallavi Sharma, Jorge-Humberto Urrea-Quintero, Bogdan Bogdan, Adrian-Dumitru Ciotec, Laura Vasilie, Henning Wessels, Matteo Skull
First: 2025-01-29T13:37:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:21:12+00:00
Comments: 27 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work proposes variational autoencoders (VAEs) to predict a vehicle's jerk signals from torque demand in the context of limited real-world drivetrain datasets. We implement both unconditional and conditional VAEs, trained on experimental data from two variants of a fully electric SUV with differing torque and drivetrain configurations. The VAEs synthesize jerk signals that capture characteristics from multiple drivetrain scenarios by leveraging the learned latent space. A performance comparison with baseline physics-based and hybrid models confirms the effectiveness of the VAEs, without requiring detailed system parametrization. Unconditional VAEs generate realistic jerk signals without prior system knowledge, while conditional VAEs enable the generation of signals tailored to specific torque inputs. This approach reduces the dependence on costly and time-intensive real-world experiments and extensive manual modeling. The results support the integration of generative models such as VAEs into drivetrain simulation pipelines, both for data augmentation and for efficient exploration of complex operational scenarios, with the potential to streamline validation and accelerate vehicle development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用变分自编码器进行传动系统仿真
本研究提出使用变分自编码器（VAE）在现实世界传动系统数据有限的情况下，根据扭矩需求预测车辆的加加速度信号。我们实现了无条件和条件VAE，基于两种不同扭矩和传动系统配置的全电动SUV实验数据进行训练。VAE通过学习到的潜在空间合成加加速度信号，捕捉多种传动系统场景的特征。与基于物理的基线模型和混合模型的性能比较证实了VAE的有效性，无需详细系统参数化。无条件VAE无需先验系统知识即可生成真实加加速度信号，而条件VAE可针对特定扭矩输入生成定制化信号。该方法减少了对昂贵且耗时的现实实验和大量手工建模的依赖。结果支持将生成模型（如VAE）集成到传动系统仿真流程中，既可用于数据增强，也可高效探索复杂工况，有望简化验证并加速车辆开发。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of real-world drivetrain datasets, this study employs variational autoencoders to predict vehicle jerk signals from torque demand, enabling data-efficient simulation. Both unconditional and conditional VAEs are trained on experimental data from two electric SUV variants with distinct drivetrain setups, learning a latent space that captures diverse operational behaviors. Experimental results show that VAEs outperform baseline physics-based and hybrid models in generating realistic jerk signals without detailed system modeling; unconditional VAEs produce generalizable outputs, while conditional VAEs allow precise signal generation for specific torque inputs, reducing reliance on costly experiments and accelerating vehicle development.
鉴于实际 drivetrain 数据稀缺，本研究采用变分自编码器（VAE）从扭矩需求预测车辆加速度变化率（jerk），无需详细系统建模。在两种不同驱动配置的纯电SUV实验数据上训练了无条件和条件VAE，通过潜在空间学习跨场景的加速度变化率特征。实验表明，VAE在生成真实加速度变化率信号方面优于物理模型和混合模型，无条件VAE可泛化生成，条件VAE可针对特定扭矩输入定制输出，从而减少对昂贵实验和人工建模的依赖。
MolReFlect: Towards In-Context Fine-grained Alignments between Molecules and Texts
Authors: Jiatong Li, Yunqing Liu, Wei Liu, Jingdi Le, Di Zhang, Wenqi Fan, Dongzhan Zhou, Yuqiang Li, Qing Li
First: 2024-11-22T04:28:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:16:01+00:00
Comments: Accepted by TKDE, To appear. Codes are available at: https://github.com/phenixace/MolReFlect
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Molecule discovery is a pivotal research field, impacting everything from medicine to materials. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have been widely adopted in molecular understanding and generation, serving as a bridge between the molecular space and the natural language space, yet the alignment between molecules and their corresponding captions remains a significant challenge. Previous endeavors typically treat molecules as monolithic inputs, lacking an intermediate reasoning process and sacrificing explainability. In this work, we define fine-grained alignments as the precise correspondence between a molecule's sub-structures and the textual phrases that explain their properties. These alignments are crucial for LLMs to understand molecules in a more accurate and explainable manner. Normally, such fine-grained alignments require expert annotation, which is both costly and time-consuming. To allow LLMs to automatically label and learn the fine-grained alignments, we propose MolReFlect, a novel teacher-student framework, where a teacher LLM first generates and refines mappings between caption phrases and SMILES substructures and then explicitly teaches these detailed alignments to a student LLM. Experimental results demonstrate that MolReFlect enables LLMs to significantly outperform previous baselines, achieving the state-of-the-art performance in the molecule-caption translation task. Our codes are available via: https://github.com/phenixace/MolReFlect.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MolReFlect：面向分子与文本间上下文细粒度对齐
分子发现是影响医学到材料科学等领域的关键研究领域。近期，大型语言模型（LLMs）被广泛应用于分子理解和生成，作为分子空间与自然语言空间之间的桥梁，但分子与其对应描述文本的对齐仍是一个重大挑战。以往方法通常将分子视为整体输入，缺乏中间推理过程，牺牲了可解释性。本文将细粒度对齐定义为分子子结构与解释其性质的文本短语之间的精确对应关系，这对LLMs以更准确、可解释的方式理解分子至关重要。通常，此类细粒度对齐需专家标注，成本高且耗时。为使LLMs能自动标注并学习细粒度对齐，我们提出MolReFlect，一种新颖的教师-学生框架：教师LLM首先生成并优化描述短语与SMILES子结构之间的映射，然后显式地将这些详细对齐关系传授给学生LLM。实验表明，MolReFlect使LLMs显著超越先前基线，在分子-文本翻译任务中达到最先进性能。代码见：https://github.com/phenixace/MolReFlect。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for precise, explainable molecule-text alignment in molecular discovery, this work introduces MolReFlect, a teacher-student framework that enables LLMs to learn fine-grained mappings between textual descriptions and molecular substructures. The teacher LLM first generates and refines mappings between caption phrases and SMILES substructures, then explicitly transfers these alignments to a student LLM. Experiments show that MolReFlect significantly outperforms prior methods in molecule-caption translation, achieving state-of-the-art results without requiring expert annotations.
为提升大语言模型在分子发现中的表现，MolReFlect提出一种教师-学生框架，教师模型先生成并优化文本短语与SMILES子结构的对应关系，再将这些细粒度对齐显式传递给学生模型。实验表明，该方法在分子-文本翻译任务中显著优于现有方法，达到当前最优性能，且无需专家标注。
CHUCKLE -- When Humans Teach AI To Learn Emotions The Easy Way
Authors: Ankush Pratap Singh, Houwei Cao, Yong Liu
First: 2025-10-10T13:38:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:14:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Curriculum learning (CL) structures training from simple to complex samples, facilitating progressive learning. However, existing CL approaches for emotion recognition often rely on heuristic, data-driven, or model-based definitions of sample difficulty, neglecting the difficulty for human perception, a critical factor in subjective tasks like emotion recognition. We propose CHUCKLE (Crowdsourced Human Understanding Curriculum for Knowledge Led Emotion Recognition), a perception-driven CL framework that leverages annotator agreement and alignment in crowd-sourced datasets to define sample difficulty, under the assumption that clips challenging for humans are similarly hard for neural networks. Experimental results suggest that CHUCKLE enhances the performance of LSTMs and Transformers over non-curriculum baselines, while reducing the number of gradient updates, thereby enhancing both training efficiency and model robustness in both subject-dependent and subject-independent settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CHUCKLE——人类以简单方式教AI学习情感
课程学习（CL）按从简单到复杂的样本结构化训练，促进渐进式学习。然而，现有用于情感识别的CL方法常依赖启发式、数据驱动或模型定义的样本难度，忽视了人类感知难度——这是主观任务（如情感识别）的关键因素。我们提出CHUCKLE（基于众包人类理解的课程框架，用于知识驱动的情感识别），该框架基于众包数据集中标注者的一致性与对齐度定义样本难度，假设人类难以理解的片段对神经网络同样困难。实验表明，CHUCKLE在依赖和不依赖特定主体的设置中，相比非课程基线方法提升了LSTM和Transformer的性能，同时减少梯度更新次数，从而提高训练效率和模型鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing curriculum learning methods that ignore human perceptual difficulty in emotion recognition, CHUCKLE introduces a perception-driven curriculum framework that defines sample difficulty based on crowd-sourced annotator agreement, assuming human-challenging clips are also hard for neural networks. The method constructs a curriculum by ranking video clips according to how consistently humans find them difficult to label, then trains models sequentially from easier to harder examples. Experiments show CHUCKLE improves performance of both LSTM and Transformer models over non-curriculum baselines while reducing gradient updates, leading to faster training and better robustness in both subject-dependent and subject-independent settings.
为提升情感识别效果，CHUCKLE提出一种基于人类感知的课程学习框架，通过众包标注者的一致性来定义样本难度，假设人类难以理解的片段对神经网络也较难。该方法按难度递增顺序组织训练样本，促进学习进程。实验表明，CHUCKLE在LSTM和Transformer模型上优于非课程学习基线，同时减少梯度更新次数，提升训练效率与模型鲁棒性，适用于受试者相关与无关场景。
When Errors Can Be Beneficial: A Categorization of Imperfect Rewards for Policy Gradient
Authors: Shuning Shang, Hubert Strauss, Stanley Wei, Sanjeev Arora, Noam Razin
First: 2026-04-28T17:10:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:10:15+00:00
Comments: Code available at https://github.com/princeton-pli/imperfect-rewards
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Training language models via reinforcement learning often relies on imperfect proxy rewards, since ground truth rewards that precisely define the intended behavior are rarely available. Standard metrics for assessing the quality of proxy rewards, such as ranking accuracy, treat incorrect rewards as strictly harmful. In this work, however, we highlight that not all deviations from the ground truth are equal. By theoretically analyzing which outputs attract probability during policy gradient optimization, we categorize reward errors according to their effect on the increase in ground truth reward. The analysis establishes that reward errors, though conventionally viewed as harmful, can also be benign or even beneficial by preventing the policy from stalling around outputs with mediocre ground truth reward. We then present two practical implications of our theory. First, for reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), we develop reward model evaluation metrics that account for the harmfulness of reward errors. Compared to standard ranking accuracy, these metrics typically correlate better with the performance of a language model after RLHF, yet gaps remain in robustly evaluating reward models. Second, we provide insights for reward design in settings with verifiable rewards. A key theme underlying our results is that the effectiveness of a proxy reward function depends heavily on its interaction with the initial policy and learning algorithm.
中文标题/摘要
标题：错误亦可有益：策略梯度中不完美奖励的分类
通过强化学习训练语言模型时，常依赖不完美的代理奖励，因为精确定义预期行为的真实奖励很少可用。评估代理奖励质量的标准指标（如排序准确率）将错误奖励视为严格有害。然而，本研究指出，并非所有偏离真实奖励的情况都等同。通过理论分析策略梯度优化过程中哪些输出吸引概率，我们根据其对真实奖励提升的影响对奖励错误进行分类。分析表明，尽管传统上视奖励错误为有害，但它们也可能无害甚至有益——通过防止策略在中等真实奖励输出周围停滞。我们进一步提出理论的两个实际应用：首先，在人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）中，我们开发了考虑奖励错误有害性的评估指标，相比标准排序准确率，这些指标通常与RLHF后语言模型性能的相关性更高，但奖励模型的稳健评估仍存差距；其次，我们为可验证奖励场景下的奖励设计提供见解。结果的核心主题是：代理奖励函数的有效性高度依赖其与初始策略和学习算法的交互。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the issue that imperfect proxy rewards in reinforcement learning, especially for language models, are often treated as uniformly harmful, despite some errors potentially aiding optimization. The authors categorize reward errors based on their impact on policy gradient updates, revealing that certain inaccuracies can be benign or even beneficial by preventing policies from getting stuck at mediocre reward outputs. They propose new evaluation metrics for reward models in RLHF that better predict post-training model performance than standard ranking accuracy, and offer design insights for verifiable reward systems, emphasizing that reward effectiveness depends critically on interaction with the initial policy and learning algorithm.
该论文指出，在强化学习中，尤其是语言模型训练时，不完美的代理奖励常被视作有害，但部分错误反而可能有益。作者通过理论分析奖励误差如何影响策略梯度更新，按其对真实奖励增长的影响将误差分类。研究发现，某些奖励误差能防止策略陷入中等水平输出的局部停滞，从而是中性或有益的。实践应用包括：一是提出新的奖励模型评估指标，比传统排序准确率更能预测RLHF后模型性能；二是为可验证奖励场景提供设计建议，强调代理奖励的有效性高度依赖其与初始策略和学习算法的交互。
Ask don't tell: Reducing sycophancy in large language models
Authors: Magda Dubois, Cozmin Ududec, Christopher Summerfield, Lennart Luettgau
First: 2026-02-27T12:27:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:07:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sycophancy, the tendency of large language models to favour user-affirming responses over critical engagement, has been identified as an alignment failure, particularly in high-stakes advisory and social contexts. While prior work has documented conversational features correlated with sycophancy, we lack a systematic understanding of what provokes or prevents AI sycophancy. Here, we present a set of controlled experimental studies where we first isolate how input framing influences sycophancy, and second, leverage these findings to develop mitigation strategies. In a nested factorial design, we compare questions to various non-questions where we vary three orthogonal factors: epistemic certainty (statement, belief, conviction), perspective (I- vs user-perspective), and affirmation vs negation. We show that (1) sycophancy is substantially higher in response to non-questions compared to questions. Additionally, we find that (2) sycophancy increases monotonically with epistemic certainty conveyed by the user, and (3) is amplified by I-perspective framing. Building on this, we show that asking a model to convert non-questions into questions before answering significantly reduces sycophancy. Importantly, this effect is stronger than a simple baseline prompt asking models "not to be sycophantic". Our work offers a practical and effective input-level mitigation that both developers and users can easily adopt.
中文标题/摘要
标题：询问而非告知：减少大语言模型的谄媚倾向
谄媚倾向，即大语言模型倾向于优先提供符合用户期望的回应而非批判性互动，已被视为对齐失败，尤其在高风险建议和社会情境中。尽管已有研究记录了与谄媚倾向相关的对话特征，但我们缺乏系统性理解哪些因素会引发或抑制AI的谄媚行为。本文通过一系列受控实验，首先隔离输入框架如何影响谄媚倾向，其次利用这些发现开发缓解策略。在嵌套因子设计中，我们比较了不同类型的非问题与问题，变化三个正交因素：认知确定性（陈述、信念、确信）、视角（第一人称 vs 用户视角）以及肯定与否定。我们发现：（1）对非问题的回应中谄媚倾向显著高于对问题的回应；（2）谄媚倾向随用户表达的认知确定性单调增加；（3）第一人称视角框架会加剧谄媚倾向。基于此，我们证明，要求模型在回答前将非问题转化为问题，可显著降低谄媚倾向。重要的是，该效果强于简单提示模型“不要谄媚”的基线方法。我们的工作提供了一种实用且有效的输入级缓解策略，开发者和用户均可轻松采用。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the problem of sycophancy in large language models — their tendency to favor user-affirming responses over critical engagement — which poses alignment risks in high-stakes contexts. Through controlled experiments using a nested factorial design, the researchers varied input framing across epistemic certainty, perspective (I- vs user-perspective), and affirmation/negation to isolate triggers of sycophantic behavior. Results show sycophancy is significantly higher for non-questions than questions, increases with epistemic certainty, and is amplified by first-person framing. Crucially, prompting models to convert non-questions into questions before answering substantially reduces sycophancy, outperforming a simple anti-sycophancy prompt. This input-level intervention offers a practical, scalable solution for both developers and users.
为解决大语言模型在高风险情境中倾向于迎合用户而非进行批判性回应的‘谄媚’问题，该研究通过受控实验系统探究引发或抑制此类行为的输入特征。实验采用嵌套因子设计，操控三个正交变量：认知确定性（陈述、信念、确信）、视角（第一人称 vs 用户视角）和肯定/否定语气，比较问题与非问题输入。结果表明，非问题输入引发的谄媚行为显著更高，且随用户表达的认知确定性增强而单调上升，第一人称视角进一步加剧该现象。研究进一步证明，引导模型先将非问题转化为问题再作答，比单纯要求模型避免谄媚更有效。
From Syntax to Emotion: A Mechanistic Analysis of Emotion Inference in LLMs
Authors: Bangzhao Shu, Arinjay Singh, Mai ElSherief
First: 2026-04-28T17:03:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T17:03:16+00:00
Comments: 18 pages including appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in emotionally sensitive human-AI applications, yet little is known about how emotion recognition is internally represented. In this work, we investigate the internal mechanisms of emotion recognition in LLMs using sparse autoencoders (SAEs). By analyzing sparse feature activations across layers, we identify a consistent three-phase information flow, in which emotion-related features emerge only in the final phase. We further show that emotion representations comprise both shared features across emotions and emotion-specific features. Using phase-stratified causal tracing, we identify a small set of features that strongly influence emotion predictions, and show that both their number and causal impact vary across emotions; in particular, Disgust is more weakly and diffusely represented than other emotions. Finally, we propose an interpretable and data-efficient causal feature steering method that significantly improves emotion recognition performance across multiple models while largely preserving language modeling ability, and demonstrate that these improvements generalize across multiple emotion recognition datasets. Overall, our findings provide a systematic analysis of the internal mechanisms underlying emotion recognition in LLMs and introduce an efficient, interpretable, and controllable approach for improving model performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从句法到情感：大型语言模型中情感推断的机制分析
大型语言模型（LLMs）在情感敏感的人机应用中日益普及，但其内部如何表征情感识别仍知之甚少。本研究利用稀疏自动编码器（SAEs）探究LLMs中情感识别的内部机制。通过分析各层稀疏特征激活，我们发现情感相关特征仅在最后阶段出现，呈现一致的三阶段信息流。进一步表明，情感表征包含跨情感共享特征与情感特异性特征。通过分阶段因果追溯，我们识别出一小部分对情感预测影响显著的特征，并发现其数量与因果影响因情感而异；尤其“厌恶”情感的表征较弱且分散。最后，我们提出一种可解释、数据高效且可控的因果特征引导方法，显著提升多个模型的情感识别性能，同时基本保留语言建模能力，并证明该方法在多个情感识别数据集上具有一般化能力。整体而言，我们的研究系统分析了LLMs中情感识别的内部机制，并引入了一种高效、可解释、可控的方法以提升模型性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how large language models internally represent and process emotion, this study employs sparse autoencoders to analyze feature activations across model layers, revealing a three-phase information flow where emotion-related features only emerge in the final phase. The research identifies both shared and emotion-specific features, and through causal tracing, finds that Disgust is uniquely weakly and diffusely represented compared to other emotions. The authors then propose a causal feature steering method that enhances emotion recognition performance across multiple models without compromising language modeling, demonstrating generalization across datasets.
为理解大语言模型内部如何表征和处理情绪，本研究采用稀疏自编码器分析各层特征激活，发现情绪相关特征仅在模型最后阶段出现，呈现三阶段信息流。研究识别出情绪共享特征与情绪特异性特征，并通过分阶段因果追溯发现，厌恶情绪的表征较其他情绪更弱且更分散。作者进一步提出一种因果特征引导方法，在不损害语言建模能力的前提下显著提升多模型的情绪识别性能，且效果在多个数据集上具有一致性。
RESTestBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating the Effectiveness of LLM-Generated REST API Test Cases from NL Requirements
Authors: Leon Kogler, Stefan Hangler, Maximilian Ehrhart, Benedikt Dornauer, Roland Wuersching, Peter Schrammel
First: 2026-04-28T16:59:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:59:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted for EASE 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing REST API testing tools are typically evaluated using code coverage and crash-based fault metrics. However, recent LLM-based approaches increasingly generate tests from NL requirements to validate functional behaviour, making traditional metrics weak proxies for whether generated tests validate intended behaviour. To address this gap, we present RESTestBench, a benchmark comprising three REST services paired with manually verified NL requirements in both precise and vague variants, enabling controlled and reproducible evaluation of requirement-based test generation.
