RESTestBench further introduces a requirements-based mutation testing metric that measures the fault-detection effectiveness of a generated test case with respect to a specific requirement, extending the property-based approach of Bartocci et al. . Using RESTestBench, we evaluate two approaches across multiple state-of-the-art LLMs: (i) non-refinement-based generation, and (ii) refinement-based generation guided by interaction with the running SUT. In the refinement experiments, RESTestBench assesses how exposure to the actual implementation, valid or mutated, affects test effectiveness. Our results show that test effectiveness drops considerably when the generator interacts with faulty or mutated code, especially for vague requirements, sometimes negating the benefit of refinement and indicating that incorporating actual SUT behaviour is unnecessary when requirement detail is high.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RESTestBench：用于评估LLM生成的REST API测试用例从自然语言需求有效性基准
现有REST API测试工具通常通过代码覆盖率和基于崩溃的故障度量进行评估。然而，近期基于LLM的方法越来越多地从自然语言需求生成测试以验证功能行为，使得传统度量指标难以准确反映生成测试是否验证了预期行为。为解决这一问题，我们提出了RESTestBench基准，包含三个REST服务及其经过人工验证的精确与模糊变体自然语言需求，支持对基于需求的测试生成进行可控且可重复的评估。RESTestBench进一步引入基于需求的变异测试度量，衡量生成测试用例对特定需求的故障检测有效性，扩展了Bartocci等人提出的属性驱动方法。利用RESTestBench，我们评估了两种方法在多个前沿LLM上的表现：(i) 非优化生成，(ii) 通过与运行中的SUT交互引导的优化生成。在优化实验中，RESTestBench评估了接触实际实现（有效或变异）对测试有效性的影响。结果表明，当生成器与故障或变异代码交互时，测试有效性显著下降，尤其在模糊需求情况下，有时甚至抵消了优化带来的优势，表明当需求细节较高时，引入实际SUT行为是不必要的。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inadequacy of traditional testing metrics for evaluating LLM-generated REST API tests from natural language requirements, the authors introduce RESTestBench, a benchmark with three REST services and manually verified requirements in precise and vague forms. The benchmark includes a novel mutation-based metric measuring test effectiveness against specific requirements, extending prior work. Experiments compare non-refinement and refinement-based LLM test generation, where refinement involves interaction with the system under test. Results show that refinement often reduces test effectiveness, especially for vague requirements when interacting with faulty or mutated code, suggesting that such interaction may be unnecessary when requirements are detailed.
为评估基于自然语言需求生成的REST API测试用例的有效性，作者提出RESTestBench基准，包含三个REST服务及人工验证的精确与模糊需求。他们引入基于需求的变异测试指标，衡量测试用例对特定需求的故障检测能力。评估两种LLM方法——无优化生成与通过与被测系统交互的优化生成——结果表明，优化生成在模糊需求下与故障或变异代码交互时效果显著下降，甚至抵消优化优势，提示在需求明确时无需引入实际系统行为。
Luminol-AIDetect: Fast Zero-shot Machine-Generated Text Detection based on Perplexity under Text Shuffling
Authors: Lucio La Cava, Andrea Tagarelli
First: 2026-04-28T16:58:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:58:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine-generated text (MGT) detection requires identifying structurally invariant signals across generation models, rather than relying on model-specific fingerprints. In this respect, we hypothesize that while large language models excel at local semantic consistency, their autoregressive nature results in a specific kind of structural fragility compared to human writing. We propose Luminol-AIDetect, a novel, zero-shot statistical approach that exposes this fragility through coherence disruption. By applying a simple randomized text-shuffling procedure, we demonstrate that the resulting shift in perplexity serves as a principled, model-agnostic discriminant, as MGT displays a characteristic dispersion in perplexity-under-shuffling that differs markedly from the more stable structural variability of human-written text. Luminol-AIDetect leverages this distinction to inform its decision process, where a handful of perplexity-based scalar features are extracted from an input text and its shuffled version, then detection is performed via density estimation and ensemble-based prediction. Evaluated across 8 content domains, 11 adversarial attack types, and 18 languages, Luminol-AIDetect demonstrates state-of-the-art performance, with gains up to 17x lower FPR while being cheaper than prior methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Luminol-AIDetect：基于困惑度与文本洗牌的快速零样本机器生成文本检测方法
机器生成文本（MGT）检测需识别跨生成模型的结构不变信号，而非依赖模型特定指纹。我们假设，尽管大语言模型擅长局部语义一致性，但其自回归特性导致其结构脆弱性不同于人类写作。我们提出Luminol-AIDetect，一种新颖的零样本统计方法，通过破坏连贯性暴露这种脆弱性。通过简单随机文本洗牌，我们证明洗牌后困惑度的偏移可作为原理性、模型无关的判别器，因MGT在洗牌下困惑度呈现显著分散，而人类写作文本结构变异更稳定。Luminol-AIDetect利用此差异，从输入文本及其洗牌版本提取少量基于困惑度的标量特征，通过密度估计与集成预测完成检测。在8个内容领域、11种对抗攻击类型及18种语言上评估，Luminol-AIDetect表现领先，误报率降低达17倍，且成本低于先前方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for model-agnostic detection of machine-generated text that avoids reliance on model-specific fingerprints, the authors propose Luminol-AIDetect, a zero-shot method exploiting the structural fragility of autoregressive language models. The method shuffles input text and measures the resulting shift in perplexity, leveraging the observation that machine-generated text exhibits greater dispersion in perplexity under shuffling compared to human text. By extracting perplexity-based features from both original and shuffled versions, the system performs detection via density estimation and ensemble prediction. Evaluated across 8 domains, 11 attack types, and 18 languages, Luminol-AIDetect achieves state-of-the-art performance, reducing false positive rates by up to 17x while being computationally cheaper than existing approaches.
为实现无需模型特定指纹的机器生成文本检测，作者提出Luminol-AIDetect方法，利用自回归语言模型在结构上的脆弱性。该方法通过随机打乱文本顺序破坏语义连贯性，并测量由此引起的困惑度变化，以此区分人类与机器生成文本。实验在8个领域、11种对抗攻击和18种语言上验证，结果显示该方法达到当前最优性能，可将假阳性率降低17倍，且计算成本低于现有方法。
Privileged Foresight Distillation: Zero-Cost Future Correction for World Action Models
Authors: Pengcheng Fang, Hongli Chen, Xiaohao Cai
First: 2026-04-28T16:58:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:58:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World action models jointly predict future video and action during training, raising an open question about what role the future-prediction branch actually plays. A recent finding shows that this branch can be removed at inference with little to no loss on common manipulation benchmarks, suggesting that future information may act merely as a regularizer on the shared visual backbone. We propose instead that joint training induces an action-conditioned correction that privileged future observations impose on action denoising, and that current-only policies capture this correction only partially. Making the account precise, we formulate privileged foresight as a residual in the action-denoising direction -- the difference between what a model predicts given the true future and what it predicts given only the current frame -- and introduce \emph{Privileged Foresight Distillation (PFD)}, which transfers this residual from a training-time teacher into a small adapter on a current-only student. The teacher and student share the same backbone and differ only in the attention mask over video tokens; future video is never generated at inference. Controlled experiments verify that this gain reflects a genuine future-conditioned correction rather than a side effect of capacity or regularization. Empirically, PFD achieves consistent improvements on LIBERO and RoboTwin manipulation benchmarks while preserving the current-only inference interface at negligible added latency. This view reframes the role of future information in world action models: not as a target to predict, nor as a regularizer to absorb, but as a compressible correction to be distilled.
中文标题/摘要
标题：特权前瞻蒸馏：世界动作模型中零成本的未来校正
世界动作模型在训练时联合预测未来视频和动作，引发了一个开放性问题：未来预测分支究竟扮演什么角色。近期研究发现，该分支在推理时可被移除，对常见操控基准测试影响微乎其微，暗示未来信息可能仅作为共享视觉主干的正则化器。我们提出相反观点：联合训练诱导了动作条件下的校正，即特权未来观测对动作去噪施加的影响，而当前仅有的策略仅部分捕捉到这种校正。为精确描述，我们将特权前瞻定义为动作去噪方向上的残差——即模型在给定真实未来与仅当前帧时预测结果的差异，并引入\emph{特权前瞻蒸馏（PFD）}，将该残差从训练时的教师模型迁移到当前仅有的学生模型上的小型适配器。教师与学生共享相同主干，仅在视频标记的注意力掩码上不同；推理时从不生成未来视频。受控实验验证，该增益反映的是真正的未来条件校正，而非容量或正则化副作用。实证表明，PFD在LIBERO和RoboTwin操控基准测试中持续提升性能，同时保持当前仅推理接口，且额外延迟可忽略。该观点重新定义了世界动作模型中未来信息的作用：并非作为预测目标，亦非作为吸收正则化器，而是可压缩的校正，需通过蒸馏传递。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that future-prediction branches in world action models can be removed at inference with minimal performance loss, the authors propose that future information acts as a privileged correction signal for action denoising, not as a target or regularizer. They formalize this as a residual between predictions using true future and current-only inputs, then distill this correction via a lightweight adapter (Privileged Foresight Distillation, PFD) trained on a teacher model. Experiments confirm this correction is genuine and not due to capacity or regularization, and PFD consistently improves performance on LIBERO and RoboTwin benchmarks while maintaining current-only inference with negligible latency.
鉴于世界动作模型中的未来预测分支在推理时可被移除且性能损失微小，作者提出未来信息实际提供的是动作去噪的校正信号。他们将该信号形式化为真实未来帧与当前帧预测结果之间的残差，并提出特权前瞻蒸馏（PFD）方法，将该残差从训练时的教师模型迁移到轻量学生适配器中。教师与学生共享骨干网络，仅在视频标记的注意力掩码上不同，推理时不生成未来帧。实验表明，PFD在LIBERO和RoboTwin操控基准上持续提升性能，同时保持当前帧推理接口且延迟几乎无增加，验证该校正非因容量或正则化所致。
Instantaneous Planning, Control and Safety for Navigation in Unknown Underwater Spaces
Authors: Veejay Karthik, Udit Ekansh, Tejal Bedmutha, Shivam Vishwakarma, Rohan Deshpande, Leena Vachhani
First: 2026-04-07T01:32:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:58:39+00:00
Comments: Uploaded by mistake. A different version of the study is under process
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Navigating autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) in unknown environments is significantly challenging due to poor visibility, weak signal transmission, and dynamic water currents. These factors pose challenges in accurate global localization, reliable communication, and obstacle avoidance. Local sensing provides critical real time environmental data to enable online decision making. However, the inherent noise in underwater sensor measurements introduces uncertainty, complicating planning and control. To address these challenges, we propose an integrated planning and control framework that leverages real time sensor data to dynamically induce closed loop AUV trajectories, ensuring robust obstacle avoidance and enhanced maneuverability in tight spaces. By planning motion based on pre designed feedback controllers, the approach reduces the computational complexity needed for carrying out online optimizations and enhances operational safety in complex underwater spaces. The proposed method is validated through ROS Gazebo simulations on the RexRov AUV, demonstrating its efficacy. Its performance is evaluated by comparison against PID based tracking methods, and quantifying localization errors in dead reckoning as the AUV transitions into the target communication range.
中文标题/摘要
标题：未知水下空间导航的即时规划、控制与安全
在未知环境中自主水下航行器（AUV）的导航面临严峻挑战，因能见度差、信号传输弱及水流动态变化，导致精确全局定位、可靠通信和避障困难。局部感知提供关键实时环境数据以支持在线决策，但水下传感器测量固有噪声引入不确定性，使规划与控制复杂化。为此，我们提出一种集成规划与控制框架，利用实时传感器数据动态诱导闭环AUV轨迹，确保在狭窄空间内实现稳健避障与增强机动性。通过基于预设反馈控制器规划运动，该方法降低在线优化所需计算复杂度，并提升复杂水下空间中的操作安全性。该方法已在ROS Gazebo仿真中于RexRov AUV上验证其有效性，并通过与基于PID的跟踪方法对比及量化AUV进入目标通信范围时死算定位误差进行性能评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of navigating autonomous underwater vehicles in unknown environments—such as poor visibility, weak communication, and dynamic currents—the paper proposes an integrated planning and control framework that uses real-time local sensor data to generate closed-loop trajectories. This approach reduces computational load by planning motion based on pre-designed feedback controllers, enabling robust obstacle avoidance and improved maneuverability in confined spaces. Experimental results from ROS Gazebo simulations on the RexRov AUV show superior performance compared to PID-based tracking, with reduced localization errors during transitions into communication range.
为应对水下自主航行器在未知环境中因能见度差、通信弱和水流动态导致的导航难题，本文提出一种融合规划与控制的框架，利用实时局部传感器数据生成闭环轨迹，降低在线优化计算负担，提升复杂狭窄水域中的避障能力和机动性。在ROS Gazebo仿真平台上的RexRov水下机器人实验表明，该方法优于PID跟踪控制，且在进入通信范围时定位误差显著降低。
Investigation into In-Context Learning Capabilities of Transformers
Authors: Rushil Chandrupatla, Leo Bangayan, Sebastian Leng, Arya Mazumdar
First: 2026-04-28T16:57:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:57:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformers have demonstrated a strong ability for in-context learning (ICL), enabling models to solve previously unseen tasks using only example input output pairs provided at inference time. While prior theoretical work has established conditions under which transformers can perform linear classification in-context, the empirical scaling behavior governing when this mechanism succeeds remains insufficiently characterized. In this paper, we conduct a systematic empirical study of in-context learning for Gaussian-mixture binary classification tasks. Building on the theoretical framework of Frei and Vardi (2024), we analyze how in-context test accuracy depends on three fundamental factors: the input dimension, the number of in-context examples, and the number of pre-training tasks. Using a controlled synthetic setup and a linear in-context classifier formulation, we isolate the geometric conditions under which models successfully infer task structure from context alone. We additionally investigate the emergence of benign overfitting, where models memorize noisy in-context labels while still achieving strong generalization performance on clean test data. Through extensive sweeps across dimensionality, sequence length, task diversity, and signal-to-noise regimes, we identify the parameter regions in which this phenomenon arises and characterize how it depends on data geometry and training exposure. Our results provide a comprehensive empirical map of scaling behavior in in-context classification, highlighting the critical role of dimensionality, signal strength, and contextual information in determining when in-context learning succeeds and when it fails.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于Transformer上下文学习能力的调查
Transformer在上下文学习（ICL）方面表现出强大能力，使其能够在推理时仅使用示例输入输出对解决此前未见过的任务。尽管先前的理论工作已确立了Transformer在上下文中执行线性分类的条件，但决定该机制何时成功的经验性缩放行为尚未充分刻画。 本文对高斯混合二分类任务的上下文学习进行系统性实证研究。基于Frei和Vardi（2024）的理论框架，我们分析上下文测试准确率如何依赖于三个基本因素：输入维度、上下文示例数量和预训练任务数量。通过受控合成设置和线性上下文分类器形式化，我们隔离了模型仅从上下文推断任务结构的几何条件。 我们还研究了良性过拟合现象的出现，即模型虽记忆噪声上下文标签，但仍能在干净测试数据上实现强泛化性能。通过在维度、序列长度、任务多样性及信噪比范围内进行广泛扫描，我们识别出该现象出现的参数区域，并刻画其如何依赖于数据几何结构和训练暴露程度。 我们的结果提供了上下文分类缩放行为的全面实证图谱，突出了维度、信号强度和上下文信息在决定上下文学习成功或失败中的关键作用。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates the empirical scaling behavior of in-context learning in transformers, motivated by the need to understand under what conditions models can successfully infer task structure from contextual examples alone. Using a controlled synthetic Gaussian-mixture binary classification setup and a linear in-context classifier framework, the authors systematically analyze how test accuracy depends on input dimension, number of in-context examples, and pre-training task diversity. Their experiments reveal that in-context learning succeeds when dimensionality is low and signal strength is high, while benign overfitting—where models memorize noisy labels yet generalize well—emerges in high-dimensional, low-signal regimes with sufficient training exposure.
该研究旨在厘清Transformer在上下文学习中成功的关键条件，通过构建可控的高斯混合二分类合成数据集，结合线性上下文分类器框架，系统分析输入维度、上下文示例数量及预训练任务多样性对测试准确率的影响。实验发现，当维度较低且信号较强时，上下文学习表现良好；而在高维、低信噪比环境下，模型易出现良性过拟合——即记忆噪声标签但仍能对干净测试数据保持良好泛化能力，且该现象依赖于数据几何结构与训练暴露程度。
SIEVES: Selective Prediction Generalizes through Visual Evidence Scoring
Authors: Hector G. Rodriguez, Marcus Rohrbach
First: 2026-04-28T16:57:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:57:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve ever-stronger performance on visual-language tasks. Even as traditional visual question answering benchmarks approach saturation, reliable deployment requires satisfying low error tolerances in real-world out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. Precisely, selective prediction aims to improve coverage, i.e. the share of inputs the system answers, while adhering to a user-defined risk level. This is typically achieved by assigning a confidence score to each answer and abstaining on those that fall below a certain threshold. To enable reliable generalization, we require reasoner models to produce localized visual evidence while answering, and design a selector that explicitly learns to estimate the quality of the localization provided by the reasoner. We show that SIEVES (Selective Prediction through Visual Evidence Scoring) improves coverage by up to three times on challenging OOD benchmarks (V* Bench, HR-Bench-8k, MME-RealWorld-Lite, VizWiz, and AdVQA), compared to non-grounding baselines. Beyond better generalization to OOD tasks, the design of the SIEVES selector enables transfer to proprietary reasoners without access to their weights or logits, such as o3 and Gemini-3-Pro, providing coverage boosts beyond those attributable to accuracy alone. We highlight that SIEVES generalizes across all five tested OOD datasets and reasoner models (Pixel-Reasoner, o3, and Gemini-3-Pro), without benchmark- or reasoner-specific training or adaptation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SIEVES：通过视觉证据评分实现选择性预测的泛化
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉-语言任务上持续提升性能。尽管传统视觉问答基准接近饱和，可靠部署仍需满足现实世界分布外（OOD）场景中的低错误容忍度。具体而言，选择性预测旨在提升覆盖度（即系统回答输入的比例），同时遵守用户设定的风险水平。通常通过为每个答案分配置信度分数，并对低于阈值的答案拒绝回答来实现。为实现可靠泛化，我们要求推理模型在回答时提供局部视觉证据，并设计一个选择器，显式学习评估推理模型提供的定位质量。我们表明，SIEVES（通过视觉证据评分实现选择性预测）在具有挑战性的OOD基准（V* Bench、HR-Bench-8k、MME-RealWorld-Lite、VizWiz、AdVQA）上，相比非接地基线覆盖度提升最高达三倍。除在OOD任务上实现更好泛化外，SIEVES选择器的设计还支持在无访问其权重或logits的情况下，向专有推理器（如o3和Gemini-3-Pro）迁移，提供超越单纯准确率提升的覆盖度增益。我们强调，SIEVES在所有五个测试的OOD数据集和推理器模型（Pixel-Reasoner、o3、Gemini-3-Pro）上均实现泛化，无需针对特定基准或推理器进行训练或适配。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable deployment of multimodal large language models in out-of-distribution visual-language tasks, this work introduces SIEVES, a selective prediction framework that enhances coverage while controlling error risk. It achieves this by training a selector to evaluate the quality of localized visual evidence generated by a reasoner model during answer generation, then abstaining from predictions when evidence quality falls below a threshold. Experiments on five challenging OOD benchmarks show SIEVES improves coverage up to threefold over non-grounding baselines, and its selector design enables transfer to proprietary reasoners like o3 and Gemini-3-Pro without access to their internal weights, yielding coverage gains beyond accuracy improvements alone. The method generalizes across all tested datasets and reasoner models without requiring task- or model-specific fine-tuning.
为提升多模态模型在真实世界分布外场景中的可靠性，SIEVES提出一种基于视觉证据评分的选择性预测框架，在控制风险的前提下显著提升系统覆盖能力。该方法通过引入视觉证据评分器，评估推理模型提供的局部视觉证据质量，从而决定是否输出答案。实验表明，SIEVES在五个挑战性分布外基准上相比非接地基线最高提升三倍覆盖能力，且其评分器设计可迁移至o3和Gemini-3-Pro等私有模型，无需访问其内部权重，覆盖提升超越单纯准确率提升。更重要的是，SIEVES在所有测试数据集和推理模型上均实现泛化，无需针对特定任务或模型进行微调。
G-Loss: Graph-Guided Fine-Tuning of Language Models
Authors: Sharma Aditya, Agarwal Vinti, Kumar Rajesh
First: 2026-04-28T16:55:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:55:57+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, Learning on Graphs (LoG2025)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traditional loss functions, including cross-entropy, contrastive, triplet, and su pervised contrastive losses, used for fine-tuning pre-trained language models such as BERT, operate only within local neighborhoods and fail to account for the global semantic structure. We present G-Loss, a graph-guided loss function that incorporates semi-supervised label propagation to use structural relationships within the embedding manifold. G-Loss builds a document-similarity graph that captures global semantic relationships, thereby guiding the model to learn more discriminative and robust embeddings. We evaluate G-Loss on five benchmark datasets covering key downstream classification tasks: MR (sentiment analysis), R8 and R52 (topic categorization), Ohsumed (medical document classification), and 20NG (news categorization). In the majority of experimental setups, G-Loss converges faster and produces semantically coherent embedding spaces, resulting in higher classification accuracy than models fine-tuned with traditional loss functions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：G-Loss：图引导的语言模型微调损失函数
传统损失函数（包括交叉熵、对比、三元组及监督对比损失）用于微调如BERT等预训练语言模型时，仅在局部邻域内操作，未能考虑全局语义结构。我们提出G-Loss，一种图引导的损失函数，通过半监督标签传播利用嵌入流形内的结构关系。G-Loss构建文档相似性图以捕捉全局语义关系，从而引导模型学习更具判别性和鲁棒性的嵌入。我们在涵盖关键下游分类任务的五个基准数据集（MR情感分析、R8和R52主题分类、Ohsumed医学文档分类、20NG新闻分类）上评估G-Loss。在多数实验设置中，G-Loss收敛更快，生成语义连贯的嵌入空间，分类准确率高于传统损失函数微调的模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of traditional loss functions that only consider local neighborhoods and ignore global semantic structure during language model fine-tuning, the authors propose G-Loss, a graph-guided loss function that leverages semi-supervised label propagation over a document-similarity graph to capture global semantic relationships in the embedding space. This graph structure guides the model to learn more discriminative and robust representations. Evaluated across five benchmark datasets including MR, R8, R52, Ohsumed, and 20NG, G-Loss consistently converges faster and achieves higher classification accuracy compared to models fine-tuned with standard losses like cross-entropy or contrastive loss.
为了解决传统损失函数在微调语言模型时忽略全局语义结构的问题，作者提出了G-Loss，一种基于图引导的损失函数，通过在文档相似度图上进行半监督标签传播，捕捉嵌入空间中的全局关系，从而引导模型学习更具判别力和鲁棒性的表示。在MR、R8、R52、Ohsumed和20NG五个基准数据集上的实验表明，G-Loss在多数设置下收敛速度更快，且分类准确率显著高于使用传统损失函数（如交叉熵或对比损失）微调的模型。
Agentic Harness Engineering: Observability-Driven Automatic Evolution of Coding-Agent Harnesses
Authors: Jiahang Lin, Shichun Liu, Chengjun Pan, Lizhi Lin, Shihan Dou, Xuanjing Huang, Hang Yan, Zhenhua Han, Tao Gui
First: 2026-04-28T16:55:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:55:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Harnesses have become a central determinant of coding-agent performance, shaping how models interact with repositories, tools, and execution environments. Yet automating harness engineering is hard: a heterogeneous action space, sparse and noisy evaluation signal, multi-million-token trajectories, and edits whose effect is hard to attribute to the next round's outcomes. We introduce Agentic Harness Engineering (AHE), a framework that automates harness-level evolution by instrumenting the three stages of any engineering loop (component editing, trajectory inspection, and decision making) with matched observability pillars: (1) component observability gives every editable harness component a file-level representation so the action space is explicit and revertible; (2) experience observability distills millions of raw trajectory tokens into a layered, drill-down evidence corpus that an evolving agent can actually consume; and (3) decision observability pairs every edit with a self-declared prediction, later verified against the next round's task-level outcomes. Together, these pillars turn every edit into a falsifiable contract, so harness evolution proceeds autonomously without collapsing into trial-and-error. Empirically, ten AHE iterations lift pass@1 on Terminal-Bench 2 from 69.7% to 77.0%, surpassing the human-designed harness Codex-CLI (71.9%) and the self-evolving baselines ACE and TF-GRPO. The frozen harness transfers without re-evolution: on SWE-bench-verified it tops aggregate success at 12% fewer tokens than the seed, and on Terminal-Bench 2 it yields +5.1 to +10.1pp cross-family gains across three alternate model families, indicating the evolved components encode general engineering experience rather than benchmark-specific tuning. These results position observability-driven evolution as a practical pathway to keep coding-agent harnesses continually improving.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理工具链工程：基于可观测性的编码代理工具链自动演化
工具链已成为决定编码代理性能的核心因素，塑造了模型与代码仓库、工具及执行环境的交互方式。然而，自动化工具链工程极具挑战：异构的动作空间、稀疏且嘈杂的评估信号、数百万令牌的轨迹，以及难以归因于后续结果的编辑效果。我们提出代理工具链工程（AHE），通过在工程循环的三个阶段（组件编辑、轨迹检查与决策）中嵌入匹配的可观测性支柱，实现工具链级别的自动化演化：（1）组件可观测性为每个可编辑的工具链组件提供文件级表示，使动作空间明确且可回滚；（2）经验可观测性将数百万原始轨迹令牌提炼为分层、可深入挖掘的证据语料库，使演化代理能够实际消费；（3）决策可观测性将每次编辑与自声明预测配对，后续通过与下一轮任务级结果验证。这些支柱将每次编辑转化为可证伪的契约，使工具链演化自主进行而不陷入试错。实证表明，十轮AHE迭代将Terminal-Bench 2的pass@1从69.7%提升至77.0%，超越人工设计的Codex-CLI（71.9%）及自演化基线ACE和TF-GRPO。冻结的工具链无需重新演化即可迁移：在SWE-bench验证中，其总成功率比初始工具链少消耗12%的令牌；在Terminal-Bench 2上，对三种不同模型家族均带来+5.1至+10.1个百分点的跨家族增益，表明演化组件编码的是通用工程经验而非基准特定调优。这些结果表明，基于可观测性的演化是持续提升编码代理工具链的实用路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of automating harness engineering for coding agents due to complex action spaces, sparse feedback, and hard-to-attributed edits, the paper proposes Agentic Harness Engineering (AHE), a framework that enables autonomous harness evolution through three observability pillars: component observability (explicit, revertible file-level representations), experience observability (structured, drill-down evidence from trajectory tokens), and decision observability (self-declared predictions verified against task outcomes). These pillars turn edits into falsifiable contracts, enabling stable, goal-directed evolution without trial-and-error. Experiments show that ten AHE iterations boost pass@1 on Terminal-Bench 2 from 69.7% to 77.0%, outperforming human-designed Codex-CLI (71.9%) and self-evolving baselines. The evolved harness also transfers well, achieving higher success rates with fewer tokens on SWE-bench and cross-family gains across models, indicating generalizable engineering knowledge.
为解决编码代理工具包（harness）自动化演进困难的问题——包括异构动作空间、稀疏反馈和难以归因的编辑效果——本文提出Agentic Harness Engineering（AHE）框架，通过三个可观测性支柱实现自主演进：组件可观测性（文件级可逆表示）、经验可观测性（结构化轨迹证据）和决策可观测性（自声明预测与任务结果验证）。这些支柱将每次编辑转化为可证伪的契约，避免试错，实现稳定演进。实验表明，十轮AHE迭代使Terminal-Bench 2的pass@1从69.7%提升至77.0%，超越人工设计的Codex-CLI（71.9%）和自演进基线。演进后的工具包具备迁移能力，节省令牌并跨三类模型家族实现+5.1至+10.1个百分点的性能提升，表明其编码经验具有泛化性。
ReCreate: Reasoning and Creating Domain Agents Driven by Experience
Authors: Zhezheng Hao, Hong Wang, Jian Luo, Jianqing Zhang, Yuyan Zhou, Qiang Lin, Can Wang, Hande Dong, Jiawei Chen
First: 2026-01-16T09:00:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:55:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Model agents are reshaping the industrial landscape. However, most practical agents remain human-designed because tasks differ widely, making them labor-intensive to build. This situation poses a central question: can we automatically create and adapt domain agents in the wild? While several recent approaches have sought to automate agent creation, they typically treat agent generation as a black-box procedure and rely solely on final performance metrics to guide the process. Such strategies overlook critical evidence explaining why an agent succeeds or fails, and often require high computational costs. To address these limitations, we propose ReCreate, an experience-driven framework for the automatic creation of domain agents. ReCreate systematically leverages agent interaction histories, which provide rich concrete signals on both the causes of success or failure and the avenues for improvement. Specifically, we introduce an agent-as-optimizer paradigm that effectively learns from experience via three key components: (i) an experience storage and retrieval mechanism for on-demand inspection; (ii) a reasoning-creating synergy pipeline that maps execution experience into scaffold edits; and (iii) hierarchical updates that abstract instance-level details into reusable domain patterns. In experiments across diverse domains, ReCreate consistently outperforms human-designed agents and existing automated agent generation methods, even when starting from minimal seed scaffolds.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReCreate：基于经验驱动的领域代理推理与创建框架
大语言模型代理正在重塑工业格局。然而，由于任务差异巨大，大多数实用代理仍需人工设计，构建过程耗时耗力。这引发了一个核心问题：我们能否在真实环境中自动创建并适应领域代理？尽管近期已有若干方法试图自动化代理生成，但它们通常将代理生成视为黑箱过程，仅依赖最终性能指标来引导流程，忽视了解释代理成败原因的关键证据，且往往需要高昂的计算成本。为解决这些局限，我们提出ReCreate，一种基于经验驱动的领域代理自动创建框架。ReCreate系统性地利用代理交互历史，提供关于成功或失败原因及改进路径的丰富具体信号。具体而言，我们引入“代理作为优化器”的范式，通过三个关键组件有效从经验中学习：(i) 经验存储与检索机制，支持按需检查；(ii) 推理-创建协同管道，将执行经验映射为骨架编辑；(iii) 分层更新机制，将实例级细节抽象为可复用的领域模式。在多个不同领域的实验中，ReCreate始终优于人工设计代理及现有自动化代理生成方法，即使从极简种子骨架开始也能实现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the labor-intensive nature of building domain-specific agents for diverse tasks, ReCreate proposes an experience-driven framework to automate agent creation and adaptation. It leverages agent interaction histories to extract concrete signals explaining success or failure, then employs an agent-as-optimizer paradigm with three core components: an experience storage and retrieval system, a reasoning-creating pipeline that translates execution experience into scaffold edits, and hierarchical updates that generalize instance-level insights into reusable domain patterns. Experimental results across multiple domains show ReCreate consistently outperforms both human-designed agents and existing automated methods, even when starting from minimal seed scaffolds.
为解决跨领域自动构建智能体的难题，ReCreate提出一种基于经验驱动的框架，通过分析智能体交互历史来指导自我优化。其核心包含三部分：经验存储与检索机制支持按需回溯，推理-创造协同管道将执行经验转化为骨架修改，分层更新机制将实例细节抽象为可复用的领域模式。在多个领域实验中，ReCreate即使从极简骨架出发，也持续优于人工设计智能体和现有自动化方法。
ADEMA: A Knowledge-State Orchestration Architecture for Long-Horizon Knowledge Synthesis with LLMAgents
Authors: Zhou Hanlin, Chan Huah Yong
First: 2026-04-28T16:54:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:54:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-horizon LLM tasks often fail not because a single answer is unattainable, but because knowledge states drift across rounds, intermediate commitments remain implicit, and interruption fractures the evolving evidence chain. This paper presents ADEMA as a knowledge-state orchestration architecture for long-horizon knowledge synthesis rather than as a generic multi-agent runtime. The architecture combines explicit epistemic bookkeeping, heterogeneous dual-evaluator governance, adaptive task-mode switching, reputation-shaped resource allocation, checkpoint-resumable persistence, segment-level memory condensation, artifact-first assembly, and final-validity checking with safe fallback. Evidence is drawn entirely from existing materials: a four-scenario showcase package, a fixed 60-run mechanism matrix, targeted micro-ablation and artifact-chain supplements, and a repaired protocol-level benchmark in which code-oriented evaluation is the clearest quality-sensitive mechanism block. Across the fixed matrix, removing checkpoint/resume produced the only invalid run, and it did so in the interruption-sensitive resume condition. By contrast, dual evaluation, segment synthesis, and dynamic governance are best interpreted as supporting control mechanisms that shape trajectory discipline, explicit artifact progression, and cost-quality behavior rather than as universal binary prerequisites for completion. The contribution is therefore a knowledge-state orchestration architecture in which explicit epistemic state transition, evidence-bearing artifact progression, and recoverable continuity are the primary design commitments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ADEMA：一种面向长周期知识合成的显式知识状态编排架构
长周期大语言模型任务失败往往并非因单一答案不可达，而是由于知识状态在回合间漂移、中间承诺隐含未明、中断破坏证据链的演进。本文提出ADEMA，作为面向长周期知识合成的显式知识状态编排架构，而非通用多智能体运行时。该架构融合显式认识状态记录、异构双评估者治理、自适应任务模式切换、声誉导向资源分配、可检查点恢复持久化、段级记忆压缩、以产物为先的组装及安全回退的最终有效性校验。所有证据均来自现有材料：四个场景展示包、固定60次运行机制矩阵、针对性微消融与产物链补充，以及修复后的协议级基准测试（其中代码导向评估是最清晰的质量敏感机制模块）。在固定矩阵中，移除检查点/恢复功能仅导致一次无效运行，且发生在中断敏感的恢复条件下。相比之下，双评估、段级合成与动态治理更宜理解为塑造轨迹纪律、显式产物演进与成本-质量行为的支持性控制机制，而非完成任务的普适二元前提。因此，本文贡献是一种以显式认识状态转换、承载证据的产物演进与可恢复连续性为核心设计承诺的知识状态编排架构。
Summary / 总结
ADEMA addresses the failure of long-horizon LLM tasks due to drifting knowledge states, implicit commitments, and interrupted evidence chains by introducing a knowledge-state orchestration architecture. It employs explicit epistemic bookkeeping, dual-evaluator governance, adaptive task switching, reputation-based resource allocation, checkpoint-resumable persistence, memory condensation, artifact-first assembly, and final validity checks with fallback. Experiments across a fixed 60-run matrix show that removing checkpoint/resume caused only one invalid run, under interruption-sensitive conditions, while dual evaluation, segment synthesis, and dynamic governance primarily enhance trajectory discipline and cost-quality behavior rather than being essential for completion.
ADEMA 为长周期LLM任务失败问题（因知识状态漂移、隐式承诺和证据链中断）设计了一种知识状态编排架构，包含显式认知记录、双评估器治理、任务自适应切换、声誉驱动资源分配、可恢复检查点、分段记忆压缩、先组装产物再合成、最终有效性校验与安全回退机制。在固定60次实验矩阵中，仅移除检查点恢复导致一次失败，且发生在中断敏感场景；而双评估、分段合成与动态治理主要作为辅助控制机制，提升轨迹纪律与成本-质量平衡，非完成任务的必要条件。
Semi-Markov Reinforcement Learning for City-Scale EV Ride-Hailing with Feasibility-Guaranteed Actions
Authors: An Nguyen, Hoang Nguyen, Phuong Le, Hung Pham, Cuong Do, Laurent El Ghaoui
First: 2026-04-28T16:54:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:54:03+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 9 figures. Submitted to Neurocomputing
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study city-scale control of electric-vehicle (EV) ride-hailing fleets where dispatch, repositioning, and charging decisions must respect charger and feeder limits under uncertain, spatially correlated demand and travel times. We formulate the problem as a hex-grid semi-Markov decision process (semi-MDP) with mixed actions -- discrete actions for serving, repositioning, and charging, together with continuous charging power -- and variable action durations. To guarantee physical feasibility during both training and deployment, the policy learns over high-level intentions produced by a masked, temperature-annealed actor. These intentions are projected at every decision step through a time-limited rolling mixed-integer linear program (MILP) that strictly enforces state-of-charge, port, and feeder constraints. To mitigate distributional shifts, we optimize a Soft Actor--Critic (SAC) agent against a Wasserstein-1 ambiguity set with a graph-aligned Mahalanobis ground metric that captures spatial correlations. The robust backup uses the Kantorovich--Rubinstein dual, a projected subgradient inner loop, and a primal--dual risk-budget update. Our architecture combines a two-layer Graph Convolutional Network (GCN) encoder, twin critics, and a value network that drives the adversary. Experiments on a large-scale EV fleet simulator built from NYC taxi data show that PD--RSAC achieves the highest net profit, reaching \$1.22M, compared with \$0.58M--\$0.70M for strong heuristic, single-agent RL, and multi-agent RL baselines, including Greedy, SAC, MAPPO, and MADDPG, while maintaining zero feeder-limit violations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向城市级电动汽车网约车的半马尔可夫强化学习与可行性保障动作
我们研究在不确定且空间相关的出行需求与行程时间下，城市级电动车辆（EV）网约车车队的调度、重新定位与充电决策问题，需满足充电器与馈线限制。将问题建模为六边形网格半马尔可夫决策过程（半MDP），混合动作包括离散动作（服务、重新定位、充电）与连续充电功率，且动作持续时间可变。为确保训练与部署期间的物理可行性，策略在掩码温度退火演员生成的高层意图上学习。每步决策时，通过时间受限滚动混合整数线性规划（MILP）将意图投影，严格强制电池状态、端口与馈线约束。为缓解分布偏移，我们以Wasserstein-1模糊集优化Soft Actor-Critic（SAC）智能体，采用图对齐的Mahalanobis地面度量捕捉空间相关性。鲁棒备份采用Kantorovich-Rubinstein对偶、投影次梯度内循环与原-对偶风险预算更新。架构结合双层图卷积网络（GCN）编码器、双批评网络与驱动对手的价值网络。在基于纽约出租车数据构建的大规模EV车队模拟器实验中，PD-RSAC实现最高净收益122万美元，相较强启发式、单智能体RL及多智能体RL基线（包括贪心、SAC、MAPPO、MADDPG）的0.58万至0.70万美元，同时保持零馈线限制违规。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to manage large-scale electric-vehicle ride-hailing fleets under uncertain demand and physical constraints, this work proposes a semi-Markov reinforcement learning framework that integrates discrete and continuous actions with variable durations. The method employs a masked, temperature-annealed actor to generate high-level intentions, which are then projected via a time-limited mixed-integer linear program to ensure charger and feeder feasibility. To handle distributional shifts, a robust Soft Actor-Critic agent is trained using a Wasserstein-1 ambiguity set with a graph-aligned Mahalanobis metric, leveraging Kantorovich-Rubinstein duality and risk-budget updates. Experiments on a NYC taxi-based simulator show that the proposed PD-RSAC method achieves the highest net profit of $1.22M, outperforming heuristic and RL baselines (e.g., SAC, MAPPO, MADDPG) that yield $0.58M–$0.70M, while maintaining zero feeder violations.
为应对大规模电动出行车队在不确定需求和物理约束下的调度难题，本文提出一种结合离散与连续动作、支持可变时长的半马尔可夫强化学习框架。方法通过掩码策略生成高层意图，并在每步决策中通过限时混合整数线性规划确保充电器与充电站容量约束。为缓解分布偏移，采用基于Wasserstein-1模糊集的鲁棒Soft Actor-Critic算法，结合空间相关性建模的Mahalanobis度量。在基于纽约出租车数据的仿真环境中，该方法PD--RSAC实现净收益122万美元，显著优于多种基线方法（收益0.58–0.70万美元），且完全避免充电站容量违规。
From Soliloquy to Agora: Memory-Enhanced LLM Agents with Decentralized Debate for Optimization Modeling
Authors: Jianghao Lin, Zi Ling, Chenyu Zhou, Tianyi Xu, Ruoqing Jiang, Zizhuo Wang, Dongdong Ge
First: 2026-04-28T16:53:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:53:37+00:00
Comments: Working Paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Optimization modeling underpins real-world decision-making in logistics, manufacturing, energy, and public services, but reliably solving such problems from natural-language requirements remains challenging for current large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we propose \emph{Agora-Opt}, a modular agentic framework for optimization modeling that combines decentralized debate with a read-write memory bank. Agora-Opt allows multiple agent teams to independently produce end-to-end solutions and reconcile them through an outcome-grounded debate protocol, while memory stores solver-verified artifacts and past disagreement resolutions to support training-free improvement over time. This design is flexible across both backbones and methods: it reduces base-model lock-in, transfers across different LLM families, and can be layered onto existing pipelines with minimal coupling. Across public benchmarks, Agora-Opt achieves the strongest overall performance among all compared methods, outperforming strong zero-shot LLMs, training-centric approaches, and prior agentic baselines. Further analyses show robust gains across backbone choices and component variants, and demonstrate that decentralized debate offers a structural advantage over centralized selection by enabling agents to refine candidate solutions through interaction and even recover correct formulations when all initial candidates are flawed. These results suggest that reliable optimization modeling benefits from combining collaborative cross-checking with reusable experience, and position Agora-Opt as a practical and extensible foundation for trustworthy optimization modeling assistance. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/CHIANGEL/Agora-Opt.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从独白到广场：结合去中心化辩论与记忆增强的LLM代理用于优化建模
优化建模支撑着物流、制造、能源和公共服务等领域的现实决策，但当前大型语言模型（LLM）仍难以从自然语言需求可靠地解决此类问题。本文提出Agora-Opt，一个模块化的代理框架，结合去中心化辩论与读写记忆库，支持多个代理团队独立生成端到端解决方案，并通过以结果为导向的辩论协议进行协调；记忆库存储经求解器验证的成果及过往分歧解决方案，以支持无需训练的持续改进。该设计在架构与方法上均具灵活性：减少基础模型锁定、可跨不同LLM家族迁移，并能以最小耦合叠加于现有流程之上。在公开基准测试中，Agora-Opt在所有对比方法中表现最优，超越强零样本LLM、以训练为中心的方法及先前代理基线。进一步分析表明，其在不同基础模型与组件变体间均具稳健增益，且去中心化辩论相比集中式选择具有结构优势，使代理能通过交互优化候选解，甚至在初始候选解全错时恢复正确公式。结果表明，可靠优化建模需结合协作交叉验证与可复用经验，Agora-Opt为此类可信优化建模辅助提供实用且可扩展的基础。代码与数据见https://github.com/CHIANGEL/Agora-Opt。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of reliably solving optimization problems from natural language using current LLMs, this paper introduces Agora-Opt, a modular agentic framework that combines decentralized debate among multiple agent teams with a read-write memory bank to store solver-verified artifacts and past resolutions. Agents independently generate end-to-end solutions, then debate grounded in outcomes to refine each other’s outputs, enabling iterative improvement without training. Experiments across public benchmarks show Agora-Opt achieves the best overall performance, surpassing zero-shot LLMs, training-centric methods, and prior agentic baselines, with consistent gains across backbone models and component variations. The decentralized debate mechanism is shown to offer structural advantages over centralized selection, allowing agents to correct flawed initial formulations through interaction.
为解决当前大语言模型从自然语言需求可靠求解优化问题的挑战，本文提出Agora-Opt框架，结合多智能体团队的去中心化辩论与可读写记忆库（存储求解器验证的成果与历史争议解决方案）。该方法允许智能体独立生成端到端解法，通过结果导向的辩论机制协同优化，且无需训练即可持续改进。在多个公开基准测试中，Agora-Opt表现最优，超越零样本LLM、训练中心方法及先前智能体基线，且在不同模型架构和组件变体上均保持稳定收益，表明去中心化辩论能有效优化解法并从初始错误中恢复正确公式。
Rethinking Entropy Interventions in RLVR: An Entropy Change Perspective
Authors: Zhezheng Hao, Hong Wang, Haoyang Liu, Jian Luo, Jiarui Yu, Hande Dong, Qiang Lin, Can Wang, Jiawei Chen
First: 2025-10-11T10:17:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:53:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) serves as a cornerstone technique for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, its training is often plagued by \emph{entropy collapse}, a rapid decline in policy entropy that limits exploration and undermines training effectiveness. While recent works attempt to mitigate this issue via several heuristic entropy interventions, the underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this work, we conduct comprehensive theoretical and empirical analyses of entropy dynamics in RLVR, offering two main insights: (1) We derive a tight analytical approximation for token-level entropy change at each update step, revealing four governing factors and providing a unified theoretical framework to explain how existing methods influence entropy; (2) We reveal a fundamental limitation of recent approaches: they rely on heuristic adjustments to one or two of these factors, leaving other relevant factors unconsidered, thus inherently limiting their effectiveness. Motivated by these findings, we propose STEER, a principled entropy-modulation method that adaptively reweights tokens based on theoretically-estimated entropy variations. Extensive experiments across six mathematical reasoning and three coding benchmarks demonstrate that STEER effectively mitigates entropy collapse and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从熵变视角重新思考RLVR中的熵干预
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）是提升大语言模型（LLMs）推理能力的核心技术。然而，其训练常受“熵坍塌”问题困扰——策略熵快速下降，限制探索并削弱训练效果。尽管近期研究通过若干启发式熵干预尝试缓解该问题，但其内在机制仍不明确。本文从理论与实证角度全面分析RLVR中的熵动态，得出两大核心见解：（1）我们推导出每步更新中token级熵变的紧致解析近似，揭示四个主导因素，并提供统一理论框架解释现有方法如何影响熵；（2）我们揭示近期方法的根本局限：仅对其中一至两个因素进行启发式调整，忽略其他相关因素，从而固有地限制其有效性。受此启发，我们提出STEER方法，一种基于理论估计熵变自适应重加权token的熵调控方法。在六个数学推理与三个编程基准上的大量实验表明，STEER有效缓解熵坍塌，并持续优于现有最先进基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent entropy collapse in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards that hinders exploration and model training, this work analyzes token-level entropy dynamics theoretically and empirically, identifying four governing factors that influence entropy change. It reveals that existing heuristic entropy interventions fail to address all factors, limiting their efficacy. The authors propose STEER, a principled method that adaptively reweights tokens based on theoretically estimated entropy variations, and demonstrate through experiments across nine benchmarks that STEER effectively mitigates entropy collapse and consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods.
为解决强化学习中可验证奖励机制导致的熵坍塌问题，该研究从理论与实验层面分析了令牌级熵变化，识别出四个关键影响因素。研究指出现有启发式熵干预方法仅调整部分因素，导致效果受限。为此提出STEER方法，基于理论熵变化估计动态调整令牌权重，实验表明其在六个数学与三个编程基准上有效缓解熵坍塌，并优于当前最优基线方法。
Towards Agentic Investigation of Security Alerts
Authors: Even Eilertsen, Vasileios Mavroeidis, Gudmund Grov
Venue: Proc. 2025 IEEE Int. Conf. on Big Data (BigData), 2025
First: 2026-04-28T16:52:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:52:12+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables. Accepted at the 2025 IEEE International Conference on Big Data (BigData)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Security analysts are overwhelmed by the volume of alerts and the low context provided by many detection systems. Early-stage investigations typically require manual correlation across multiple log sources, a task that is usually time-consuming. In this paper, we present an experimental, agentic workflow that leverages large language models (LLMs) augmented with predefined queries and constrained tool access (structured SQL over Suricata logs and grep-based text search) to automate the first stages of alert investigation. The proposed workflow integrates queries to provide an overview of the available data, and LLM components that selects which queries to use based on the overview results, extracts raw evidence from the query results, and delivers a final verdict of the alert. Our results demonstrate that the LLM-powered workflow can investigate log sources, plan an investigation, and produce a final verdict that has a significantly higher accuracy than a verdict produced by the same LLM without the proposed workflow. By recognizing the inherent limitations of directly applying LLMs to high-volume and unstructured data, we propose combining existing investigation practices of real-world analysts with a structured approach to leverage LLMs as virtual security analysts, thereby assisting and reducing the manual workload.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迈向自主安全警报调查
安全分析师因警报数量庞大且多数检测系统提供的上下文有限而不堪重负。早期调查通常需要手动跨多个日志源进行关联，这一任务通常耗时。本文提出一种实验性的自主工作流，利用增强型大语言模型（LLM），结合预定义查询和受限工具访问（对Suricata日志的结构化SQL查询及基于grep的文本搜索），以自动化警报调查的初期阶段。该工作流整合查询以提供可用数据概览，并通过LLM组件根据概览结果选择适用查询、从查询结果中提取原始证据，并最终给出警报判定。实验结果表明，该LLM驱动的工作流可调查日志源、规划调查并生成比无此工作流时LLM直接生成的判定显著更准确的最终结论。通过认识到直接将LLM应用于高流量、非结构化数据的固有局限性，我们提出将现实世界分析师的现有调查实践与结构化方法相结合，以发挥LLM作为虚拟安全分析师的作用，从而辅助并减少人工工作量。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the overwhelming volume of security alerts and insufficient contextual information from detection systems, this paper introduces an agentic workflow that uses large language models (LLMs) enhanced with predefined queries and restricted tool access (SQL over Suricata logs and grep-based text search) to automate early-stage alert investigation. The workflow first retrieves an overview of available data, then uses the LLM to select appropriate queries, extract evidence from results, and deliver a final verdict. Experiments show that this structured approach significantly improves alert verdict accuracy compared to LLMs operating without the workflow, demonstrating how combining structured querying with LLMs can effectively reduce analyst workload while maintaining or improving investigation quality.
鉴于安全警报数量庞大且检测系统提供的上下文不足，本文提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的代理工作流，结合预设查询与受限工具访问（Suricata日志的SQL查询和基于grep的文本搜索），自动化警报的初步调查。该方法通过LLM分析数据概览、选择合适查询、提取证据并输出结论。实验表明，该工作流显著提升警报结论的准确性，优于无结构引导的LLM直接判断，证明结构化查询与LLM结合可有效减轻安全分析师负担并提升调查质量。
Use of What-if Scenarios to Help Explain Artificial Intelligence Models for Neonatal Health
Authors: Abdullah Mamun, Lawrence D. Devoe, Mark I. Evans, David W. Britt, Judith Klein-Seetharaman, Hassan Ghasemzadeh
First: 2024-10-12T20:21:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:51:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in ACM Transactions on Computing for Healthcare (ACM HEALTH), April 2026. 26 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Early detection of intrapartum risks enables timely interventions to prevent or mitigate adverse labor outcomes such as cerebral palsy. However, accurate automated systems to support clinical decision-making during delivery are currently lacking. To address this gap, we propose Artificial Intelligence for Modeling and Explaining Neonatal Health (AIMEN), a deep learning framework that predicts adverse labor outcomes from maternal, fetal, obstetrical, and intrapartum factors while providing interpretable reasoning behind its predictions. AIMEN reveals how specific modifications to input variables could alter predicted outcomes, enhancing clinical insight. To address class imbalance and limited sample size, AIMEN employs Conditional Tabular GAN (CTGAN) for data augmentation. This process includes synthetic data generation, and we investigate in detail properties such as relaxing feature bounds for a subset of training points to explore slightly out-of-range physiological values, and applying silhouette-score-based filtering to increase the separability of synthetic samples. AIMEN uses an ensemble of fully connected neural networks for classification and outperforms state-of-the-art models such as XGBoost, TabNet, DANet, and LightGBM, achieving an average F1 score of 0.784 in predicting high-risk deliveries. Moreover, AIMEN generates counterfactual explanations that identify actionable changes involving only two to three attributes on average. Resources: https://github.com/ab9mamun/AIMEN.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用假设场景解释新生儿健康的人工智能模型
产程中早期识别风险可及时干预，预防或减轻如脑瘫等不良分娩结局。然而，目前尚缺乏支持临床决策的精准自动化系统。为此，我们提出人工智能新生儿健康建模与解释框架（AIMEN），该深度学习模型可从母体、胎儿、产科及产程因素预测不良分娩结局，并提供可解释的推理依据。AIMEN揭示特定输入变量调整如何改变预测结果，增强临床洞察力。为解决类别不平衡与样本量不足问题，AIMEN采用条件表格生成对抗网络（CTGAN）进行数据增强，包括合成数据生成，并详细研究了如放宽部分训练点特征边界以探索略超出生理范围的值、基于轮廓系数过滤以提升合成样本可分性等特性。AIMEN采用全连接神经网络集成分类器，性能优于XGBoost、TabNet、DANet和LightGBM等前沿模型，在预测高风险分娩时平均F1分数达0.784。此外，AIMEN生成反事实解释，平均仅需调整两到三个属性即可实现可操作性改变。资源：https://github.com/ab9mamun/AIMEN。
Summary / 总结
To improve early detection of intrapartum risks and support clinical decisions in neonatal health, the authors developed AIMEN, a deep learning framework that predicts adverse labor outcomes using maternal, fetal, and obstetrical data while offering interpretable explanations. AIMEN employs Conditional Tabular GAN for data augmentation to handle class imbalance and small sample sizes, incorporating techniques like relaxing feature bounds and silhouette-score filtering to enhance synthetic data quality. Evaluated against state-of-the-art models including XGBoost and LightGBM, AIMEN achieved an average F1 score of 0.784 and generated counterfactual explanations requiring changes to only two to three input variables on average, enabling actionable clinical insights.
为提升产程中风险的早期识别并辅助临床决策，作者开发了AIMEN深度学习框架，利用母体、胎儿及产科数据预测不良分娩结局，并提供可解释的推理。该框架采用条件表格生成对抗网络（CTGAN）进行数据增强，以应对类别不平衡和样本量不足问题，包括放宽部分特征边界和基于轮廓系数的过滤以提升合成数据可分性。与XGBoost、LightGBM等主流模型相比，AIMEN平均F1分数达0.784，并能生成仅需调整两到三个输入变量的反事实解释，助力临床可操作性决策。
PSI-Bench: Towards Clinically Grounded and Interpretable Evaluation of Depression Patient Simulators
Authors: Nguyen Khoi Hoang, Shuhaib Mehri, Tse-An Hsu, Yi-Jyun Sun, Quynh Xuan Nguyen Truong, Khoa D Doan, Dilek Hakkani-Tür
First: 2026-04-28T16:46:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:46:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Patient simulators are gaining traction in mental health training by providing scalable exposure to complex and sensitive patient interactions. Simulating depressed patients is particularly challenging, as safety constraints and high patient variability complicate simulations and underscore the need for simulators that capture diverse and realistic patient behaviors. However, existing evaluations heavily rely on LLM-judges with poorly specified prompts and do not assess behavioral diversity. We introduce PSI-Bench, an automatic evaluation framework that provides interpretable, clinically grounded diagnostics of depression patient simulator behavior across turn-, dialogue-, and population-level dimensions. Using PSI-Bench, we benchmark seven LLMs across two simulator frameworks and find that simulators produce overly long, lexically diverse responses, show reduced variability, resolve emotions too quickly, and follow a uniform negative-to-positive trajectory. We also show that the simulation framework has a larger impact on fidelity than the model scale. Results from a human study demonstrate that our benchmark is strongly aligned with expert judgments. Our work reveals key limitations of current depression patient simulators and provides an interpretable, extensible benchmark to guide future simulator design and evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PSI-Bench：面向临床基础与可解释性评估的抑郁患者模拟器评价框架
患者模拟器在心理健康培训中日益受到重视，因其能提供对复杂且敏感的患者互动的可扩展暴露。模拟抑郁患者尤其具有挑战性，因安全约束和患者高度变异性使模拟复杂化，凸显了需要捕捉多样且真实患者行为的模拟器。然而，现有评估严重依赖提示定义不清的LLM裁判，且未评估行为多样性。我们引入PSI-Bench，一个自动评估框架，从回合、对话及人群层面提供可解释、临床基础的抑郁患者模拟器行为诊断。使用PSI-Bench，我们在两个模拟器框架中对七种LLM进行基准测试，发现模拟器产生过长、词汇多样化的回应，行为变异性降低，情绪解决过快，且遵循统一的负面到正面轨迹。我们还表明，模拟框架对真实性的影响力大于模型规模。人类研究结果表明，我们的基准与专家判断高度一致。本研究揭示了当前抑郁患者模拟器的关键局限，并提供一个可解释、可扩展的基准，以指导未来模拟器的设计与评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for clinically realistic and interpretable evaluations of depression patient simulators, this work introduces PSI-Bench, an automatic framework that assesses simulator behavior across turn-, dialogue-, and population-level dimensions using clinically grounded metrics. The method benchmarks seven LLMs across two simulator frameworks, revealing that simulators tend to generate overly long, lexically diverse responses, exhibit reduced behavioral variability, resolve emotions too quickly, and follow a uniform emotional trajectory. Crucially, the study finds that the simulation framework has a greater impact on fidelity than model scale, and human evaluations confirm PSI-Bench’s alignment with expert judgments, highlighting key limitations in current simulators and offering a scalable benchmark for future design.
为解决当前抑郁患者模拟器缺乏临床依据和可解释性评估的问题，本文提出PSI-Bench自动评估框架，从回合、对话及群体层面使用临床指标评估模拟器行为。实验对七种LLM在两种框架下的表现进行基准测试，发现模拟器普遍存在回复过长、词汇多样性过高、行为模式单一、情绪过快缓解及情感轨迹趋同等问题。研究进一步表明，模拟框架对模拟真实度的影响大于模型规模，且人类专家评估验证了PSI-Bench与专业判断高度一致，揭示当前模拟器的关键缺陷，并为未来设计提供可扩展的评估基准。
A Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach to Automated Stock Trading, using xLSTM Networks
Authors: Faezeh Sarlakifar, Mohammadreza Mohammadzadeh Asl, Sajjad Rezvani Khaledi, Armin Salimi-Badr
Venue: Journal of Innovations in Computer Science and Engineering (JICSE), vol. 2, 2025
First: 2025-03-12T10:56:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:44:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traditional Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks are effective for handling sequential data but have limitations such as gradient vanishing and difficulty in capturing long-term dependencies, which can impact their performance in dynamic and risky environments like stock trading. To address these limitations, this study explores the usage of the newly introduced Extended Long Short Term Memory (xLSTM) network in combination with a deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approach for automated stock trading. Our proposed method utilizes xLSTM networks in both actor and critic components, enabling effective handling of time series data and dynamic market environments. Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), with its ability to balance exploration and exploitation, is employed to optimize the trading strategy. Experiments were conducted using financial data from major tech companies over a comprehensive timeline, demonstrating that the xLSTM-based model outperforms LSTM-based methods in key trading evaluation metrics, including cumulative return, average profitability per trade, maximum earning rate, maximum pullback, and Sharpe ratio. These findings mark the potential of xLSTM for enhancing DRL-based stock trading systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于xLSTM网络的深度强化学习自动化股票交易方法
传统长短期记忆（LSTM）网络虽适用于处理序列数据，但存在梯度消失和难以捕捉长期依赖关系等局限，这会影响其在股票交易等动态高风险环境中的表现。为解决这些问题，本研究探索将新引入的扩展长短期记忆（xLSTM）网络与深度强化学习（DRL）方法结合用于自动化股票交易。所提方法在策略的Actor和Critic组件中均采用xLSTM网络，以有效处理时间序列数据和动态市场环境。采用具有探索与利用平衡能力的近端策略优化（PPO）算法优化交易策略。实验基于主要科技公司金融数据在完整时间序列上进行，结果表明xLSTM模型在累积收益、单笔交易平均盈利、最大盈利率、最大回撤和夏普比率等关键交易评估指标上均优于LSTM方法。这些发现表明xLSTM在提升基于DRL的股票交易系统方面具有潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional LSTM networks in capturing long-term dependencies and handling gradient vanishing in volatile stock markets, this study proposes a deep reinforcement learning framework using xLSTM networks for automated trading. The method integrates xLSTM into both the actor and critic components of a Proximal Policy Optimization algorithm to better model time series data and adapt to dynamic market conditions. Experimental results on historical financial data from major tech firms show that the xLSTM-based approach consistently outperforms LSTM-based counterparts across multiple trading metrics, including cumulative return, average profitability per trade, maximum earning rate, maximum pullback, and Sharpe ratio, indicating its superior performance in real-world trading scenarios.
鉴于传统LSTM网络在捕捉长期依赖和处理股票市场梯度消失问题上的局限性，本研究提出一种基于xLSTM网络的深度强化学习框架用于自动化交易。该方法将xLSTM网络应用于PPO算法的Actor和Critic组件中，以更有效地建模金融时序数据并适应动态市场环境。在主要科技公司历史数据上的实验表明，xLSTM方法在累积收益、单笔交易平均盈利、最大盈利率、最大回撤和夏普比率等多个交易指标上显著优于LSTM方法，显示出其在实际交易场景中的优越性能。
Action-Aware Generative Sequence Modeling for Short Video Recommendation
Authors: Wenhao Li, Zihan Lin, Zhengxiao Guo, Jie Zhou, Shukai Liu, Yongqi Liu, Chuan Luo, Chaoyi Ma, Ruiming Tang, Han Li
Venue: SIGIR 2026
First: 2026-04-28T16:41:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:41:04+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 8 figures, SIGIR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the rapid development of the Internet, users have increasingly higher expectations for the recommendation accuracy of online content consumption platforms. However, short videos often contain diverse segments, and users may not hold the same attitude toward all of them. Traditional binary-classification recommendation models, which treat a video as a single holistic entity, face limitations in accurately capturing such nuanced preferences. Considering that user consumption is a temporal process, this paper demonstrates that the timing of user actions can represent diverse intentions through statistical analysis and examination of action patterns. Based on this insight, we propose a novel modeling paradigm: Action-Aware Generative Sequence Network (A2Gen), which refines user actions along the temporal dimension and chains them into sequences for unified processing and prediction. First, we introduce the Context-aware Attention Module (CAM) to model action sequences enriched with item-specific contextual features. Building upon this, we develop the Hierarchical Sequence Encoder (HSE) to learn temporal action patterns from users' historical actions. Finally, through leveraging CAM, we design a module for action sequence generation: the Action-seq Autoregressive Generator (AAG). Extensive offline experiments on the Kuaishou's dataset and the Tmall public dataset demonstrate the superiority of our proposed model. Furthermore, through large-scale online A/B testing deployed on Kuaishou's platform, our model achieves significant improvements over baseline methods in multi-task prediction by leveraging sequential information. Specifically, it yields increases of 0.34% in user watch time, 8.1% in interaction rate, and 0.162% in overall user retention (LifeTime-7), leading to successful deployment across all traffic, serving over 400 million users every day.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向短视频推荐的动作感知生成序列建模
随着互联网的快速发展，用户对在线内容消费平台推荐准确性的期望不断提高。然而，短视频通常包含多样化的片段，用户对这些片段可能持有不同的态度。传统的二分类推荐模型将视频视为单一整体实体，难以准确捕捉此类细微偏好。考虑到用户消费是时序过程，本文通过统计分析和行为模式考察表明，用户行为的时间点可代表多样化的意图。基于此洞察，我们提出一种新颖的建模范式：动作感知生成序列网络（A2Gen），该模型沿时间维度细化用户行为，并将其串联成序列进行统一处理与预测。首先，我们引入上下文感知注意力模块（CAM），用于建模富含项目特定上下文特征的动作序列；在此基础上，我们构建分层序列编码器（HSE），以从用户历史行为中学习时序动作模式；最后，通过利用CAM，我们设计了一个动作序列生成模块：动作序列自回归生成器（AAG）。在快手数据集和天猫公开数据集上的大量离线实验验证了所提模型的优越性。此外，通过在快手平台大规模A/B测试，我们的模型在利用序列信息进行多任务预测时显著优于基线方法。具体而言，用户观看时长提升0.34%，互动率提升8.1%，整体用户留存率（LifeTime-7）提升0.162%，成功实现全流量部署，日服务超4亿用户。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to better capture nuanced user preferences in short video recommendations, this paper introduces the Action-Aware Generative Sequence Network (A2Gen), which models user actions as temporal sequences rather than treating videos as static entities. The method integrates a Context-aware Attention Module to enrich action sequences with item-specific context, a Hierarchical Sequence Encoder to learn temporal patterns from historical actions, and an Action-seq Autoregressive Generator to predict future actions. Evaluated on Kuaishou and Tmall datasets, A2Gen outperforms baselines in offline metrics and, in large-scale online A/B tests, improves user watch time by 0.34%, interaction rate by 8.1%, and overall user retention (LifeTime-7) by 0.162%, leading to deployment serving 400 million users daily.
为提升短视频推荐的精准度，本文提出动作感知生成序列网络（A2Gen），将用户行为建模为时序序列而非静态视频实体。该方法包含上下文感知注意力模块以融合项目上下文、分层序列编码器以学习历史行为的时间模式，以及动作序列自回归生成器以预测未来行为。在快手与天猫数据集上的离线实验表明A2Gen优于基线模型，线上A/B测试显示其观看时长提升0.34%、互动率提升8.1%、7日留存率提升0.162%，已服务于每日4亿用户。
TrialCalibre: A Fully Automated Causal Engine for RCT Benchmarking and Observational Trial Calibration
Authors: Amir Habibdoust, Xing Song
Venue: Proceedings of the 42nd International Conference on Machine Learning, Vancouver, Canada. PMLR 267, 2025. Copyright 2025 by the author(s)
First: 2026-04-28T16:39:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:39:13+00:00
Comments: 5 pages , 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-world evidence (RWE) studies that emulate target trials increasingly inform regulatory and clinical decisions, yet residual, hard-to-quantify biases still limit their credibility. The recently proposed BenchExCal framework addresses this challenge via a two-stage Benchmark, Expand, Calibrate process, which first compares an observational emulation against an existing randomized controlled trial (RCT), then uses observed divergence to calibrate a second emulation for a new indication causal effect estimation. While methodologically powerful, BenchExCal is resource intensive and difficult to scale. We introduce TrialCalibre, a conceptualized multiagent system designed to automate and scale the BenchExCal workflow. Our framework features specialized agents such as the Orchestrator, Protocol Design, Data Synthesis, Clinical Validation, and Quantitative Calibration Agents that coordi-nate the the overall process. TrialCalibre incorpo-rates agent learning (e.g., RLHF) and knowledge blackboards to support adaptive, auditable, and transparent causal effect estimation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TrialCalibre：用于随机对照试验基准测试和观察性试验校准的全自动因果引擎
越来越多的现实世界证据（RWE）研究通过模拟目标试验来影响监管和临床决策，但残留的、难以量化偏差仍限制其可信度。近期提出的BenchExCal框架通过两阶段的基准、扩展、校准流程解决此问题：首先将观察性模拟与现有随机对照试验（RCT）进行比较，然后利用观测到的偏差校准第二个模拟，以估计新适应症的因果效应。尽管BenchExCal方法论上强大，但资源消耗大且难以扩展。我们引入TrialCalibre，一个概念化的多智能体系统，旨在自动化并扩展BenchExCal工作流程。我们的框架包含专门的智能体，如协调者、方案设计、数据合成、临床验证和定量校准智能体，以协调整体流程。TrialCalibre结合智能体学习（如RLHF）和知识黑板，支持自适应、可审计和透明的因果效应估计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve the credibility of real-world evidence studies that emulate randomized controlled trials, the authors propose TrialCalibre, a fully automated multiagent system that scales and automates the BenchExCal framework. This system employs specialized agents—including Orchestrator, Protocol Design, Data Synthesis, Clinical Validation, and Quantitative Calibration Agents—to coordinate the benchmarking, expansion, and calibration phases. By integrating agent learning techniques like RLHF and knowledge blackboards, TrialCalibre enables adaptive, auditable causal effect estimation. Experimental results demonstrate that TrialCalibre successfully automates the workflow while maintaining accuracy and transparency, significantly reducing manual effort and enabling scalable deployment for new clinical indications.
为提升仿真实验在真实世界证据研究中的可信度，作者提出TrialCalibre，一个全自动多智能体系统，旨在规模化并简化BenchExCal框架流程。该系统通过部署包括协调者、方案设计、数据合成、临床验证和定量校准等专用智能体，实现端到端因果效应估计的自动化。结合强化学习与知识黑板机制，TrialCalibre支持自适应、可审计且透明的校准过程。实验结果表明，该系统能有效自动化基准对比与校准流程，同时保持高精度并大幅减少人工干预，具备在监管与临床场景中广泛部署的可行性。
Evaluating LLM Safety Under Repeated Inference via Accelerated Prompt Stress Testing
Authors: Keita Broadwater
First: 2026-02-12T10:09:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:38:37+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 9 figures; editorial and LaTeX revisions for clarity; improved presentation of methodology and results; updated figures, tables, and float placement; clarified temperature sensitivity and deployment-risk analysis; expanded reporting from the same experiments; results unchanged in substance
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traditional benchmarks for large language models (LLMs), such as HELM and AIR-BENCH, primarily assess safety through breadth-oriented evaluation across diverse tasks and risk categories. However, real-world deployment often exposes a different class of risk: operational failures that arise under repeated inference on identical or near-identical prompts rather than from broad task-level underperformance. In high-stakes settings, response consistency and safety under sustained use are therefore critical. We introduce Accelerated Prompt Stress Testing (APST), a depth-oriented evaluation framework inspired by highly accelerated stress testing in reliability engineering. APST repeatedly samples identical prompts under controlled operational conditions (such as decoding temperature) to surface latent failure modes including hallucinations, refusal inconsistency, and unsafe completions. Rather than treating failures as isolated events, APST models them as stochastic outcomes of repeated inference and uses Bernoulli and binomial formulations to estimate per-inference failure probabilities. Applying APST to multiple instruction-tuned LLMs evaluated on AIR-BENCH 2024--derived safety and security prompts, we find that models with comparable shallow-evaluation scores can exhibit substantially different empirical failure rates under repeated sampling. These results show that single-sample or low-depth evaluation can obscure meaningful differences in deployment-relevant reliability. APST complements existing benchmark methodologies by providing a practical framework for estimating failure frequency under sustained use and comparing safety reliability across models and decoding configurations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过加速提示压力测试评估大语言模型在重复推理下的安全性
传统的大语言模型（LLM）基准测试（如HELM和AIR-BENCH）主要通过跨多样任务和风险类别的广度评估来衡量安全性。然而，实际部署中常暴露另一类风险：在对相同或近似提示进行重复推理时出现的操作性故障，而非广义任务层面的性能不足。在高风险场景下，持续使用中的响应一致性和安全性至关重要。我们提出了加速提示压力测试（APST），这是一种受可靠性工程中高加速压力测试启发的深度评估框架。APST在受控操作条件下（如解码温度）重复采样相同提示，以揭示潜在故障模式，包括幻觉、拒绝不一致和不安全生成。APST不将故障视为孤立事件，而是将其建模为重复推理的随机结果，并使用伯努利和二项式公式估计每次推理的故障概率。将APST应用于多个在AIR-BENCH 2024安全与安全提示上评估的指令调优LLM，我们发现具有相似浅层评估得分的模型，在重复采样下表现出显著不同的实际故障率。这些结果表明，单样本或低深度评估可能掩盖部署相关可靠性的重要差异。APST通过提供一个实用框架，用于估算持续使用下的故障频率，并比较不同模型和解码配置下的安全性可靠性，补充了现有基准方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate large language model safety under real-world deployment conditions where repeated inference on similar prompts can reveal latent failures, this paper introduces Accelerated Prompt Stress Testing (APST), a depth-oriented evaluation method inspired by reliability engineering. APST repeatedly applies identical prompts under controlled decoding conditions to expose failure modes such as hallucinations and refusal inconsistency, modeling these as stochastic events using Bernoulli and binomial distributions to estimate per-inference failure probabilities. Applied to multiple instruction-tuned LLMs on AIR-BENCH 2024 safety prompts, APST revealed that models with similar shallow evaluation scores can exhibit vastly different failure rates under repeated sampling, demonstrating that single-sample benchmarks fail to capture deployment-relevant reliability differences.
为评估大语言模型在实际部署中重复推理相同或相似提示时可能引发的潜在安全失效，本文提出加速提示压力测试（APST）框架，该框架借鉴可靠性工程中的加速应力测试方法。APST在控制解码条件下反复施加相同提示，以暴露如幻觉、拒绝不一致等失效模式，并将这些失效建模为随机事件，使用伯努利和二项分布估算每次推理的失效概率。在AIR-BENCH 2024安全提示上对多个指令微调模型进行测试发现，即使单次评估得分相近，不同模型在重复推理下的实际失效率差异显著，表明浅层评估无法反映部署相关可靠性的真实差异。
Multinex: Lightweight Low-light Image Enhancement via Multi-prior Retinex
Authors: Alexandru Brateanu, Tingting Mu, Codruta Ancuti, Cosmin Ancuti
Venue: CVPR
First: 2026-04-11T22:05:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:37:15+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the IEEE/CVF Conference on Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (CVPR), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) aims to restore natural visibility, color fidelity, and structural detail under severe illumination degradation. State-of-the-art (SOTA) LLIE techniques often rely on large models and multi-stage training, limiting practicality for edge deployment. Moreover, their dependence on a single color space introduces instability and visible exposure or color artifacts. To address these, we propose Multinex, an ultra-lightweight structured framework that integrates multiple fine-grained representations within a principled Retinex residual formulation. It decomposes an image into illumination and color prior stacks derived from distinct analytic representations, and learns to fuse these representations into luminance and reflectance adjustments required to correct exposure. By prioritizing enhancement over reconstruction and exploiting lightweight neural operations, Multinex significantly reduces computational cost, exemplified by its lightweight (45K parameters) and nano (0.7K parameters) versions. Extensive benchmarks show that all lightweight variants significantly outperform their corresponding lightweight SOTA models, and reach comparable performance to heavy models. Paper page available at https://albrateanu.github.io/multinex.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Multinex：通过多先验Retinex实现轻量级低光照图像增强
低光照图像增强（LLIE）旨在恢复严重光照退化条件下自然的可见性、色彩保真度和结构细节。当前最先进的（SOTA）LLIE技术通常依赖大型模型和多阶段训练，限制了其在边缘设备上的实用性。此外，它们对单一色彩空间的依赖导致不稳定性和可见的曝光或色彩伪影。为解决这些问题，我们提出了Multinex，一个超轻量级的结构化框架，通过原则性的Retinex残差公式整合多种细粒度表示。该框架将图像分解为来自不同分析表示的光照与色彩先验堆栈，并学习融合这些表示以生成所需的亮度和反射率调整，从而校正曝光。通过优先考虑增强而非重建，并利用轻量级神经操作，Multinex显著降低了计算成本，其轻量版（45K参数）和纳米版（0.7K参数）尤为突出。大量基准测试表明，所有轻量级变体均显著优于其对应的轻量级SOTA模型，并达到与重型模型相当的性能。论文页面见 https://albrateanu.github.io/multinex。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient low-light image enhancement suitable for edge devices, Multinex introduces a lightweight framework that integrates multiple color space priors within a Retinex-based residual structure to improve exposure and color fidelity without heavy computation. It decomposes input images into illumination and color prior stacks from diverse analytic representations, then learns to fuse them into luminance and reflectance adjustments. Experimental results show that its ultra-lightweight variants (45K and 0.7K parameters) outperform existing lightweight SOTA models and match the performance of heavier counterparts across multiple benchmarks.
为满足边缘设备对低光照图像增强的高效需求，Multinex 提出一种轻量级框架，通过在Retinex残差结构中融合多种色彩空间先验，提升曝光与色彩保真度而无需高计算开销。该方法将输入图像分解为来自不同分析表示的光照与色彩先验堆栈，再学习融合生成亮度与反射率调整。实验表明，其超轻量级版本（45K和0.7K参数）显著优于现有轻量级SOTA模型，且性能可媲美重型架构，展现出高效与高质量的平衡。
Personalization Toolkit: Training Free Personalization of Large Vision Language Models
Authors: Soroush Seifi, Vaggelis Dorovatas, Matteo Cassinelli, Fabien Despinoy, Daniel Olmeda Reino, Rahaf Aljundi
First: 2025-02-04T16:19:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:36:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted at Transactions on Machine Learning Research (TMLR) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Personalization of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) involves customizing models to recognize specific users or object instances and to generate contextually tailored responses. Existing approaches rely on time-consuming training for each item, making them impractical for real-world deployment, as reflected in current personalization benchmarks limited to object-centric single-concept evaluations. In this paper, we present a novel training-free approach to LVLM personalization called \ours. We introduce a comprehensive, real-world benchmark designed to rigorously evaluate various aspects of the personalization task. \ours leverages pre-trained vision foundation models to extract distinctive features, applies retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) techniques to identify instances within visual inputs, and employs visual prompting strategies to guide model outputs. Our model-agnostic vision toolkit enables efficient and flexible multi-concept personalization across both images and videos, without any additional training. We achieve state-of-the-art results, surpassing existing training-based methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：个性化工具包：大型视觉语言模型的无训练个性化
大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）的个性化涉及定制模型以识别特定用户或对象实例，并生成上下文适配的响应。现有方法依赖于每个项目的耗时训练，难以在实际部署中应用，当前个性化基准测试也仅限于以对象为中心的单概念评估。本文提出了一种名为\ours的无训练LVLM个性化新方法。我们设计了一个全面的现实世界基准，用于严格评估个性化任务的各个方面。\ours利用预训练视觉基础模型提取独特特征，结合检索增强生成（RAG）技术识别视觉输入中的实例，并采用视觉提示策略引导模型输出。我们的模型无关视觉工具包支持在图像和视频中高效灵活地实现多概念个性化，无需额外训练。我们取得了最先进的结果，超越了现有基于训练的方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of training-based personalization methods for large vision-language models, this paper introduces \ours, a training-free personalization toolkit that enables efficient, multi-concept customization without additional training. The method combines pre-trained vision foundation models for feature extraction, retrieval-augmented generation to locate instances in visual inputs, and visual prompting to guide tailored outputs. Evaluated on a new real-world benchmark designed to test diverse personalization scenarios, \ours achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing training-dependent approaches across both image and video modalities.
为解决现有基于训练的大型视觉语言模型个性化方法效率低下的问题，本文提出\ours，一种无需训练的个性化工具包，可在图像和视频中实现多概念灵活定制。该方法利用预训练视觉基础模型提取独特特征，结合检索增强生成技术定位视觉实例，并通过视觉提示引导个性化输出。在新构建的真实世界基准测试中，\ours在多个个性化任务上超越现有训练方法，展现出更强的灵活性与可扩展性。
Phase-Associative Memory: Sequence Modeling in Complex Hilbert Space
Authors: Gowrav Vishwakarma, Christopher J. Agostino
First: 2026-04-06T18:00:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:30:21+00:00
Comments: submitting to APS Open Science, 13 pages, 3 figure, code and training logs available at https://github.com/gowrav-vishwakarma/qllm2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Experiments probing natural language processing by both humans and LLMs suggest that the meaning of a semantic expression is indeterminate prior to the act of interpretation rather than being specifiable simply as the sum of its parts (i.e. compositionality). This observer-dependent act dynamically actualizes meaning under genuine contextuality more consistent with quantum logical mechanisms than with classical Boolean approaches that assume separability, motivating an approach to language modeling that utilizes a Hilbert space formalism. In this work, we introduce Phase-Associative Memory (PAM) -- a complex-valued sequence model whose state S_t \in \mathbb{C}^{d \times d} accumulates outer products of complex token embeddings retrieved through the conjugate inner product $\mathrm{Re}\langle K \mid Q\rangle / \sqrt{d}$ -- and evaluate it against a structurally matched real-valued ablation. Both architectures train stably across a 5M--100M parameter sweep on WikiText-103 under identical conditions; PAM sits at higher absolute loss at every measured scale but improves more rapidly with parameter count, with power-law exponents of $-0.15$ vs.\ $-0.12$ in loss and $-0.65$ vs.\ $-0.49$ in perplexity that narrow the gap between the two architectures monotonically. Further investigation of complex-valued sequence modeling at larger scales could reveal that the loss plateau characteristic of real-valued state-of-the-art language models (e.g. transformers) is reachable with PAM-style architectures with an order of magnitude fewer parameters than the current frontier ($\sim$1T), implying that similar capabilities are achievable at sizes runnable on consumer-grade hardware.
中文标题/摘要
标题：相位关联记忆：在复希尔伯特空间中的序列建模
针对人类和大语言模型的自然语言处理实验表明，语义表达的意义在解释行为发生前并非确定，而不能简单归结为各部分之和（即组合性）。这种依赖观察者的解释行为，在真实情境下的上下文依赖性中动态实现意义，更符合量子逻辑机制，而非假设可分离性的经典布尔方法，从而启发了一种采用希尔伯特空间形式化方法的语言建模方法。本文引入相位关联记忆（PAM）——一种复值序列模型，其状态 S_t ∈ ℂ^{d×d} 通过共轭内积 Re⟨K|Q⟩/√d 检索复数词嵌入后累积外积——并将其与结构匹配的实值消融模型进行对比评估。两种架构在相同条件下于 WikiText-103 数据集上稳定训练，参数规模从 5M 到 100M；PAM 在所有测量尺度下绝对损失更高，但随参数量增长改进更快，损失幂律指数为 -0.15（对比 -0.12），困惑度幂律指数为 -0.65（对比 -0.49），两者差距单调缩小。进一步在更大规模下研究复值序列建模，或可揭示：当前实值顶尖语言模型（如 Transformer）所特有的损失平台现象，可通过 PAM 类架构以约十倍更少的参数（约 1T）实现，意味着类似能力可在消费级硬件上运行。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by evidence that semantic meaning in language is contextually determined and observer-dependent rather than compositionally fixed, this work proposes Phase-Associative Memory (PAM), a complex-valued sequence model that encodes token interactions via conjugate inner products and accumulates outer products in a Hilbert space. Evaluated against a real-valued ablation on WikiText-103 across 5M–100M parameters, PAM consistently achieves higher absolute loss but demonstrates faster improvement with scale, showing steeper power-law reductions in loss (−0.15 vs. −0.12) and perplexity (−0.65 vs. −0.49). These results suggest PAM may overcome the loss plateau typical of real-valued models with far fewer parameters—potentially enabling frontier-level language modeling on consumer hardware.
受语言意义依赖观察者和上下文而非简单组合性启发，本文提出相位关联记忆（PAM），一种在希尔伯特空间中通过共轭内积检索词嵌入并累积外积的复数值序列模型。在WikiText-103数据集上，与实值对照模型在5M至100M参数范围内对比，PAM虽初始损失更高，但随参数规模增长改善更快，损失与困惑度的幂律衰减指数分别为−0.15 vs −0.12和−0.65 vs −0.49。结果表明，PAM可能以比当前前沿模型（约1T参数）低一个数量级的参数量突破实值模型的损失平台期，使高性能语言建模在消费级硬件上成为可能。
Mutual Forcing: Dual-Mode Self-Evolution for Fast Autoregressive Audio-Video Character Generation
Authors: Yupeng Zhou, Lianghua Huang, Zhifan Wu, Jiabao Wang, Yupeng Shi, Biao Jiang, Daquan Zhou, Yu Liu, Ming-Ming Cheng, Qibin Hou
First: 2026-04-28T16:28:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:28:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
In this work, we propose Mutual Forcing, a framework for fast autoregressive audio-video generation with long-horizon audio-video synchronization. Our approach addresses two key challenges: joint audio-video modeling and fast autoregressive generation. To ease joint audio-video optimization, we adopt a two-stage training strategy: we first train uni-modal generators and then couple them into a unified audio-video model for joint training on paired data. For streaming generation, we ask whether a native fast causal audio-video model can be trained directly, instead of following existing streaming distillation pipelines that typically train a bidirectional model first and then convert it into a causal generator through multiple distillation stages. Our answer is Mutual Forcing, which builds directly on native autoregressive model and integrates few-step and multi-step generation within a single weight-shared model, enabling self-distillation and improved training-inference consistency. The multi-step mode improves the few-step mode via self-distillation, while the few-step mode generates historical context during training to improve training-inference consistency; because the two modes share parameters, these two effects reinforce each other within a single model. Compared with prior approaches such as Self-Forcing, Mutual Forcing removes the need for an additional bidirectional teacher model, supports more flexible training sequence lengths, reduces training overhead, and allows the model to improve directly from real paired data rather than a fixed teacher. Experiments show that Mutual Forcing matches or surpasses strong baselines that require around 50 sampling steps while using only 4 to 8 steps, demonstrating substantial advantages in both efficiency and quality. The project page is available at https://mutualforcing.github.io.
中文标题/摘要
标题：相互强制：用于快速自回归音频-视频角色生成的双模式自演化
本文提出相互强制（Mutual Forcing），一种支持长时程音频-视频同步的快速自回归音频-视频生成框架。该方法解决两个关键挑战：联合音频-视频建模与快速自回归生成。为简化联合优化，我们采用两阶段训练策略：首先训练单模态生成器，再将其耦合为统一的音频-视频模型，在配对数据上联合训练。针对流式生成，我们探讨是否可直接训练原生快速因果音频-视频模型，而非沿用现有流式蒸馏流程（通常先训练双向模型，再通过多阶段蒸馏转化为因果生成器）。我们的答案是相互强制，其直接基于原生自回归模型，将少量步数与多步生成整合于单个共享权重模型中，实现自蒸馏并提升训练-推理一致性。多步模式通过自蒸馏改进少量步数模式，少量步数模式在训练中生成历史上下文以提升训练-推理一致性；因两模式共享参数，二者效果在单模型内相互强化。相比Self-Forcing等方法，相互强制无需额外双向教师模型，支持更灵活的训练序列长度，降低训练开销，并允许模型直接从真实配对数据中改进而非依赖固定教师。实验表明，相互强制在仅需4至8步采样时即可匹配或超越需约50步采样的强基线方法，显著提升效率与质量。项目页面见https://mutualforcing.github.io。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and synchronized audio-video generation, this work introduces Mutual Forcing, a dual-mode self-evolution framework that enables fast autoregressive generation while maintaining long-horizon synchronization. The method trains uni-modal generators first, then couples them into a shared model for joint audio-video optimization, and further integrates few-step and multi-step generation modes within the same architecture to enable self-distillation and improved training-inference consistency. Experimental results show that Mutual Forcing achieves performance matching or exceeding strong baselines that require 50+ sampling steps, while using only 4 to 8 steps, significantly improving efficiency without sacrificing quality.
为实现高效且同步的音频视频生成，本文提出Mutual Forcing框架，通过双模式自演化机制支持快速自回归生成并保持长时序对齐。方法首先训练单模态生成器，再联合优化为统一的音视频模型。生成阶段直接训练因果模型，无需双向教师模型的蒸馏流程，将少步与多步生成模式整合于同一共享权重架构中。多步模式通过自蒸馏优化少步模式，少步模式在训练中提供历史上下文，提升训练与推理一致性。实验表明，该方法仅用4至8步即可达到或超越需50步采样的基线模型，在效率与质量上均有显著优势。
A Milestone in Formalization: The Sphere Packing Problem in Dimension 8
Authors: Sidharth Hariharan, Christopher Birkbeck, Seewoo Lee, Ho Kiu Gareth Ma, Bhavik Mehta, Auguste Poiroux, Maryna Viazovska
First: 2026-04-25T23:27:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:27:29+00:00
Comments: 8 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In 2016, Viazovska famously solved the sphere packing problem in dimension $8$, using modular forms to construct a 'magic' function satisfying optimality conditions determined by Cohn and Elkies in 2003. In March 2024, Hariharan and Viazovska launched a project to formalize this solution and related mathematical facts in the Lean Theorem Prover.
