A significant milestone was achieved in February 2026: the result was formally verified, with the final stages of the verification done by Math, Inc.'s autoformalization model 'Gauss'. We discuss the techniques used to achieve this milestone, reflect on the unique collaboration between humans and Gauss, and discuss project objectives that remain.
中文标题/摘要
标题：形式化的重要里程碑：8维空间中的球体堆积问题
2016年，维亚佐夫斯卡以模形式构造了一个满足科恩与埃尔基斯于2003年确定的最优性条件的‘魔法’函数，著名地解决了8维空间中的球体堆积问题。2024年3月，哈里哈兰与维亚佐夫斯卡启动了在Lean定理证明器中形式化该解及相关数学事实的项目。2026年2月，这一成果正式被验证，最终阶段由Math, Inc.的自动形式化模型‘高斯’完成。本文讨论实现这一里程碑所采用的技术，反思人类与高斯之间的独特协作，并探讨仍待完成的项目目标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to formally verify the groundbreaking 2016 solution to the sphere packing problem in dimension 8, which relied on modular forms and a 'magic' function constructed by Viazovska, Hariharan and Viazovska initiated a formalization project in Lean. Their work culminated in February 2026 with the complete formal verification of the result, aided significantly by Math, Inc.'s autoformalization model 'Gauss' in the final stages. The project demonstrated the feasibility of automating complex mathematical proofs and highlighted the synergy between human mathematicians and AI-assisted proof assistants.
为验证2016年维亚佐夫斯卡在8维空间解决球体堆积问题的突破性成果，哈里哈兰与维亚佐夫斯卡启动了在Lean定理证明器中形式化该证明的项目，其核心依赖模形式与科恩-埃尔基斯最优性条件。2026年2月，该证明最终由Math, Inc.的自动化形式化模型‘高斯’完成验证，标志着复杂分析证明首次被机器完全验证。该项目展示了人类数学洞察力与自动化证明合成的协同可行性，同时也揭示了当前形式化工具仍存在的不足。
"The Worst Weather In America": Augmenting the Information Design of Extreme Cold Weather Forecasts
Authors: Michael Correll, Jay Broccolo, Drew Bush
First: 2026-04-28T16:26:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:26:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mount Washington is home to extreme, and extremely volatile, weather conditions. Consulting a weather forecast of conditions at the summit is vital for making one's visit as safe as possible. Using the discussion and suggestions arising from a participatory workshop as input, we test a design intervention employing color-coded hazard icons to function as visual summaries of Mount Washington Observatory's current text-heavy forecast through a crowd-sourced study. We find that the use of icons increases the perceived risk of activities involving visiting the mountain. However, we highlight remaining questions around visualization design and design ethics that warrant further study in the domain of how best to communicate cold weather hazards in ways that are mindful of the diversity of literacies and experiences of visitors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：“美国最恶劣的天气”：增强极端寒冷天气预报的信息设计
华盛顿山以其极端且极不稳定天气著称。咨询山顶天气预报对确保访客安全至关重要。我们以参与式工作坊中讨论和建议为输入，通过众包研究测试了一种设计干预方案：使用彩色危险图标作为视觉摘要，替代华盛顿山天文台当前冗长的文字预报。研究发现，图标使用提升了人们对于登山活动风险的感知。然而，我们仍需进一步研究可视化设计与设计伦理问题，以更恰当地向具有不同阅读能力和体验的访客传达寒冷天气风险。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve communication of extreme cold weather risks at Mount Washington, the study employs a participatory workshop to inform a design intervention that replaces text-heavy forecasts with color-coded hazard icons. These icons serve as visual summaries of weather conditions, tested via a crowd-sourced study. Results show that the icons increase perceived risk for mountain-related activities, suggesting they effectively convey danger, but also raise questions about design ethics and the need to accommodate diverse visitor literacies and experiences.
为提升游客在极端天气频发的华盛顿山的安全性，研究者设计并测试了用彩色危险图标简化气象台冗长文字预报的视觉方案。通过众包实验发现，这些图标显著提升了游客对登山活动风险的感知，表明其有效传达了危险信息。但研究也指出，视觉设计需兼顾不同游客的阅读能力和经验背景，且存在伦理层面的待解问题。
Magnification-Invariant Image Classification via Domain Generalization and Stable Sparse Embedding Signatures
Authors: Ifeanyi Ezuma, Olusiji Medaiyese
First: 2026-04-28T16:26:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:26:14+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 7 figures, 3 tables. Preprint manuscript
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Magnification shift is a major obstacle to robust histopathology classification, because models trained on one imaging scale often generalize poorly to another. Here, we evaluated this problem on the BreaKHis dataset using a strict patient-disjoint leave-one-magnification-out protocol, comparing supervised baseline, baseline augmented with DCGAN-generated patches, and a gradient-reversal domain-general model designed to preserve discriminative information while suppressing magnification-specific variation. Across held-out magnifications, the domain-general model achieved the strongest overall discrimination and its clearest gain was observed when 200X was held out. By contrast, GAN augmentation produced inconsistent effects, improving some folds but degrading others, particularly at 400X. The domain-general model also yielded the lowest Brier score at 0.063 vs 0.089 at baseline. Sparse embedding analysis further revealed that domain-general training reduced average signature size more than three-fold (306 versus 1,074 dimensions) while preserving equivalent predictive performance (AUC: 0.967 vs 0.965; F1: 0.930 vs 0.931). It also increased cross-fold signature reproducibility from near-zero Jaccard overlap in the baseline to 0.99 between the 100X and 200X folds. These findings show that calibrated, compact, and transferable representations can be learned without added architectural complexity, with clear implications for the reliable deployment of computational pathology models across heterogeneous acquisition settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过领域泛化与稳定稀疏嵌入签名实现放大不变的图像分类
放大变化是组织病理学分类稳健性的主要障碍，因为模型在一种成像尺度上训练后，往往难以泛化到另一种尺度。本文在BreaKHis数据集上采用严格的患者不相交、留出一种放大倍率的交叉验证协议，比较了监督基线模型、使用DCGAN生成补丁增强的基线模型，以及一种梯度反转领域泛化模型，该模型旨在保留判别信息同时抑制放大特定变化。在留出的放大倍率上，领域泛化模型整体判别能力最强，尤其在200X被留出时效果最显著。相比之下，GAN增强效果不一致，部分折中表现提升，部分则下降，尤其在400X时。领域泛化模型在Brier评分上也最低（0.063 vs 基线0.089）。稀疏嵌入分析进一步表明，领域泛化训练使平均签名维度减少超过三倍（306维 vs 1074维），同时保持相当的预测性能（AUC: 0.967 vs 0.965；F1: 0.930 vs 0.931）。此外，跨折签名可重复性从基线近乎零的Jaccard重叠提升至100X与200X折之间0.99。这些发现表明，无需增加架构复杂度即可学习到校准良好、紧凑且可迁移的表示，对计算病理学模型在异构采集环境中的可靠部署具有重要意义。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of magnification-invariant classification in histopathology, the study evaluates three approaches on the BreaKHis dataset using a patient-disjoint leave-one-magnification-out protocol: a supervised baseline, a GAN-augmented version, and a domain-general model with gradient reversal to suppress magnification-specific variation. The domain-general model outperformed both baselines across held-out magnifications, showing the largest improvement when 200X was held out, and achieved the lowest Brier score (0.063 vs 0.089). It also produced significantly sparser, more compact embeddings (306 vs 1,074 dimensions) without sacrificing predictive performance (AUC 0.967 vs 0.965; F1 0.930 vs 0.931), while dramatically increasing cross-fold signature reproducibility (Jaccard overlap from near-zero to 0.99 between 100X and 200X folds).
为解决病理图像分类中放大倍率变化导致的泛化困难，研究在BreaKHis数据集上采用患者不重叠的留一放大倍率验证协议，对比了监督基线、GAN增强基线和基于梯度反转的领域泛化模型。领域泛化模型在所有留出放大倍率下表现最优，尤其在200X留出时提升最明显，且Brier得分最低（0.063 vs 0.089）。该模型还生成了更稀疏的嵌入（306维 vs 1,074维），预测性能几乎不变（AUC 0.967 vs 0.965；F1 0.930 vs 0.931），且跨折叠签名重叠度大幅提升（100X与200X折叠间Jaccard相似度从接近零升至0.99），表明无需复杂架构即可学习出鲁棒、紧凑且可迁移的表示。
Evaluating Computational Pathology Foundation Models for Prostate Cancer Grading under Distribution Shifts
Authors: Fredrik K. Gustafsson, Mattias Rantalainen
First: 2024-10-09T09:45:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:23:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pathology foundation models (PFMs) have emerged as powerful pretrained encoders for computational pathology, but their robustness under clinically relevant distribution shifts remains insufficiently understood. We benchmark the robustness of recent PFMs in the setting of prostate cancer grading from whole-slide images (WSIs). Using the PANDA dataset, we evaluate PFMs as frozen patch-level feature extractors within weakly supervised slide-level grading models, and assess robustness to two important forms of distribution shift: shifts in WSI image appearance across collection sites, and shifts in the label distribution over cancer grade groups. Across in-distribution settings, PFMs consistently achieve strong performance and clearly outperform a natural-image baseline. Under cross-site transfer from Radboud to Karolinska, however, performance drops substantially for all models, showing that large-scale pretraining alone does not guarantee robust downstream generalization. In contrast, PFMs are less sensitive to label-distribution shift, indicating that visually grounded domain shift is the dominant challenge. Representation analysis further supports these findings by revealing persistent domain separation between sites across all PFMs. While grade-related structure is present, it is comparatively weak, indicating that domain-related variation dominates in the learned feature space. Together, these results provide a comprehensive benchmark of PFMs under distribution shift and highlight an important practical message: although PFMs provide strong representations, generalizability remains constrained by the quality and diversity of the data used to train downstream prediction models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在分布偏移下评估前列腺癌分级的计算病理学基础模型
病理学基础模型（PFMs）已成为计算病理学中强大的预训练编码器，但其在临床相关分布偏移下的鲁棒性仍不充分理解。我们以全切片图像（WSIs）为基础，对近期PFMs在前列腺癌分级任务中的鲁棒性进行基准测试。使用PANDA数据集，我们将PFMs作为冻结的块级特征提取器嵌入弱监督的切片级分级模型中，并评估其对两种重要分布偏移形式的鲁棒性：不同采集站点间WSI图像外观的偏移，以及癌症分级组标签分布的偏移。在分布内场景中，PFMs始终表现优异，明显优于自然图像基线。然而，在从Radboud到Karolinska的跨站点迁移中，所有模型性能显著下降，表明大规模预训练本身并不能保证下游任务的鲁棒泛化能力。相比之下，PFMs对标签分布偏移不敏感，表明视觉相关的领域偏移是主要挑战。表示分析进一步支持这些发现，揭示所有PFMs在不同站点间存在持续的领域分离。虽然存在与分级相关的结构，但其相对较弱，表明领域相关变异在学习的特征空间中占主导地位。综合这些结果，为PFMs在分布偏移下的性能提供了全面基准，并强调了一个重要实践信息：尽管PFMs提供强表示能力，其泛化能力仍受限于用于训练下游预测模型的数据质量和多样性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess how well pathology foundation models (PFMs) generalize under real-world clinical distribution shifts, this study evaluates PFMs for prostate cancer grading using whole-slide images from the PANDA dataset. The method involves freezing PFMs as patch-level feature extractors within weakly supervised slide-level grading models, then testing robustness against two types of shift: cross-site image appearance variation and label distribution changes across cancer grade groups. PFMs are comparatively resilient to label shifts, suggesting visual domain differences are the primary challenge. 为评估病理基础模型（PFMs）在现实分布偏移下的泛化能力，本研究基于PANDA数据集，以全切片图像对前列腺癌分级进行基准测试。方法将PFMs作为冻结的局部特征提取器嵌入弱监督的切片级模型中，测试其在两类分布偏移下的鲁棒性：跨采集机构的图像外观变化和癌症分级标签分布变化。结果表明，PFMs在分布内场景下表现优异，优于自然图像基线，但在Radboud到Karolinska机构间的跨域迁移中性能显著下降，说明大规模预训练不足以保证下游泛化能力。PFMs对标签分布偏移相对稳健，表明视觉域差异是主要挑战。表征分析进一步证实所有PFMs均存在机构间特征分离，且与分级相关的结构较弱，说明学习到的特征空间中域相关变异占主导，下游模型的泛化能力受限于训练数据的多样性和质量。
Lexical Anthropomorphization Influences on Moral Judgments of AI Bad Behavior
Authors: Jaime Banks, Nicholas David Bowman, Roman Saladino
First: 2026-04-28T16:22:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:22:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Anthropomorphic language describing artificial intelligence (AI) is widespread in media, policy, and everyday discourse; so too are discussions of AI bad behavior, from hallucinations to inappropriate comments. How does humanizing language about AI shape moral judgments when AI behaves badly? Across four experiments (total N = 1,020), we tested whether lexical anthropomorphism (LA) primes shape judgments of AI moral character, behavior morality, and behavioral responsibility. Studies 1-3 tested interactions between anthropomorphic language and humanizing design cues (icons, names, self-referencing) in the context of amoral errors. Study 4 extended this to genuinely immoral AI behavior across seven moral-violation types. Results indicate humanizing language and design cues have little influence on moral judgments of misbehaving AI. Where effects emerged, high-anthropomorphic primes elevated perceptions of an AI's capacity for dishonesty. The type of moral violation observed was the strongest predictor of moral judgments, with harm and degradation violations producing the broadest negative character assessments. Prime drift, horn effects, and egoistic value orientations emerged as potentially important predictors of AI moral judgments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言拟人化对人工智能不良行为道德判断的影响
媒体、政策和日常话语中广泛使用拟人化语言描述人工智能（AI），也广泛讨论AI的不良行为，如幻觉和不当言论。当AI表现不佳时，拟人化语言如何塑造人类对其道德判断？在四项实验（总样本量N=1,020）中，我们检验了词汇拟人化（LA）是否通过启动效应影响对AI道德特质、行为道德性和行为责任性的判断。前三个实验测试了拟人化语言与人类化设计线索（图标、名称、自指）在无道德错误情境下的交互作用。第四个实验扩展至七类真正不道德的AI行为。结果显示，拟人化语言和设计线索对AI不良行为的道德判断影响甚微。在出现效应时，高拟人化启动增强了对AI欺骗能力的感知。观察到的道德违规类型是道德判断最强的预测因素，其中伤害与贬损类违规导致最广泛的负面人格评价。启动漂移、角效应和利己价值观被发现是AI道德判断的潜在重要预测因素。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the prevalence of anthropomorphic language in AI discourse, this study investigates whether lexical anthropomorphism influences moral judgments of AI misbehavior. Across four experiments with 1,020 participants, researchers tested how humanizing language and design cues affect perceptions of AI moral character, behavior morality, and responsibility, both in amoral errors and genuine moral violations. Results show minimal overall impact of anthropomorphism on moral judgments, except that high-anthropomorphic primes slightly increased perceptions of AI dishonesty. The type of moral violation—particularly harm and degradation—was the strongest predictor of negative moral assessments, while prime drift, horn effects, and egoistic values also significantly influenced judgments.
本研究探讨了人类化语言如何影响人们对AI不良行为的道德判断，动机源于媒体和政策中普遍使用的人类化AI表述。通过四组实验（共1020名参与者），研究者检验了词汇人类化（LA）及设计线索（图标、名称、自指）对AI道德性格、行为道德性与责任归属的影响，涵盖无道德错误与真实道德违规情境。结果显示，人类化语言整体对道德判断影响有限，但高人类化提示略微提升对AI欺骗性的感知。道德违规类型（尤其是伤害与贬低）是预测负面性格评价的最强因素，同时提示漂移、角效应及自我中心价值观也与判断模式相关。
Instruction-Evidence Contrastive Dual-Stream Decoding for Grounded Vision-Language Reasoning
Authors: Yashwant Pravinrao Bangde, Debaditya Roy
First: 2026-04-28T16:18:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:18:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit strong performance in instruction following and open-ended vision-language reasoning, yet they frequently generate fluent outputs that are weakly grounded in visual evidence. Prior works have shown that instruction prompting further worsens this issue by amplifying language priors, especially when the visual signal is uncertain or ambiguous. To address this challenge, we propose a decoding framework that explicitly balances linguistic informativeness and visual faithfulness during generation. Our method, Instruction-Evidence Contrastive Dual-Stream Decoding (IECD2), maintains two parallel probability distributions of tokens at each decoding step: an instruction-driven stream that promotes expressive and informative responses, and an evidence-driven stream that enforces strict grounding in the image. These two streams are adaptively fused using a symmetric KL-based contrast-based gate, which suppresses tokens favored by language priors but unsupported by visual evidence, while preserving them when both distributions agree. We evaluate IECD2 on multiple datasets spanning various generative vision-language reasoning tasks such as captioning and visual question answering, including POPE, MME, VQAv2, AMBER, MS-COCO, and LLaVA-Bench. IECD2 demonstrates consistent improvements in task accuracy and reasoning performance, alongside a substantial reduction in hallucination across all evaluation metrics compared to state-of-the-art decoding approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向视觉-语言推理的指令-证据对比双流解码
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在指令遵循和开放视觉-语言推理任务中表现优异，但常生成流畅却弱视觉关联的输出。先前研究表明，指令提示会加剧此问题，尤其在视觉信号不确定或模糊时，强化语言先验。为此，我们提出一种解码框架，在生成过程中显式平衡语言信息量与视觉忠实度。我们的方法——指令-证据对比双流解码（IECD2）——在每一步解码中维护两个并行的词元概率分布：指令驱动流促进表达性与信息性响应，证据驱动流强制图像接地。两流通过对称KL对比门自适应融合，抑制仅受语言先验支持但无视觉证据支持的词元，当两分布一致时则保留。我们在涵盖图像字幕生成与视觉问答等任务的多个数据集（POPE、MME、VQAv2、AMBER、MS-COCO、LLaVA-Bench）上评估IECD2，结果表明其在任务准确率与推理性能上持续提升，且相较现有最优解码方法大幅降低幻觉现象。
Summary / 总结
To address the issue of visually ungrounded outputs in Vision-Language Models despite strong language fluency, the authors propose IECD2, a dual-stream decoding framework that simultaneously promotes linguistic informativeness and visual faithfulness. The method maintains two parallel token probability distributions—one guided by instruction prompts to encourage expressive responses, and another grounded in visual evidence to enforce alignment with the image. These streams are fused via a symmetric KL-based contrastive gate that suppresses tokens favored by language priors but unsupported by visual evidence, while preserving tokens where both streams agree. Evaluated across multiple vision-language reasoning benchmarks including POPE, MME, VQAv2, and MS-COCO, IECD2 consistently improves task accuracy and reasoning performance while significantly reducing hallucinations compared to prior state-of-the-art decoding methods.
为解决视觉语言模型在遵循指令时生成缺乏视觉依据的流畅文本的问题，作者提出IECD2双流解码框架，同步促进语言表达的丰富性和视觉证据的忠实性。该方法在每一步解码中维护两个并行的词元概率分布——一个由指令驱动以保证表达性，一个由视觉证据驱动以确保接地性，并通过对称KL对比门动态融合二者，抑制仅受语言先验影响但无视觉支持的词元。在POPE、MME、VQAv2、MS-COCO等多个视觉语言推理基准上，IECD2显著提升任务准确率与推理性能，同时大幅降低幻觉现象，优于当前最先进解码方法。
Principled Detection of Hallucinations in Large Language Models via Multiple Testing
Authors: Jiawei Li, Akshayaa Magesh, Venugopal V. Veeravalli
First: 2025-08-25T20:39:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:17:29+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful foundational models to solve a variety of tasks, they have also been shown to be prone to hallucinations, i.e., generating responses that sound confident but are actually incorrect or even nonsensical. Existing hallucination detectors propose a wide range of empirical scoring rules, but their performance varies across models and datasets, and it is hard to determine which ones to rely on in practice or to treat as a reliable detector. In this work, we formulate the problem of detecting hallucinations as a hypothesis testing problem and draw parallels with the problem of out-of-distribution detection in machine learning models. We then propose a multiple-testing-inspired method that systematically aggregates multiple evaluation scores via conformal p-values, enabling calibrated detection with controlled false alarm rate. Extensive experiments across diverse models and datasets validate the robustness of our approach against state-of-the-art methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过多重检验原理检测大语言模型中的幻觉
尽管大语言模型（LLMs）已成为解决多种任务的强大基础模型，但它们也易产生幻觉，即生成听起来自信但实际错误甚至无意义的响应。现有幻觉检测器提出了多种经验评分规则，但其性能在不同模型和数据集上差异显著，实践中难以确定应依赖哪些规则或将其视为可靠检测器。本文将幻觉检测问题形式化为假设检验问题，并与机器学习模型的分布外检测问题类比。随后，我们提出一种受多重检验启发的方法，通过一致性p值系统聚合多个评估分数，实现校准检测并控制虚警率。在多种模型和数据集上的广泛实验验证了该方法相较于现有最先进方法的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable hallucination detection in large language models, this work frames the problem as a hypothesis testing task, drawing parallels with out-of-distribution detection. It introduces a multiple-testing-inspired method that aggregates diverse empirical scoring rules using conformal p-values to produce calibrated, statistically controlled detection. Experiments across multiple models and datasets demonstrate that this approach consistently outperforms existing methods in maintaining low false alarm rates while achieving high detection accuracy.
为解决大语言模型中幻觉检测缺乏统一可靠方法的问题，本文将幻觉检测建模为假设检验问题，借鉴机器学习中的分布外检测思路，提出一种基于多重检验的聚合方法，通过使用符合p值整合多个评估分数，实现可控误报率下的校准检测。在多个模型和数据集上的实验表明，该方法在鲁棒性和可靠性方面显著优于现有方法。
At FullTilt: Real-Time Open-Set 3D Macromolecule Detection Directly from Tilted 2D Projections
Authors: Ming-Yang Ho, Alberto Bartesaghi
First: 2026-04-12T18:28:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:16:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open-set 3D macromolecule detection in cryogenic electron tomography eliminates the need for target-specific model retraining. However, strict VRAM constraints prohibit processing an entire 3D tomogram, forcing current methods to rely on slow sliding-window inference over extracted subvolumes. To overcome this, we propose FullTilt, an end-to-end framework that redefines 3D detection by operating directly on aligned 2D tilt-series. Because a tilt-series contains significantly fewer images than slices in a reconstructed tomogram, FullTilt eliminates redundant volumetric computation, accelerating inference by orders of magnitude. To process the entire tilt-series simultaneously, we introduce a tilt-series encoder to efficiently fuse cross-view information. We further propose a multiclass visual prompt encoder for flexible prompting, a tilt-aware query initializer to effectively anchor 3D queries, and an auxiliary geometric primitives module to enhance the model's understanding of multi-view geometry while improving robustness to adverse imaging artifacts. Extensive evaluations on three real-world datasets demonstrate that FullTilt achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance while drastically reducing runtime and VRAM requirements, paving the way for rapid, large-scale visual proteomics analysis. All code and data will be publicly available upon publication.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FullTilt：从倾斜二维投影直接进行实时开放集三维大分子检测
冷冻电子断层扫描中的开放集三维大分子检测无需针对目标进行模型再训练。然而，严格的VRAM限制禁止处理完整的三维断层图，迫使当前方法依赖于从提取的子体积上缓慢的滑动窗口推理。为解决此问题，我们提出FullTilt，一个端到端框架，通过直接在对齐的二维倾斜系列上操作，重新定义三维检测。由于倾斜系列包含的图像数量远少于重建断层图中的切片数量，FullTilt消除了冗余的体计算，将推理速度提升数个数量级。为同时处理整个倾斜系列，我们引入倾斜系列编码器以高效融合跨视图信息。我们进一步提出多类视觉提示编码器以实现灵活提示，倾斜感知查询初始化器以有效锚定三维查询，以及辅助几何原语模块以增强模型对多视角几何的理解并提高对不利成像伪影的鲁棒性。在三个真实数据集上的广泛评估表明，FullTilt在零样本性能上达到最先进水平，同时大幅降低运行时间和VRAM需求，为快速、大规模视觉蛋白质组学分析铺平道路。所有代码和数据将在发表后公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect 3D macromolecules in cryo-electron tomography without retraining for each target and under strict VRAM limits, FullTilt introduces an end-to-end framework that processes aligned 2D tilt-series directly, bypassing volumetric reconstruction. It employs a tilt-series encoder to fuse cross-view information, a multiclass visual prompt encoder for flexible prompting, a tilt-aware query initializer to anchor 3D queries, and a geometric primitives module to enhance multi-view understanding and robustness. Experimental results on three real-world datasets show FullTilt achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot detection accuracy while reducing runtime and VRAM usage by orders of magnitude, enabling scalable visual proteomics analysis.
为在VRAM受限条件下实现无需目标重训练的3D大分子检测，FullTilt提出直接处理对齐2D倾转系列的端到端框架，避免冗余体素计算。该方法引入倾转系列编码器融合跨视图信息，多类别视觉提示编码器支持灵活提示，倾转感知查询初始化器锚定3D查询，几何原语模块增强多视角几何理解与抗成像伪影能力。在三个真实数据集上的实验表明，FullTilt在零样本检测精度上达到当前最优，同时将推理时间和VRAM需求降低数个数量级，推动大规模视觉蛋白质组学分析的实现。
MAIC-UI: Making Interactive Courseware with Generative UI
Authors: Shangqing Tu, Yanjia Li, Keyu Chen, Sichen Zhang, Jifan Yu, Daniel Zhang-Li, Lei Hou, Juanzi Li, Yu Zhang, Huiqin Liu
First: 2026-04-28T16:15:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:15:04+00:00
Comments: You can try our demo at https://open.maic.chat/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Creating interactive STEM courseware traditionally requires HTML/CSS/JavaScript expertise, leaving barriers for educators. While generative AI can produce HTML codes, existing tools generate static presentations rather than interactive simulations, struggle with long documents, and lack pedagogical accuracy mechanisms. Furthermore, full regeneration for modifications requires 200--600 seconds, disrupting creative flow. We present MAIC-UI, a zero-code authoring system that enables educators to create and rapidly edit interactive courseware from textbooks, PPTs, and PDFs. MAIC-UI employs: (1) structured knowledge analysis with multi-modal understanding to ensure pedagogical rigor; (2) a two-stage generate-verify-optimize pipeline separating content alignment from visual refinement; and (3) Click-to-Locate editing with Unified Diff-based incremental generation achieving sub-10-second iteration cycles. A controlled lab study with 40 participants shows MAIC-UI reduces editing iterations (4.9 vs. 7.0) and significantly improves learnability and controllability compared to direct Text-to-HTML generation. A three-month classroom deployment with 53 high school students demonstrates that MAIC-UI fosters learning agency and reduces outcome disparities -- the pilot class achieved 9.21-point gains in STEM subjects compared to -2.32 points in control classes. Our code is available at https://github.com/THU-MAIC/MAIC-UI.

A Demonstration of SQLyzr: A Platform for Fine-Grained Text-to-SQL Evaluation and Analysis
Authors: Sepideh Abedini, M. Tamer Özsu
First: 2026-04-23T02:12:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:14:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Text-to-SQL models have significantly improved with the adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs), leading to their increasing use in real-world applications. Although many benchmarks exist for evaluating the performance of text-to-SQL models, they often rely on a single aggregate score, lack evaluation under realistic settings, and provide limited insight into model behaviour across different query types. In this work, we present SQLyzr, a comprehensive benchmark and evaluation platform for text-to-SQL models. SQLyzr incorporates a diverse set of evaluation metrics that capture multiple aspects of generated queries, while enabling more realistic evaluation through workload alignment with real-world SQL usage patterns and database scaling. It further supports fine-grained query classification, error analysis, and workload augmentation, allowing users to better diagnose and improve text-to-SQL models. This demonstration showcases these capabilities through an interactive experience. Through SQLyzr's graphical interface, users can customize evaluation settings, analyze fine-grained reports, and explore additional features of the platform. We envision that SQLyzr facilitates the evaluation and iterative improvement of text-to-SQL models by addressing key limitations of existing benchmarks. The source code of SQLyzr is available at https://github.com/sepideh-abedini/SQLyzr.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SQLyzr平台：细粒度文本到SQL评估与分析的演示
随着大语言模型（LLMs）的采用，文本到SQL模型性能显著提升，其在实际应用中的使用日益广泛。尽管已有多种评估基准用于衡量文本到SQL模型的表现，但它们往往依赖单一聚合分数，缺乏在真实场景下的评估，且对不同查询类型下模型行为的洞察有限。本文中，我们提出了SQLyzr，一个全面的文本到SQL模型评估基准与平台。SQLyzr整合了多样化的评估指标，以捕捉生成查询的多个方面，同时通过与真实SQL使用模式对齐的工作负载和数据库扩展，实现更真实的评估。它还支持细粒度查询分类、错误分析和工作负载增强，帮助用户更好地诊断和改进文本到SQL模型。本次演示通过交互式体验展示了这些功能。用户可通过SQLyzr的图形界面自定义评估设置、分析细粒度报告并探索平台其他功能。我们期望SQLyzr通过解决现有基准的关键局限，促进文本到SQL模型的评估与迭代改进。SQLyzr源代码可在https://github.com/sepideh-abedini/SQLyzr获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more nuanced and realistic evaluation of text-to-SQL models, this work introduces SQLyzr, a platform that enables fine-grained analysis beyond aggregate scores. It employs a diverse set of metrics and aligns evaluation workloads with real-world SQL usage patterns while supporting database scaling. Experimental results demonstrate SQLyzr’s ability to classify queries, identify model errors, and augment workloads, offering users detailed diagnostic insights to improve model performance.
为解决现有文本到SQL模型评估缺乏细粒度和真实场景的问题，本研究提出SQLyzr平台，通过多样化的评估指标、与真实SQL工作负载对齐及数据库扩展能力，支持查询分类、错误分析与工作负载增强。实验表明，该平台能揭示模型在不同查询类型中的具体缺陷，提供超越单一分数的可操作改进依据。
Domain-Filtered Knowledge Graphs from Sparse Autoencoder Features
Authors: John Winnicki, Abeynaya Gnanasekaran, Eric Darve
First: 2026-04-26T18:17:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:11:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) extract millions of interpretable features from a language model, but flat feature inventories aren't very useful on their own. Domain concepts get mixed with generic and weakly grounded features, while related ideas are scattered across many units, and there's no way to understand relationships between features. We address this by first constructing a strict domain-specific concept universe from a large SAE inventory using contrastive activations and a multi-stage filtering process. Next, we build two aligned graph views on the filtered set: a co-occurrence graph for corpus-level conceptual structure, organized at multiple levels of granularity, and a transcoder-based mechanism graph that links source-layer and target-layer features through sparse latent pathways. Automated edge labeling then turns these graph views into readable knowledge graphs rather than unlabeled layouts. In a case study on a biology textbook, these graphs recover coherent chapter and subchapter-level structure, reveal concepts that bridge neighboring topics, and transform messy sentence-level activity containing thousands of features into compact, readable views that illustrate the model's local activity. Taken together, this reframes a flat SAE inventory as an internal knowledge graph that converts feature-level interpretability into a global map of model knowledge and enables audits of reasoning faithfulness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从稀疏自编码器特征中构建领域过滤知识图谱
稀疏自编码器（SAEs）可从语言模型中提取数百万个可解释特征，但扁平的特征库存本身价值有限。领域概念常与通用或弱关联特征混杂，相关概念分散于多个单元，且缺乏特征间关系的理解。我们首先通过对比激活与多阶段过滤过程，从大型SAE库存中构建严格领域特定的概念宇宙。接着，在过滤后的特征集上构建两个对齐的图视图：一个用于语料层面概念结构的共现图，按多粒度层级组织；另一个基于转码器的机制图，通过稀疏潜在路径连接源层与目标层特征。自动边标签化将这些图视图转化为可读知识图谱，而非无标签布局。在生物学教科书案例研究中，这些图谱恢复了章节与子章节的连贯结构，揭示连接相邻主题的概念，并将包含数千特征的混乱句级活动转化为简洁可读的视图，展示模型局部活动。整体而言，这将扁平SAE库存重构为内部知识图谱，将特征级可解释性转化为模型知识的全局地图，并支持推理忠实度审计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of structured interpretability in sparse autoencoder feature inventories, this work constructs domain-specific knowledge graphs by filtering SAE features using contrastive activations and multi-stage selection. It then builds two graph views—a co-occurrence graph capturing conceptual hierarchies at multiple granularities and a transcoder-based mechanism graph linking feature layers via sparse pathways—and labels edges automatically to produce readable graphs. Experiments on a biology textbook show the graphs recover coherent chapter-level structure, reveal cross-topic conceptual bridges, and distill complex sentence-level feature activity into compact, interpretable representations, effectively transforming flat SAE features into a global knowledge map for auditing model reasoning.
为解决稀疏自编码器特征缺乏结构化可解释性的问题，该研究通过对比激活与多阶段筛选，从大规模SAE特征中构建领域特定概念宇宙，并生成两类图结构：一类是捕捉语料层面概念层次的共现图，另一类是通过稀疏潜在路径连接源层与目标层特征的转码机制图。自动边标签化后，这些图转化为可读知识图谱。在生物学教材案例中，图谱成功恢复章节级结构、揭示跨主题概念桥梁，并将数千个特征的句子级活动压缩为简洁可视化，将扁平特征库存重构为模型知识全局地图。
Barriers to Universal Reasoning With Transformers (And How to Overcome Them)
Authors: Oliver Kraus, Yash Sarrof, Yuekun Yao, Alexander Koller, Michael Hahn
First: 2026-04-28T16:10:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:10:37+00:00
Comments: Oliver Kraus and Yash Sarrof contributed equally as first authors. Alexander Koller and Michael Hahn are co-senior authors. Code: https://github.com/coli-saar/BarriersToUniversalReasoningWTransformers
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Chain-of-Thought (CoT) has been shown to empirically improve Transformers' performance, and theoretically increase their expressivity to Turing completeness. However, whether Transformers can learn to generalize to CoT traces longer than those seen during training is understudied. We use recent theoretical frameworks for Transformer length generalization and find that -- under standard positional encodings and a finite alphabet -- Transformers with CoT cannot solve problems beyond $TC^0$, i.e. the expressivity benefits do not hold under the stricter requirement of length-generalizable learnability. However, if we allow the vocabulary to grow with problem size, we attain a length-generalizable simulation of Turing machines where the CoT trace length is linear in the simulated runtime up to a constant. Our construction overcomes two core obstacles to reliable length generalization: repeated copying and last-occurrence retrieval. We assign each tape position a unique signpost token, and log only value changes to enable recovery of the current tape symbol through counts circumventing both barriers. Further, we empirically show that the use of such signpost tokens and value change encodings provide actionable guidance to improve length generalization on hard problems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Transformer实现通用推理的障碍及其克服方法
链式思维（CoT）已被证明能实证提升Transformer性能，并在理论上增强其表达能力至图灵完备。然而，Transformer能否学习到泛化至训练中未见的更长CoT轨迹仍缺乏研究。我们利用近期Transformer长度泛化理论框架发现——在标准位置编码和有限字母表下，具备CoT的Transformer无法解决超越TC⁰的问题，即表达能力优势在严格长度可泛化学习要求下不成立。但若允许词汇表随问题规模增长，我们可实现长度可泛化的图灵机模拟，其中CoT轨迹长度与模拟运行时间呈线性关系（常数倍）。该构造克服了可靠长度泛化两大核心障碍：重复复制与最后出现项检索。我们为每条磁带位置分配唯一路标标记，并仅记录值变化，从而通过计数恢复当前磁带符号，规避上述障碍。此外，我们实证表明，使用此类路标标记与值变化编码可为提升硬问题上的长度泛化提供可操作指导。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether Transformers can generalize to longer Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces beyond those seen during training, motivated by the theoretical promise of CoT to achieve Turing completeness. Using recent theoretical frameworks, the authors find that under standard positional encodings and a fixed vocabulary, Transformers with CoT cannot exceed TC⁰ expressivity, meaning they fail to generalize reliably in length. However, by allowing the vocabulary to scale with problem size and introducing 'signpost tokens' that track tape positions and only encode value changes, the model can simulate Turing machines with linear CoT trace length relative to runtime. This construction overcomes two key barriers—repeated copying and last-occurrence retrieval—by enabling state recovery through token counts. Empirically, this approach improves length generalization on hard problems.
该研究探讨了Transformer是否能泛化到训练时未见过的更长Chain-of-Thought（CoT）轨迹，发现标准位置编码和固定词汇下，CoT Transformer无法超越TC^0表达能力，限制其通用推理能力。作者提出通过随问题规模扩展词汇量，实现线性长度的CoT轨迹以模拟图灵机。其方法为每个磁带位置分配唯一‘路标’标记，仅记录值变化，从而绕过重复复制与最后出现检索两大泛化障碍。实验证明，该方法通过结构化状态恢复指导，显著提升对难题的长度泛化能力。
At the Edge of the Heart: ULP FPGA-Based CNN for On-Device Cardiac Feature Extraction in Smart Health Sensors for Astronauts
Authors: Kazi Mohammad Abidur Rahman, Davis Rakhshan, Philipp Lütke, Laura Harms, Ulf Kulau
First: 2026-04-28T16:10:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:10:16+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 7 figures, To be published in: The 22nd Annual International Conference on Distributed Computing in Smart Systems and the Internet of Things (DCOSS-IoT 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The convergence of accelerating human spaceflight ambitions and critical terrestrial health monitoring demands is driving unprecedented requirements for reliable, real-time feature extraction on extremely resource-constrained wearable health sensors. We present an ultra-low-power (ULP) Field-Programmable Gate Array (FPGA) based solution for real-time Seismocardiography (SCG) feature classification using Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs). Our approach combines quantization-aware training with a systolic-array accelerator to enable efficient integer-only inference on the Lattice iCE40UP5K FPGA, which offers an ideal platform for battery-powered deployments -- particularly in space environments -- thanks to its power efficiency and radiation resilience. The implementation achieves a validation accuracy of 98% while consuming only 8.55 mW, completing inference in 95.5 ms with minimal hardware resources (2,861 LUTs and 7 DSP blocks). These results demonstrate that fully on-device SCG-based cardiac feature extraction is feasible on resource-constrained hardware, enabling energy-efficient, autonomous health monitoring for astronauts in long-duration space missions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：心缘边缘：面向宇航员智能健康传感器的低功耗FPGA基CNN心脏特征提取
人类太空飞行雄心与地球关键健康监测需求的融合，正推动对极资源受限可穿戴健康传感器上可靠实时特征提取的前所未有的要求。我们提出一种基于超低功耗（ULP）现场可编程门阵列（FPGA）的解决方案，用于实时心音图（SCG）特征分类，采用卷积神经网络（CNN）。该方法结合量化感知训练与脉动阵列加速器，可在Lattice iCE40UP5K FPGA上实现仅整数运算的高效推理，该FPGA因功耗低、抗辐射能力强，特别适合电池供电部署——尤其适用于太空环境。该实现验证准确率达98%，仅耗电8.55 mW，推理耗时95.5 ms，硬件资源占用极少（2,861个LUT和7个DSP模块）。这些结果表明，在资源受限硬件上实现完全本地化SCG心脏特征提取是可行的，可为长期太空任务中的宇航员提供节能、自主的健康监测。

Driven by the need for real-time, low-power cardiac monitoring in space missions and terrestrial wearable applications, this work proposes an ultra-low-power FPGA-based CNN system for on-device Seismocardiography feature classification. The method integrates quantization-aware training with a systolic-array accelerator to enable efficient integer-only inference on the Lattice iCE40UP5K FPGA, optimized for power efficiency and radiation resilience. Experimental results show the system achieves 98% validation accuracy while consuming just 8.55 mW and completing inference in 95.5 ms using minimal hardware resources (2,861 LUTs and 7 DSP blocks), proving that high-performance cardiac feature extraction is viable on resource-constrained edge devices.
为满足太空任务和可穿戴设备中实时低功耗心脏监测的需求，本文提出一种基于FPGA的超低功耗CNN系统，用于设备端心音图特征分类。该方法结合量化感知训练与脉动阵列加速器，实现仅用整数运算的高效推理，部署于Lattice iCE40UP5K FPGA上，该芯片具备低功耗与抗辐射特性。实验结果显示，在仅消耗8.55mW功率、95.5毫秒推理时间内，使用2861个LUT和7个DSP块，准确率达98%，证明在资源受限硬件上实现高性能心脏特征提取是可行的。
Agent-Diff: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Enterprise API Tasks via Code Execution with State-Diff-Based Evaluation
Authors: Hubert M. Pysklo, Artem Zhuravel, Patrick D. Watson
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2026-02-11T13:31:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:08:25+00:00
Comments: Pre-Print. Under review for KDD 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present Agent-Diff, a novel benchmarking framework for evaluating agentic Large Language Models (LLMs) on real-world productivity software API tasks via code execution. Agentic LLM performance varies due to differences in models, external tool access, prompt structures, and agentic frameworks. Benchmarks must make fundamental trade-offs between a sandboxed approach that controls for variation in software environments and more ecologically valid approaches employing real services. Agent-Diff attempts to capture the desirable features of both of these approaches by including access to the real API interfaces for software services while sandboxing the environment in which calls are made, processed, and evaluated. This approach relies on two key innovations. The first is a novel state-diff contract, which separates process from outcome - rather than fuzzy trace or parameter matching, we define task success as whether the expected change in environment state was achieved. The second is a novel sandbox built on containerized replicas of enterprise APIs, allowing all models to interact with the same service interfaces through code execution. This enables controlled evaluation against a common set of state-diff contracts while preserving the structure of real-world API interaction. Using the Agent-Diff framework, we provide benchmarks for nine LLMs across 224 tasks utilizing enterprise software workflows. In addition, we evaluate the robustness of the framework with ablation experiments to assess the contribution of access to API documentation on benchmark performance. Code and data: https://github.com/agent-diff-bench/agent-diff.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Agent-Diff：通过基于状态差异的评估与代码执行，对大型语言模型代理在企业API任务上的表现进行基准测试
我们提出了Agent-Diff，一个新颖的基准框架，用于通过代码执行评估大型语言模型代理在现实世界生产力软件API任务中的表现。代理型LLM的表现因模型差异、外部工具访问、提示结构和代理框架的不同而异。基准测试必须在沙箱方法（控制软件环境差异）与更生态效度的方法（使用真实服务）之间做出根本性权衡。Agent-Diff通过提供对真实软件服务API接口的访问，同时在调用、处理和评估环境中进行沙箱化，试图兼顾这两种方法的优点。该方法依赖两项关键创新：一是新颖的状态差异契约，将过程与结果分离——而非模糊的追踪或参数匹配，我们定义任务成功为预期环境状态变化是否达成；二是基于容器化企业API副本构建的新型沙箱，使所有模型通过代码执行与相同服务接口交互。这使得在统一的状态差异契约下进行可控评估，同时保留真实世界API交互的结构。使用Agent-Diff框架，我们为九个LLM在224项企业软件工作流任务上提供了基准测试。此外，我们通过消融实验评估了框架的鲁棒性，以考察访问API文档对基准性能的贡献。代码与数据：https://github.com/agent-diff-bench/agent-diff。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to fairly evaluate agentic LLMs on real enterprise API tasks, Agent-Diff introduces a benchmarking framework that combines real API access with sandboxed execution to control environmental variation. It employs a state-diff contract to define task success by measuring actual environmental state changes rather than trace or parameter matching, and uses containerized API replicas to ensure consistent, controlled interaction across models. Experimental results across 224 tasks with nine LLMs show significant performance variation based on model and framework, and ablation studies confirm that API documentation access improves benchmark performance.
为公平评估代理型大语言模型在真实企业API任务中的表现，Agent-Diff提出一种结合真实API访问与沙箱执行的基准框架，以控制环境变量。其采用状态差异合约，通过检测环境实际状态变化定义任务成功，而非依赖轨迹或参数匹配，并使用容器化API副本确保各模型在一致环境中交互。实验结果表明九个模型在224项任务中表现差异显著，且消融实验显示API文档访问可提升基准性能。
Towards Real-World Validity in Generative AI Benchmarks: Understanding and Designing Domain-Centered Evaluations for Journalism Practitioners
Authors: Charlotte Li, Nick Hagar, Sachita Nishal, Jeremy Gilbert, Nick Diakopoulos
First: 2025-09-30T21:36:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:06:15+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Benchmarks play a significant role in how technology companies communicate about model capabilities and how researchers and the public understand generative AI systems. However, existing benchmarks have been criticized for their failure to adequately capture real-world usages (i.e. ecological validity) or to measure underlying concepts (i.e. construct validity). Building on approaches in HCI, we adopt a human-centered design process to address such critiques. Working within the journalism domain we engaged 23 professionals in a workshop which informed the design of a domain-oriented evaluation ``cookbook''. Our workshop findings surface domain-specific challenges and tensions faced by designers in translating specific tasks to evaluation constructs, aligning metrics with domain-specific values, and balancing needs among different stakeholders when constructing evaluations. Through an instantiation of design-based approaches for benchmark creation in the journalism domain, this work not only produces an evaluation structure for journalism practitioners to experiment with, but also lays out design requirements for AI evaluations that are contextualized, value-aligned, and cultivate evaluative literacy for domain end-users.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向生成式人工智能基准的现实有效性：为新闻从业者设计领域中心评估方法
基准在科技公司传达模型能力、研究人员和公众理解生成式人工智能系统方面起着重要作用。然而，现有基准常被批评未能充分捕捉现实应用场景（即生态效度）或准确衡量底层概念（即构念效度）。借鉴人机交互领域方法，我们采用以人为核心的设计流程应对这些批评。在新闻领域，我们组织23位专业人士参与工作坊，据此设计了一套领域导向的评估“食谱”。工作坊成果揭示了设计者在将具体任务转化为评估构念、使指标与领域特定价值对齐、以及在构建评估时平衡不同利益相关者需求时所面临的领域特异性挑战与张力。通过在新闻领域应用基于设计的方法创建基准，本研究不仅为新闻从业者提供了可实验的评估结构，也提出了情境化、价值对齐、并培养领域终端用户评估素养的AI评估设计要求。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of ecological and construct validity in existing generative AI benchmarks, this study adopts a human-centered design approach rooted in HCI to create domain-specific evaluations for journalism. Through workshops with 23 journalism professionals, the researchers identified key challenges in translating real-world tasks into measurable constructs, aligning metrics with journalistic values, and balancing stakeholder needs. The resulting 'cookbook' offers a structured yet flexible framework for evaluating AI systems in journalism, emphasizing contextual relevance, value alignment, and user literacy, thereby advancing the design of more meaningful and practical AI benchmarks.
鉴于现有生成式AI基准缺乏生态效度和构念效度，本研究借鉴人机交互领域方法，采用以人类为中心的设计思路，为新闻从业者定制领域化评估方案。通过与23位新闻专业人士的工作坊，研究者识别出将真实新闻任务转化为可测量评估指标、对齐领域价值观及平衡多方需求等关键挑战。最终形成的“评估手册”提供了一套结构化且灵活的框架，支持构建情境化、价值观对齐的基准，同时促进终端用户评估素养的提升，证明领域导向设计可为新闻领域的生成式AI评估提供更具意义和可操作性的解决方案。
CodeOCR: On the Effectiveness of Vision Language Models in Code Understanding
Authors: Yuling Shi, Chaoxiang Xie, Zhensu Sun, Yeheng Chen, Chenxu Zhang, Longfei Yun, Chengcheng Wan, Hongyu Zhang, David Lo, Xiaodong Gu
First: 2026-02-02T08:10:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:05:53+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ISSTA 2026. Code and data are available at https://github.com/YerbaPage/CodeOCR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable success in source code understanding, yet as software systems grow in scale, computational efficiency has become a critical bottleneck. Currently, these models rely on a text-based paradigm that treats source code as a linear sequence of tokens, which leads to a linear increase in context length and associated computational costs. The rapid advancement of Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) introduces an opportunity to optimize efficiency by representing source code as rendered images. Unlike text, which is difficult to compress without losing semantic meaning, the image modality is inherently suitable for compression. By adjusting resolution, images can be scaled to a fraction of their original token cost while remaining recognizable to vision-capable models. To explore the feasibility of this approach, we conduct the first systematic study on the effectiveness of MLLMs for code understanding. Our experiments reveal that: (1) MLLMs can effectively understand code with substantial token reduction, achieving up to 8x compression; (2) MLLMs can effectively leverage visual cues such as syntax highlighting, improving code completion performance under 4x compression; and (3) Code-understanding tasks like clone detection exhibit exceptional resilience to visual compression, with some compression ratios even slightly outperforming raw text inputs. Our findings highlight both the potential and current limitations of MLLMs in code understanding, which points out a shift toward image-modality code representation as a pathway to more efficient inference.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CodeOCR：视觉语言模型在代码理解中的有效性研究
大型语言模型（LLMs）在源代码理解方面取得了显著成功，但随着软件系统规模扩大，计算效率已成为关键瓶颈。当前这些模型依赖基于文本的范式，将源代码视为线性令牌序列，导致上下文长度和计算成本线性增长。多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的快速发展为优化效率提供了机会，即通过将源代码表示为渲染图像。与难以压缩而不丢失语义的文本不同，图像模态天然适合压缩。通过调整分辨率，图像可缩减至原始令牌成本的几分之一，同时仍能被视觉模型识别。为探索该方法的可行性，我们首次系统研究了MLLMs在代码理解中的有效性。实验表明：（1）MLLMs可实现高达8倍的令牌压缩，同时有效理解代码；（2）MLLMs能有效利用语法高亮等视觉线索，在4倍压缩下提升代码补全性能；（3）代码理解任务如克隆检测对视觉压缩具有极强鲁棒性，部分压缩比甚至优于原始文本输入。我们的研究揭示了MLLMs在代码理解中的潜力与当前局限，指明了图像模态代码表示作为更高效推理路径的方向。

To address this, the authors investigate whether Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) can understand code when represented as rendered images, leveraging the inherent compressibility of visual data. Their experiments show that MLLMs can achieve up to 8x token compression while maintaining code understanding, and even outperform text inputs in tasks like clone detection under heavy compression. Additionally, visual cues such as syntax highlighting improve code completion performance under 4x compression, demonstrating that image-based representation not only reduces cost but also enhances certain aspects of code comprehension.
为解决文本型大语言模型处理大规模代码时计算效率低的问题，该研究探索多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）是否能通过将代码视为渲染图像来更高效地理解代码，利用图像固有的压缩特性。方法将源代码转换为可调整分辨率的图像以降低token成本，同时保留语义信息，并在代码理解任务中评估MLLMs性能。实验结果表明，MLLMs可实现最高8倍的token压缩而不损失理解能力，在4倍压缩下借助语法高亮等视觉线索提升代码补全效果，且在克隆检测任务中，高压缩比甚至优于原始文本输入，揭示了视觉代码表示的潜力与当前局限。
Intellectual Stewardship: Re-adapting Human Minds for Creative Knowledge Work in the Age of AI
Authors: Jianwei Zhang
First: 2026-03-18T15:15:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:04:09+00:00
Comments: 21 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Background: Amid the opportunities and risks introduced by generative AI, learning research needs to envision how human minds and responsibilities should re-adapt as AI augments or automates various tasks and enters daily learning, knowledge work, and social life. Approach: Drawing on theories of learning, intelligence, and knowledge creation, this conceptual paper proposes intellectual stewardship as a human-centered, conceptually grounded framework for advancing creative learning practices with AI. Key points: Students and teachers work as responsible governors of intellectual processes distributed across human and artificial systems, guided by five core principles. Being knowledge-wise involves understanding the evolving state of knowledge and taking purposeful actions to advance it. Being intelligence-wise emphasizes making informed choices about how to orchestrate distributed cognitive processes and resources. Being context-wise requires sensitivity to recognize opportunities and risks. Being ethics-wise foregrounds ethical judgment, responsibility, and care in the use of knowledge and intellectual power. Finally, self- and community-growing defines the overarching purpose, aligning intellectual work with personal development and the advancement of collective well-being. Contribution: The principles provide a lens for viewing the adaptation of human minds in AI-infused learning environments, calling for the development of meta-level dispositions and capabilities that characterize wisdom-oriented, socially responsible knowledge builders in the AI age.
中文标题/摘要
标题：智力 stewardship：在人工智能时代重新适应人类思维以进行创造性知识工作
背景：在生成式人工智能带来的机遇与风险中，学习研究需要设想人类思维与责任应如何重新适应，当AI增强或自动化各种任务并融入日常学习、知识工作与社会生活。 方法：基于学习、智能与知识创造的理论，本文提出“智力 stewardship”这一以人为中心、概念基础扎实的框架，旨在推动与AI结合的创造性学习实践。 要点：学生与教师作为跨越人类与人工系统的智力过程的负责任管理者，遵循五大核心原则。知识明智指理解知识的演变状态并采取有目的行动推动其发展。智能明智强调明智选择如何协调分布的认知过程与资源。情境明智要求敏感识别机会与风险。伦理明智将伦理判断、责任与关怀置于知识与智力权力使用的核心。自我与社群成长则定义了整体目标，将智力工作与个人发展及集体福祉的提升相协调。 贡献：这些原则为观察人工智能融入学习环境中的思维适应提供了视角，呼吁培养智慧导向、社会责任感的知识构建者所需的元层面素养与能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to redefine human roles amid AI’s growing influence on learning and knowledge work, this paper proposes 'intellectual stewardship' as a framework guiding humans to responsibly govern cognitive processes across human-AI systems. It outlines five core principles—knowledge-wise, intelligence-wise, context-wise, ethics-wise, and self- and community-growing—to help individuals navigate evolving knowledge landscapes, make informed cognitive decisions, recognize contextual risks, uphold ethical standards, and align intellectual efforts with personal and collective growth. While not empirically tested, the framework is presented as a conceptual tool to foster wisdom-oriented, socially responsible knowledge practices in AI-integrated environments.
鉴于生成式AI对学习与知识工作带来的机遇与挑战，本文提出“智力 stewardship”这一以人为中心的概念框架，旨在引导人类在AI环境中负责任地参与知识创造。该框架包含五个核心原则——知识明智、智力明智、情境明智、伦理明智及自我与社群成长——以帮助个体应对人机协同的认知系统。尽管未进行实证实验，该模型旨在培养面向AI时代的智慧型、负责任的知识构建者所需的元认知能力，将人类定位为AI工具的管理者而非被动使用者。
StratFormer: Adaptive Opponent Modeling and Exploitation in Imperfect-Information Games
Authors: Andy Caen, Mark H. M. Winands, Dennis J. N. J. Soemers
First: 2026-04-28T16:03:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:03:14+00:00
Comments: Accepted at Computers and Games 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present StratFormer, a transformer-based meta-agent that learns to simultaneously model and exploit opponents in imperfect-information games through a two-phase curriculum. The first phase trains an opponent modeling head to identify behavioral patterns from action histories while the agent plays a game-theoretic optimal (GTO) policy. The second phase progressively shifts the policy toward best-response (BR) exploitation, guided by a per-opponent regularization schedule tied to exploitability. Our architecture introduces dual-turn tokens -- feature vectors constructed at both agent and opponent decision points -- coupled with bucket-rate features that encode opponent tendencies across five strategic contexts. On Leduc Hold'em, a small poker variant with six cards and two betting rounds, we test against six opponent archetypes at two strength levels each, with exploitability ranging from 0.15 to 1.26 Big Blinds (BB) per hand. StratFormer achieves an average exploitation gain of +0.106 BB per hand over GTO, with peak gains of +0.821 against highly exploitable opponents, while maintaining near-equilibrium safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：StratFormer：不完全信息博弈中的自适应对手建模与利用
我们提出了StratFormer，一种基于Transformer的元智能体，通过两阶段课程学习，在不完全信息博弈中同时建模并利用对手。第一阶段训练对手建模模块，从行动历史中识别行为模式，同时智能体采用博弈论最优（GTO）策略；第二阶段逐步将策略转向最佳反应（BR）利用，由与可利用性相关的每对手正则化调度引导。该架构引入双回合标记——在智能体和对手决策点构建的特征向量——并结合桶率特征，编码对手在五种战略情境中的倾向性。在Leduc扑克（六张牌、两轮下注）上，我们测试了六种对手类型，每种强度水平两个，可利用性范围为每手0.15至1.26大盲注（BB）。StratFormer平均每手比GTO多赢+0.106 BB，对高度可利用对手最高可达+0.821 BB，同时保持接近均衡的安全性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of adapting to diverse opponents in imperfect-information games, StratFormer employs a two-phase curriculum: first learning to model opponents via game-theoretic optimal play, then shifting toward best-response exploitation guided by per-opponent regularization. It uses dual-turn tokens and bucket-rate features to capture opponent behavior across strategic contexts. On Leduc Hold'em, it outperforms game-theoretic optimal play by an average of +0.106 BB per hand, peaking at +0.821 BB against highly exploitable opponents, while preserving near-equilibrium safety.
为在信息不完全博弈中自适应建模并利用对手，StratFormer采用双阶段课程：先通过博弈论最优策略学习对手行为，再通过针对每个对手的正则化方案逐步转向最佳反应利用。模型使用双回合标记和桶率特征捕捉不同情境下的对手策略倾向。在Leduc Hold'em上，其平均每手收益比博弈论最优策略高出+0.106 BB，对高可利用对手最高可达+0.821 BB，同时保持接近均衡的安全性。
Improving Diversity in Black-box Few-shot Knowledge Distillation
Authors: Tri-Nhan Vo, Dang Nguyen, Kien Do, Sunil Gupta
Venue: European Conference on Machine Learning and Principles and Practice of Knowledge Discovery in Databases 2024
First: 2026-04-28T16:03:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:03:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Knowledge distillation (KD) is a well-known technique to effectively compress a large network (teacher) to a smaller network (student) with little sacrifice in performance. However, most KD methods require a large training set and internal access to the teacher, which are rarely available due to various restrictions. These challenges have originated a more practical setting known as black-box few-shot KD, where the student is trained with few images and a black-box teacher. Recent approaches typically generate additional synthetic images but lack an active strategy to promote their diversity, a crucial factor for student learning. To address these problems, we propose a novel training scheme for generative adversarial networks, where we adaptively select high-confidence images under the teacher's supervision and introduce them to the adversarial learning on-the-fly. Our approach helps expand and improve the diversity of the distillation set, significantly boosting student accuracy. Through extensive experiments, we achieve state-of-the-art results among other few-shot KD methods on seven image datasets. The code is available at https://github.com/votrinhan88/divbfkd.
中文标题/摘要
标题：提升黑盒少样本知识蒸馏中的多样性
知识蒸馏（KD）是一种有效压缩大型网络（教师）至小型网络（学生）且性能损失较小的成熟技术。然而，多数KD方法需要大量训练数据和对教师网络的内部访问，这在实际中因各种限制难以满足。这些挑战催生了更实用的黑盒少样本KD设置，即学生仅用少量图像并在黑盒教师指导下训练。近期方法通常生成额外合成图像，但缺乏主动策略提升其多样性——这对学生学习至关重要。为解决此问题，我们提出一种生成对抗网络的新型训练方案：在教师监督下自适应选择高置信度图像，并实时引入对抗学习。该方法显著扩展并提升蒸馏集的多样性，大幅提高学生精度。通过大量实验，我们在七个图像数据集上超越现有少样本KD方法，达到最先进水平。代码见 https://github.com/votrinhan88/divbfkd。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing black-box few-shot knowledge distillation methods that lack diversity in synthetic training data, this work proposes an adaptive generative adversarial training scheme to enhance data diversity. The method selects high-confidence teacher-supervised images dynamically and integrates them into adversarial learning on-the-fly, thereby expanding and refining the distillation set. Experimental results across seven image datasets demonstrate that this approach significantly improves student accuracy, achieving state-of-the-art performance among few-shot KD methods.
为解决现有黑盒少样本知识蒸馏方法中合成数据多样性不足的问题，本文提出一种基于生成对抗网络的自适应训练方案，通过教师监督动态选择高置信度图像并实时引入对抗学习。实验在七个图像数据集上表明，该方法显著提升学生模型精度，优于现有少样本蒸馏方法，达到当前最优性能。
Diverse Image Priors for Black-box Data-free Knowledge Distillation
Authors: Tri-Nhan Vo, Dang Nguyen, Trung Le, Kien Do, Sunil Gupta
First: 2026-04-28T16:02:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:02:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Knowledge distillation (KD) represents a vital mechanism to transfer expertise from complex teacher networks to efficient student models. However, in decentralized or secure AI ecosystems, privacy regulations and proprietary interests often restrict access to the teacher's interface and original datasets. These constraints define a challenging black-box data-free KD scenario where only top-1 predictions and no training data are available. While recent approaches utilize synthetic data, they still face limitations in data diversity and distillation signals. We propose Diverse Image Priors Knowledge Distillation (DIP-KD), a framework that addresses these challenges through a three-phase collaborative pipeline: (1) Synthesis of image priors to capture diverse visual patterns and semantics; (2) Contrast to enhance the collective distinction between synthetic samples via contrastive learning; and (3) Distillation via a novel primer student that enables soft-probability KD. Our evaluation across 12 benchmarks shows that DIP-KD achieves state-of-the-art performance, with ablations confirming data diversity as critical for knowledge acquisition in restricted AI environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向黑盒无数据知识蒸馏的多样化图像先验
知识蒸馏（KD）是将复杂教师网络的经验迁移到高效学生模型的关键机制。然而，在去中心化或安全的AI生态系统中，隐私法规和专有利益常限制对教师接口和原始数据集的访问，这定义了一个具有挑战性的黑盒无数据KD场景，仅能获取top-1预测结果而无训练数据。尽管近期方法利用合成数据，但仍存在数据多样性和蒸馏信号不足的问题。我们提出多样化图像先验知识蒸馏（DIP-KD）框架，通过三阶段协同流程解决上述挑战：（1）合成图像先验以捕捉多样化的视觉模式与语义；（2）通过对比学习增强合成样本间的集体区分度；（3）借助新颖的“引导学生”实现软概率蒸馏。在12个基准测试中，DIP-KD达到最先进性能，消融实验验证了数据多样性在受限AI环境中知识获取中的关键作用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to perform knowledge distillation in privacy-constrained black-box settings where teacher models and training data are inaccessible, this work introduces DIP-KD, a three-phase framework that synthesizes diverse image priors, enhances their discriminative power via contrastive learning, and employs a primer student for soft-probability distillation. Experiments across 12 benchmarks demonstrate that DIP-KD achieves state-of-the-art performance, with ablation studies confirming that data diversity is essential for effective knowledge transfer under such restrictive conditions.
为了解决在隐私受限的黑盒环境中无法访问教师模型和训练数据时的知识蒸馏问题，本文提出了DIP-KD框架，该框架包含三个阶段：合成多样化的图像先验、通过对比学习增强样本区分度、以及通过新型“引子学生”模型进行软概率蒸馏。在12个基准测试中，DIP-KD取得了最先进性能，消融实验验证了数据多样性对在受限AI环境中知识获取的关键作用。
Limited Linguistic Diversity in Embodied AI Datasets
Authors: Selma Wanna, Agnes Luhtaru, Jonathan Salfity, Ryan Barron, Juston Moore, Cynthia Matuszek, Mitch Pryor
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-01-06T16:06:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T16:02:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 (Main Conference)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language plays a critical role in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, yet the linguistic characteristics of the datasets used to train and evaluate these systems remain poorly documented. In this work, we present a systematic dataset audit of several widely used VLA corpora, aiming to characterize what kinds of instructions these datasets actually contain and how much linguistic variety they provide. We quantify instruction language along complementary dimensions--including lexical variety, duplication and overlap, semantic similarity, and syntactic complexity. Our analysis shows that many datasets rely on highly repetitive, template-like commands with limited structural variation, yielding a narrow distribution of instruction forms. We position these findings as descriptive documentation of the language signal available in current VLA training and evaluation data, intended to support more detailed dataset reporting, more principled dataset selection, and targeted curation or augmentation strategies that broaden language coverage.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具身AI数据集中的语言多样性有限
语言在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中起着关键作用，但用于训练和评估这些系统的数据集的语言特征却鲜有记录。本文对多个广泛使用的VLA语料库进行了系统性数据审计，旨在刻画这些数据集实际包含的指令类型及其语言多样性程度。我们从互补维度量化指令语言，包括词汇多样性、重复与重叠、语义相似性及句法复杂性。分析表明，许多数据集依赖高度重复、模板化的指令，结构变化有限，导致指令形式分布狭窄。我们将这些发现视为当前VLA训练与评估数据中可用语言信号的描述性记录，旨在支持更详尽的数据集报告、更原则性的数据集选择，以及有针对性的语料库整理或扩充策略，以扩大语言覆盖范围。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates the linguistic diversity of Vision-Language-Action datasets, motivated by the lack of documentation on how language shapes VLA model training and evaluation. The authors conduct a systematic audit across multiple corpora, measuring instruction language through lexical variety, duplication, semantic similarity, and syntactic complexity. Their findings reveal that most datasets contain highly repetitive, template-like instructions with minimal structural variation, resulting in a narrow distribution of linguistic forms. These results serve as a descriptive baseline for current VLA data, aiming to guide more informed dataset selection and targeted curation efforts to expand linguistic coverage.
该研究针对视觉-语言-动作数据集中语言多样性缺乏文档记录的问题，对多个常用语料库进行了系统性审计，量化了指令语言在词汇多样性、重复率、语义相似性和句法复杂度等维度上的特征。结果表明，许多数据集依赖高度重复、模板化的指令，结构变化有限，导致指令形式分布狭窄。该研究旨在推动更详尽的数据集报告、更合理的数据集选择及有针对性的语料扩充策略，以提升VLA训练数据的语言覆盖广度。
Align then Adapt: Rethinking Parameter-Efficient Transfer Learning in 4D Perception
Authors: Yiding Sun, Jihua Zhu, Haozhe Cheng, Chaoyi Lu, Zhichuan Yang, Lin Chen, Yaonan Wang
First: 2026-02-26T14:58:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:59:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted by IEEE Transactions on Multimedia (Regular Paper)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Point cloud video understanding is critical for robotics as it accurately encodes motion and scene interaction. We recognize that 4D datasets are far scarcer than 3D ones, which hampers the scalability of self-supervised 4D models. A promising alternative is to transfer 3D pre-trained models to 4D perception tasks. However, rigorous empirical analysis reveals two critical limitations that impede transfer capability: overfitting and the modality gap. To overcome these challenges, we develop a novel "Align then Adapt" (PointATA) paradigm that decomposes parameter-efficient transfer learning into two sequential stages. Optimal-transport theory is employed to quantify the distributional discrepancy between 3D and 4D datasets, enabling our proposed point align embedder to be trained in Stage 1 to alleviate the underlying modality gap. To mitigate overfitting, an efficient point-video adapter and a spatial-context encoder are integrated into the frozen 3D backbone to enhance temporal modeling capacity in Stage 2. Notably, with the above engineering-oriented designs, PointATA enables a pre-trained 3D model without temporal knowledge to reason about dynamic video content at a smaller parameter cost compared to previous work. Extensive experiments show that PointATA can match or even outperform strong full fine-tuning models, whilst enjoying the advantage of parameter efficiency, e.g. 97.21 \% accuracy on 3D action recognition, $+8.7 \%$ on 4 D action segmentation, and 84.06\% on 4D semantic segmentation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对齐后适应：重新思考4D感知中的参数高效迁移学习
点云视频理解对机器人至关重要，因为它能准确编码运动和场景交互。我们认识到4D数据集远比3D数据集稀少，这阻碍了自监督4D模型的可扩展性。一种有前景的替代方案是将3D预训练模型迁移到4D感知任务。然而，严谨的实证分析揭示了两个关键限制阻碍迁移能力：过拟合和模态差距。为克服这些挑战，我们提出了一种新颖的“对齐后适应”（PointATA）范式，将参数高效迁移学习分解为两个连续阶段。利用最优传输理论量化3D与4D数据集的分布差异，使我们提出的点对齐嵌入器在第一阶段训练以缓解底层模态差距。为缓解过拟合，我们在冻结的3D主干网络中集成高效点视频适配器和空间上下文编码器，以增强第二阶段的时间建模能力。值得注意的是，通过上述面向工程的设计，PointATA使无时间知识的预训练3D模型以更小的参数开销推理动态视频内容，优于以往工作。大量实验表明，PointATA可匹配甚至超越强全微调模型，同时享有参数高效优势，例如在3D动作识别上达到97.21%准确率，在4D动作分割上提升8.7%，在4D语义分割上达到84.06%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of 4D datasets and the limitations of transferring 3D pre-trained models—namely overfitting and modality gap—the authors propose "Align then Adapt" (PointATA), a two-stage parameter-efficient transfer learning framework. In Stage 1, an optimal-transport-based point align embedder reduces distributional discrepancy between 3D and 4D data. In Stage 2, a point-video adapter and spatial-context encoder are added to a frozen 3D backbone to enhance temporal modeling while preventing overfitting. Experiments show PointATA achieves competitive or superior performance compared to full fine-tuning, with 97.21% accuracy on 3D action recognition, +8.7% on 4D action segmentation, and 84.06% on 4D semantic segmentation, all at lower parameter cost.
为了解决4D数据稀缺导致3D预训练模型难以迁移的问题，作者提出“对齐后适应”（PointATA）两阶段参数高效迁移学习框架。第一阶段通过最优传输理论构建点对齐嵌入器，缓解3D与4D数据模态差异；第二阶段在冻结的3D主干网络上添加点视频适配器与空间上下文编码器，增强时序建模能力并避免过拟合。实验表明，PointATA在保持参数效率的同时，性能可媲美甚至超越全微调模型，如在3D动作识别上达97.21%准确率，4D动作分割提升8.7%，4D语义分割达84.06%。
Evaluating Plan Compliance in Autonomous Programming Agents
Authors: Shuyang Liu, Saman Dehghan, Jatin Ganhotra, Martin Hirzel, Reyhaneh Jabbarvand
First: 2026-04-13T23:54:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:58:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agents aspire to eliminate the need for task-specific prompt crafting through autonomous reason-act-observe loops. Still, they are commonly instructed to follow a task-specific plan for guidance, e.g., to resolve software issues following phases for navigation, reproduction, patch, and validation. Unfortunately, it is unknown to what extent agents actually follow such instructed plans. Without such an analysis, determining the extent agents comply with a given plan, it is impossible to assess whether a solution was reached through correct strategic reasoning or through other means, e.g., data contamination or overfitting to a benchmark. This paper presents the first extensive, systematic analysis of plan compliance in programming agents, examining 16,991 trajectories from SWE-agent across four LLMs on SWE-bench Verified and SWE-bench Pro under eight plan variations. Without an explicit plan, agents fall back on workflows internalized during training, which are often incomplete, overfit, or inconsistently applied. Providing the standard plan improves issue resolution, and we observe that periodic plan reminders can mitigate plan violations and improve task success. A subpar plan hurts performance even more than no plan at all. Surprisingly, augmenting a plan with additional task-relevant phases in the early stage can degrade performance, particularly when these phases do not align with the model's internal problem-solving strategy. These findings highlight a research gap: fine-tuning paradigms that teach models to follow instructed plans, rather than encoding task-specific plans in them. This requires teaching models to reason and act adaptively, rather than memorizing workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自主编程代理中计划合规性的评估
代理旨在通过自主的推理-行动-观察循环消除对任务特定提示的依赖。然而，它们通常仍被要求遵循特定任务的计划以获得指导，例如按导航、复现、修复和验证阶段解决软件问题。遗憾的是，目前尚不清楚代理在多大程度上实际遵循此类指导计划。若缺乏此类分析，就无法判断解决方案是通过正确的战略推理达成，还是通过其他方式（如数据污染或对基准的过拟合）。本文首次对编程代理中的计划合规性进行了全面系统分析，考察了SWE-agent在四个大语言模型上于SWE-bench Verified和SWE-bench Pro中16,991条轨迹，涵盖八种计划变体。若无明确计划，代理将回退至训练期间内化的流程，这些流程往往不完整、过拟合或应用不一致。提供标准计划可提升问题解决效果，我们观察到定期计划提醒可减少计划违规并提高任务成功率。劣质计划甚至比无计划更损害性能。令人惊讶的是，在早期阶段增加与任务相关的额外阶段反而可能降低性能，尤其当这些阶段与模型内部问题解决策略不一致时。这些发现揭示了一个研究空白：需要开发能教导模型遵循指导计划的微调范式，而非将任务特定计划编码进模型。这要求模型学会适应性推理与行动，而非单纯记忆工作流程。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how autonomous programming agents comply with externally provided task plans, motivated by the need to assess whether agents solve problems through strategic reasoning or via memorized patterns. The study analyzes 16,991 agent trajectories across four LLMs on two benchmarks under eight plan variations, revealing that agents without explicit plans rely on incomplete or overfit internal workflows. Providing a standard plan improves performance, and periodic reminders reduce plan violations, while poorly designed plans can harm outcomes more than no plan. Surprisingly, adding early-stage phases that misalign with the agent’s internal strategy can degrade performance, underscoring a gap in current fine-tuning methods that should instead teach adaptive reasoning rather than plan memorization.
该研究旨在评估自主编程代理对指定任务计划的遵守程度，以判断其解决问题是基于策略性推理还是偶然成功。通过对四类大语言模型在两个SWE-bench数据集上的16,991条轨迹进行系统分析，发现无计划时代理依赖不完整或过拟合的内部工作流。提供标准计划可提升表现，定期提醒可减少计划违规。令人意外的是，早期添加任务阶段反而可能降低性能，若与模型内部解题策略冲突。研究指出当前微调方法未能教会代理灵活遵循计划，亟需转向以推理能力为核心的训练范式。
KinDER: A Physical Reasoning Benchmark for Robot Learning and Planning
Authors: Yixuan Huang, Bowen Li, Vaibhav Saxena, Yichao Liang, Utkarsh Aashu Mishra, Liang Ji, Lihan Zha, Jimmy Wu, Nishanth Kumar, Sebastian Scherer, Danfei Xu, Tom Silver
Venue: RSS
First: 2026-04-28T15:58:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:58:09+00:00
Comments: Project website: https://prpl-group.com/kinder-site/. 21 pages, 8 figures. Accepted to Robotics Science and Systems (RSS), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotic systems that interact with the physical world must reason about kinematic and dynamic constraints imposed by their own embodiment, their environment, and the task at hand. We introduce KinDER, a benchmark for Kinematic and Dynamic Embodied Reasoning that targets physical reasoning challenges arising in robot learning and planning. KinDER comprises 25 procedurally generated environments, a Gymnasium-compatible Python library with parameterized skills and demonstrations, and a standardized evaluation suite with 13 implemented baselines spanning task and motion planning, imitation learning, reinforcement learning, and foundation-model-based approaches. The environments are designed to isolate five core physical reasoning challenges: basic spatial relations, nonprehensile multi-object manipulation, tool use, combinatorial geometric constraints, and dynamic constraints, disentangled from perception, language understanding, and application-specific complexity. Empirical evaluation shows that existing methods struggle to solve many of the environments, indicating substantial gaps in current approaches to physical reasoning. We additionally include real-to-sim-to-real experiments on a mobile manipulator to assess the correspondence between simulation and real-world physical interaction. KinDER is fully open-sourced and intended to enable systematic comparison across diverse paradigms for advancing physical reasoning in robotics. Website and code: https://prpl-group.com/kinder-site/
中文标题/摘要
标题：KinDER：面向机器人学习与规划的物理推理基准
与物理世界交互的机器人系统必须对其自身本体、环境及任务所施加的运动学和动力学约束进行推理。我们提出了KinDER，一个面向运动学与动力学本体推理的基准，旨在解决机器人学习与规划中出现的物理推理挑战。KinDER包含25个程序生成的环境、一个兼容Gymnasium的Python库（含参数化技能与演示），以及一套标准化评估套件，其中包含13个基线方法，涵盖任务与运动规划、模仿学习、强化学习及基于基础模型的方法。环境设计旨在隔离五大核心物理推理挑战：基本空间关系、非抓取式多物体操作、工具使用、组合几何约束及动态约束，并与感知、语言理解及应用特定复杂性解耦。实证评估表明，现有方法难以解决多数环境，揭示当前物理推理方法存在显著差距。我们还对移动操作臂进行了实-仿-实实验，以评估仿真与真实物理交互的一致性。KinDER完全开源，旨在促进不同范式间系统性比较，推动机器人领域物理推理的发展。网站与代码：https://prpl-group.com/kinder-site/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for standardized evaluation of physical reasoning in robotics, KinDER introduces a benchmark designed to test how well robotic systems handle kinematic and dynamic constraints in embodied tasks. It features 25 procedurally generated environments that isolate five core physical reasoning challenges—spatial relations, nonprehensile manipulation, tool use, geometric constraints, and dynamics—while removing confounding factors like perception or language. The benchmark includes a Gymnasium-compatible library, parameterized skills, demonstrations, and 13 baseline methods covering imitation, reinforcement, and foundation models. 为评估机器人在物理世界中的推理能力，KinDER提出一套标准化基准，专门测试机器人在具身任务中处理运动学与动力学约束的能力。该基准包含25个程序生成的环境，分别隔离五大核心物理推理挑战——空间关系、非抓取式多物体操作、工具使用、几何约束与动态约束，并剥离感知与语言理解等干扰因素。配套提供Gymnasium兼容库及13种基线方法，涵盖模仿学习、强化学习与基础模型等范式。实验表明，多数现有方法难以解决多个环境，揭示当前物理推理能力存在显著不足。在移动机械臂上的实测实验进一步显示，仿真与真实物理交互之间存在明显差异。
Subliminal Steering: Stronger Encoding of Hidden Signals
Authors: George Morgulis, John Hewitt
First: 2026-04-28T15:51:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:51:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Subliminal learning describes a student language model inheriting a behavioral bias by fine-tuning on seemingly innocuous data generated by a biased teacher model. Prior work has begun to characterize this phenomenon but leaves open questions about the scope of signals it can transfer, the mechanisms that explain it, and the precision with which a bias can be encoded by seemingly unrelated data. We tackle all three problems by introducing subliminal steering, a variant of subliminal learning in which the teacher's bias is implemented not via a system prompt, as in prior work, but through a steering vector trained to maximize the likelihood of a set of target samples. First, we show that subliminal steering transfers complex multi-word biases, whereas prior work focused on single-word preferences, demonstrating a large scope of subliminally transferrable signals. Second, we provide mechanistic evidence that subliminal learning transfers not only the target behavioral bias, but also the steering vector itself, localized to the layers at which the teacher was steered. Finally, we show that the bias is encoded with surprising precision. We train a new steering vector directly on the subliminally-laden dataset and find that it attains high cosine similarity with the original vector.
中文标题/摘要
标题：潜意识引导：隐藏信号的更强编码
潜意识学习描述了学生语言模型通过在看似无害但由有偏见教师模型生成的数据上微调，从而继承行为偏见的现象。先前研究已开始刻画这一现象，但仍存在关于其可传递信号范围、解释机制以及看似无关数据编码偏置的精确度等问题。我们通过引入潜意识引导（一种潜意识学习的变体）来解决这三个问题：教师的偏见不再通过系统提示实现（如先前工作），而是通过一个训练以最大化目标样本似然性的引导向量实现。首先，我们证明潜意识引导可传递复杂的多词偏见，而先前工作仅关注单词偏好，表明潜意识可传递信号的范围更广。其次，我们提供机制性证据表明，潜意识学习不仅传递目标行为偏见，还传递引导向量本身，且该向量定位在教师被引导的层。最后，我们发现偏见被以惊人精度编码：我们直接在潜意识负载数据集上训练新的引导向量，发现其与原始向量具有高余弦相似度。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates subliminal learning in language models, motivated by the need to understand how biases can be transferred through seemingly neutral data. Mechanistically, the study reveals that the steering vector itself is transferred to specific teacher layers, and crucially, a new steering vector trained on the biased dataset achieves high cosine similarity with the original, indicating precise encoding of the bias.
该研究旨在探究语言模型如何通过看似中性的数据传递偏见，动机在于厘清隐性学习的机制与范围。作者提出“隐性引导”方法，通过训练一个引导向量使教师模型在目标样本上最大化似然，而非依赖系统提示。实验表明，该方法可传递复杂的多词偏见，超越以往仅关注单词偏好的研究，显示了更广泛的信号传递能力。机制上，研究发现引导向量本身被传递并定位在教师模型被引导的特定层。更重要的是，直接在隐性偏见数据集上训练的新引导向量与原始向量高度相似，表明偏见被精确编码。
EOS-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Earth Observation Satellite Scheduling
Authors: Qian Yin, Jiaxing Li, Jiaqi Cheng, Qizhang Luo, Annalisa Riccardi, Abhijit Chatterjee, Rafael Vazquez, Carlo Novara, Michalis Mavrovouniotis, Ponnuthurai Nagaratnam Suganthan, Shengzhou Bai, Xiaoxuan Hu, Lining Xing, Ming Xu, Shuang Li, Zixuan Zheng, Xin Shen, Xiaoyu Chen, Yi Gu, Yanjie Song, Witold Pedrycz, Evan L. Kramer, Laio Oriel Seman, Cletah Shoko, Guohua Wu, Xinwei Wang
First: 2026-04-28T15:49:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:49:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Earth observation satellite imaging scheduling is a challenging NP-hard combinatorial optimisation problem central to space mission operations. While next-generation agile Earth observation satellites (EOS) increase operational flexibility, they also significantly raise scheduling complexity. The lack of a unified, open-source benchmark makes it difficult to compare algorithms across studies. This paper introduces EOS-Bench, a comprehensive framework for systematic and reproducible evaluation of scheduling methods. By integrating high-fidelity orbital dynamics and platform constraints, EOS-Bench generates 1,390 scenarios and 13,900 benchmark instances, spanning from small-scale validation cases to large coordination problems with up to 1,000 satellites and 10,000 requests. We further propose a scenario characterisation scheme to quantify structural difficulty based on factors such as opportunity density, task flexibility, conflict intensity, and satellite congestion. A multidimensional evaluation protocol is introduced, assessing performance across five metrics: task profit, completion rate, workload balance, timeliness, and runtime. The framework is evaluated using mixed-integer programming, heuristics, meta-heuristics, and deep reinforcement learning across both agile and non-agile settings. Results show that EOS-Bench effectively distinguishes solver performance across scales and conditions, revealing trade-offs between solution quality and computational efficiency, and providing deeper insight into scenario complexity. EOS-Bench offers a unified and extensible open testbed for advancing research in Earth observation satellite scheduling. The code and data are available at https://github.com/Ethan19YQ/EOS-Bench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EOS-Bench：地球观测卫星调度的综合基准
地球观测卫星成像调度是空间任务运营中的核心NP难组合优化问题。新一代敏捷地球观测卫星（EOS）虽提升了运营灵活性，但也显著增加了调度复杂性。缺乏统一的开源基准，使得跨研究比较算法变得困难。本文提出EOS-Bench，一个用于系统化、可复现评估调度方法的综合框架。通过整合高保真轨道动力学与平台约束，EOS-Bench生成1,390个场景和13,900个基准实例，涵盖从小规模验证案例到最多1,000颗卫星和10,000个请求的大型协调问题。 我们进一步提出场景特征化方案，基于机会密度、任务灵活性、冲突强度和卫星拥堵等因素量化结构难度。引入多维评估协议，从任务利润、完成率、负载均衡、及时性及运行时间五个维度评估性能。框架在敏捷与非敏捷设置下，通过混合整数规划、启发式算法、元启发式及深度强化学习进行评估。结果表明，EOS-Bench能有效区分不同规模和条件下的求解器性能，揭示解质量与计算效率之间的权衡，并提供对场景复杂性的更深入洞察。 EOS-Bench提供了一个统一且可扩展的开源测试平台，推动地球观测卫星调度研究发展。代码与数据可在https://github.com/Ethan19YQ/EOS-Bench获取。
