It generates 13,900 diverse scheduling instances across 1,390 scenarios, incorporating realistic orbital dynamics and platform constraints, and scales up to 1,000 satellites and 10,000 requests. The framework employs a scenario characterization scheme measuring factors like opportunity density and conflict intensity, and evaluates algorithms using five metrics: task profit, completion rate, workload balance, timeliness, and runtime. Experiments with mixed-integer programming, heuristics, meta-heuristics, and deep reinforcement learning demonstrate EOS-Bench’s ability to reliably differentiate solver performance, reveal trade-offs between solution quality and efficiency, and expose structural complexity in scheduling problems.
为应对敏捷地球观测卫星调度日益增长的复杂性，本文提出EOS-Bench，一个包含1390个场景和13900个实例的开源统一基准框架，结合高保真轨道动力学与平台约束。该框架通过任务利润、完成率、负载均衡、及时性与运行时间五个维度评估算法性能，涵盖混合整数规划、启发式、元启发式及深度强化学习方法。实验表明，该框架能有效区分不同算法在不同规模与条件下的表现，揭示解质量与计算效率间的权衡，并揭示机会密度、卫星拥堵等场景特征对调度难度的影响。
Sketch2Arti: Sketch-based Articulation Modeling of CAD Objects
Authors: Yi Yang, Hao Pan, Yijing Cui, Alla Sheffer, Changjian Li
First: 2026-04-28T15:47:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:47:30+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://arlo-yang.github.io/Sketch2Arti
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Articulation modeling aims to infer movable parts and their motion parameters for a 3D object, enabling interactive animation, simulation, and shape editing. In this paper, we present Sketch2Arti, the first sketch-based articulation modeling system for CAD objects. Our key observation is that designers naturally communicate articulation intent through lightweight sketches (e.g., arrows and strokes) that indicate how parts should move, yet translating such sketches into articulated 3D models remains largely manual. Sketch2Arti bridges this gap by enabling users to specify articulation through simple 2D sketches drawn from a chosen viewpoint. Given a CAD model and user sketches, our approach automatically discovers the corresponding movable parts and predicts their motion parameters, allowing iterative modeling of multiple articulations on complex objects with fine-grained control. Importantly, Sketch2Arti is trained in a category-agnostic manner without requiring object category information, leading to strong generalization to diverse objects beyond existing articulation datasets. Moreover, for shell models lacking interior structures, Sketch2Arti supports controllable internal completion guided by user sketches, generating plausible internal components consistent with the existing geometry and predicted motion constraints. Comprehensive experiments and user evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness, controllability, and generalization of Sketch2Arti. The code, dataset, and the prototype system are at https://arlo-yang.github.io/Sketch2Arti.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Sketch2Arti：基于草图的CAD对象运动建模
运动建模旨在推断3D对象的可动部件及其运动参数，以支持交互式动画、仿真和形状编辑。本文提出Sketch2Arti，这是首个面向CAD对象的基于草图的运动建模系统。我们的核心观察是，设计师常通过轻量级草图（如箭头和笔画）自然表达运动意图，但将此类草图转化为可动3D模型仍主要依赖手工操作。Sketch2Arti弥合了这一差距，允许用户从选定视角绘制简单2D草图来指定运动。给定CAD模型和用户草图，我们的方法可自动发现对应可动部件并预测其运动参数，支持对复杂对象进行多运动建模并实现精细控制。重要的是，Sketch2Arti以无类别依赖方式训练，无需对象类别信息，从而在现有运动数据集之外展现出强大的泛化能力。此外，对于缺乏内部结构的壳模型，Sketch2Arti支持基于用户草图的可控内部补全，生成与现有几何结构和预测运动约束一致的合理内部组件。全面的实验和用户评估验证了Sketch2Arti的有效性、可控性和泛化能力。代码、数据集和原型系统见https://arlo-yang.github.io/Sketch2Arti。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate articulation modeling for CAD objects using intuitive user input, Sketch2Arti enables designers to specify movable parts and motion parameters via simple 2D sketches drawn from a chosen viewpoint. The system automatically infers articulation by aligning user sketches with the 3D geometry, predicting motion constraints without requiring object category labels, which enhances its generalization across diverse CAD models. Experimental results show that Sketch2Arti successfully models multiple articulations with fine-grained control and can even generate plausible internal components for shell models, demonstrating strong performance and user satisfaction in both quantitative evaluations and real-world use cases.
为解决CAD对象自动建模关节运动的问题，Sketch2Arti允许用户通过选定视角下的简单2D草图指定可动部件及其运动参数。系统无需物体类别信息，自动将草图与3D几何对齐，预测运动约束，从而实现对多种复杂对象的精细控制与泛化建模。实验表明，该方法能有效建模多关节运动，并支持壳体模型的可控内部补全，生成符合运动约束的合理内部组件，经定量评估与用户测试验证其有效性。
Sustained Gradient Alignment Mediates Subliminal Learning in a Multi-Step Setting: Evidence from MNIST Auxiliary Logit Distillation Experiment
Authors: Chayanon Kitkana, Shivam Arora
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-04-28T15:46:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:46:18+00:00
Comments: Published in ICLR 2026 Sci4DL Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In the MNIST auxiliary logit distillation experiment, a student can acquire an unintended teacher trait despite distilling only on no-class logits through a phenomenon called subliminal learning. Under a single-step gradient descent assumption, subliminal learning theory attributes this effect to alignment between the trait and distillation gradients, but does not guarantee that this alignment persists in a multi-step setting. We empirically show that gradient alignment remains weakly but consistently positive throughout training and causally contributes to trait acquisition. We show that a mitigation method called liminal training works by attenuating the alignment and fails to stop trait acquisition in this setup. These results suggest that mitigation methods that operate in this regime may not reliably suppress trait acquisition when the first-order drive dominates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：持续梯度对齐介导多步设置中的潜意识学习：来自MNIST辅助logit蒸馏实验的证据
在MNIST辅助logit蒸馏实验中，尽管仅蒸馏无类别logit，学生仍可习得教师的非预期特征，这一现象称为潜意识学习。在单步梯度下降假设下，潜意识学习理论将此效应归因于特征与蒸馏梯度间的对齐，但无法保证该对齐在多步设置中持续存在。我们实证表明，梯度对齐在训练过程中始终弱但稳定地呈正相关，并因果性地促进特征习得。我们证明，一种称为‘临界训练’的缓解方法通过削弱对齐而起作用，但在该设置下无法阻止特征习得。这些结果表明，在一阶驱动占主导的环境下，此类缓解方法可能无法可靠抑制特征习得。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how unintended traits are acquired during logit distillation, this study investigates subliminal learning in a multi-step training setting using MNIST auxiliary logit distillation. The method involves training a student model to mimic teacher logits without explicit class supervision, and the authors measure gradient alignment between the teacher trait and student gradients across training steps. Experimental results show that gradient alignment remains weakly but consistently positive throughout training, and causal analysis confirms its contribution to trait acquisition. Importantly, a mitigation technique called liminal training, which aims to reduce alignment, fails to prevent trait transfer when first-order gradient dynamics dominate.
为探究在多步训练中，教师特征与蒸馏梯度之间的梯度对齐是否仍能驱动潜意识学习，本研究基于MNIST辅助logit蒸馏实验，发现梯度对齐在训练过程中虽强度较弱但持续为正，并因果性地促进特征获取。进一步实验表明，旨在削弱梯度对齐的缓解方法“liminal训练”在此设定下无法阻止特征迁移，暗示当一阶梯度主导时，此类缓解方法可能无法可靠抑制特征泄露。
Can Code Evaluation Metrics Detect Code Plagiarism?
Authors: Fahad Ebrahim, Mike Joy
First: 2026-04-28T15:45:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:45:44+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 5 figures, accepted at LEARNER 2026 workshop (associated with EASE 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Source Code Plagiarism Detection (SCPD) plays an important role in maintaining fairness and academic integrity in software engineering education. Code Evaluation Metrics (CEMs) are developed for assessing code generation tasks. However, it remains unclear whether such metrics can reliably detect plagiarism across different levels of modification (L1-L6), increasing in complexity. In this paper, we perform a comparative empirical study using two open-source labelled datasets, ConPlag (raw and template-free versions) and IRPlag. We evaluate five CEMs, namely CodeBLEU, CrystalBLEU, RUBY, Tree Structured Edit Distance (TSED), and CodeBERTScore. The performance is evaluated using threshold-free ranking-based measures to assess overall, per dataset, and per-level plagiarism performance. The results are compared against state-of-the-art (SOTA) Source Code Plagiarism Detection Tools (SCPDTs), JPlag and Dolos. Our findings show that without preprocessing, Dolos achieves the highest overall ranking performance, while among the individual metrics, CrystalBLEU, CodeBLEU, and RUBY outperform JPlag. Performance is strongest at L1 and drops from L4 onward, while CrystalBLEU remains competitive on L6. With preprocessing, CrystalBLEU surpasses Dolos overall. Per dataset, Dolos achieved the best ranking on the ConPlag raw dataset, while CrystalBLEU was the best-performing metric on the remaining datasets. At the plagiarism levels, Dolos remains strongest on L4, while Crystal-BLEU leads most of the remaining difficult levels. These results indicate that CEMs are comparable to dedicated tools in terms of ranking metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代码评估指标能检测代码抄袭吗？
源代码抄袭检测（SCPD）在维护软件工程教育的公平性和学术诚信方面起着重要作用。代码评估指标（CEMs）用于评估代码生成任务，但尚不清楚这些指标能否可靠地检测不同修改级别（L1-L6，复杂度递增）下的抄袭。 本文使用两个开源标注数据集ConPlag（原始版和模板无版本）和IRPlag，进行比较性实证研究。我们评估了五种CEMs：CodeBLEU、CrystalBLEU、RUBY、树结构编辑距离（TSED）和CodeBERTScore。性能通过无阈值的排名型指标评估，涵盖整体、各数据集及各抄袭级别表现。结果与当前最先进的源代码抄袭检测工具（SCPDTs）JPlag和Dolos进行对比。 研究发现：未经预处理时，Dolos整体排名表现最佳；在单个指标中，CrystalBLEU、CodeBLEU和RUBY优于JPlag。性能在L1最强，从L4开始下降，而CrystalBLEU在L6仍具竞争力。经预处理后，CrystalBLEU整体超越Dolos。按数据集划分，Dolos在ConPlag原始数据集上排名最佳，而CrystalBLEU在其余数据集中表现最佳。按抄袭级别，Dolos在L4表现最强，CrystalBLEU在其余较难级别中领先。结果表明，CEMs在排名指标上与专用工具相当。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether Code Evaluation Metrics (CEMs) can detect source code plagiarism across varying levels of modification, motivated by the need for fair academic assessment in software education. Five CEMs—CodeBLEU, CrystalBLEU, RUBY, TSED, and CodeBERTScore—are evaluated against two open-source datasets (ConPlag and IRPlag) using threshold-free ranking metrics, and compared with state-of-the-art plagiarism tools JPlag and Dolos. Results show that without preprocessing, Dolos ranks highest overall, while CrystalBLEU, CodeBLEU, and RUBY outperform JPlag; performance declines from L4 onward but CrystalBLEU remains strong at L6. With preprocessing, CrystalBLEU surpasses Dolos overall. Dolos excels on ConPlag raw, while CrystalBLEU dominates other datasets and most difficult plagiarism levels (L5–L6).
为评估代码评价指标能否检测不同修改程度下的代码抄袭，本研究对比了五种CEM（CodeBLEU、CrystalBLEU、RUBY、TSED、CodeBERTScore）与两种顶尖抄袭检测工具（JPlag和Dolos）在ConPlag和IRPlag数据集上的表现。结果显示，未经预处理时Dolos整体排名最高，而CrystalBLEU、CodeBLEU和RUBY优于JPlag；性能从L4级开始下降，但CrystalBLEU在L6仍具竞争力。经预处理后，CrystalBLEU整体超越Dolos；在数据集层面，Dolos在ConPlag原始数据集表现最佳，CrystalBLEU在其余数据集表现最优；在抄袭级别上，Dolos在L4表现最强，CrystalBLEU在多数困难级别领先，表明CEM在排名评估上可媲美专用工具。
Multimodal Contextualized Support for Enhancing Video Retrieval System
Authors: Quoc-Bao Nguyen-Le, Thanh-Huy Le-Nguyen
First: 2024-12-10T15:20:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:45:36+00:00
Comments: This paper has been withdrawn by the author. After further review, the author believes that the current version does not meet the desired standards and plans to revise the work before any potential resubmission
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current video retrieval systems, especially those used in competitions, primarily focus on querying individual keyframes or images rather than encoding an entire clip or video segment. However, queries often describe an action or event over a series of frames, not a specific image. This results in insufficient information when analyzing a single frame, leading to less accurate query results. Moreover, extracting embeddings solely from images (keyframes) does not provide enough information for models to encode higher-level, more abstract insights inferred from the video. These models tend to only describe the objects present in the frame, lacking a deeper understanding. In this work, we propose a system that integrates the latest methodologies, introducing a novel pipeline that extracts multimodal data, and incorporate information from multiple frames within a video, enabling the model to abstract higher-level information that captures latent meanings, focusing on what can be inferred from the video clip, rather than just focusing on object detection in one single image.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多模态上下文支持增强视频检索系统
当前视频检索系统，尤其是竞赛中使用的系统，主要侧重于查询单个关键帧或图像，而非整个视频片段。然而，查询通常描述的是多帧中的动作或事件，而非特定图像。这导致单帧分析信息不足，检索结果不够准确。此外，仅从图像（关键帧）提取嵌入信息，无法为模型提供足够的信息以编码从视频中推断出的更高层次、更抽象的语义。这些模型往往仅描述帧内存在的物体，缺乏深层理解。本文提出一种整合最新方法的系统，引入新颖的多模态数据提取流程，并结合视频内多帧信息，使模型能够抽象出捕捉潜在语义的高层信息，关注从视频片段中可推断的内容，而非仅关注单帧中的物体检测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of current video retrieval systems that rely on single keyframes rather than full video segments, this work proposes a multimodal contextualized approach to better capture action and event semantics across multiple frames. The method integrates information from multiple frames within a video clip to extract richer, higher-level representations that go beyond object detection in individual images. Experimental results demonstrate that this pipeline significantly improves retrieval accuracy by enabling models to infer latent meanings from video context rather than relying on isolated frame features.
鉴于当前视频检索系统主要依赖单帧关键图而非完整视频片段，本文提出一种多模态上下文增强方法，通过整合视频中多帧信息以捕捉动作与事件的语义。该方法构建了新的处理流程，使模型能从视频整体中抽象出更高层次的语义，突破仅依赖单帧物体检测的局限。实验表明，该方法显著提升了检索准确率，使模型能从视频上下文推断出潜在含义，而非仅依赖静态帧内容。
Novel 3D Binary Indexed Tree for Volume Computation of 3D Reconstructed Models from Volumetric Data
Authors: Quoc-Bao Nguyen-Le, Tuan-Hy Le, Anh-Triet Do
First: 2024-12-11T11:29:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:44:22+00:00
Comments: This paper has been withdrawn by the author. After further review, the author believes that the current version does not meet the desired standards and plans to revise the work before any potential resubmission
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In the burgeoning field of medical imaging, precise computation of 3D volume holds a significant importance for subsequent qualitative analysis of 3D reconstructed objects. Combining multivariate calculus, marching cube algorithm, and binary indexed tree data structure, we developed an algorithm for efficient computation of intrinsic volume of any volumetric data recovered from computed tomography (CT) or magnetic resonance (MR). We proposed the 30 configurations of volume values based on the polygonal mesh generation method. Our algorithm processes the data in scan-line order simultaneously with reconstruction algorithm to create a Fenwick tree, ensuring query time much faster and assisting users' edition of slicing or transforming model. We tested the algorithm's accuracy on simple 3D objects (e.g., sphere, cylinder) to complicated structures (e.g., lungs, cardiac chambers). The result deviated within $\pm 0.004 \text{cm}^3$ and there is still room for further improvement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于从体数据重建三维模型体积计算的新型三维二进制索引树
在蓬勃发展的医学成像领域，精确计算三维体积对后续三维重建对象的定性分析具有重要意义。结合多元微积分、Marching Cube算法和二进制索引树数据结构，我们开发了一种高效计算从计算机断层扫描（CT）或磁共振（MR）获取的任意体数据内在体积的算法。我们基于多边形网格生成方法提出了30种体积值配置。该算法在扫描线顺序下与重建算法同步处理数据，构建Fenwick树，确保查询时间显著加快，并辅助用户编辑切片或变换模型。我们在简单三维物体（如球体、圆柱体）到复杂结构（如肺、心腔）上测试了算法的准确性，结果偏差在±0.004 cm³内，仍有进一步改进空间。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate 3D volume computation in medical imaging, this study combines multivariate calculus, the marching cube algorithm, and a binary indexed tree (Fenwick tree) to efficiently calculate intrinsic volumes from CT or MR volumetric data. The method generates 30 volume configurations via polygonal meshing and processes data in scan-line order during reconstruction to enable fast querying and support model editing. Experimental results on simple shapes like spheres and cylinders as well as complex anatomical structures such as lungs and cardiac chambers showed volume deviations within ±0.004 cm³, indicating high accuracy with potential for further refinement.
为满足医学影像中三维体积精确计算的需求，该研究结合多元微积分、Marching Cube算法和二叉索引树（Fenwick树），提出一种高效计算CT或MR体数据内在体积的方法。该方法通过多边形网格生成30种体积配置，并在重建过程中按扫描线顺序处理数据，以实现快速查询和模型编辑支持。实验在球体、圆柱等简单结构及肺部、心腔等复杂解剖结构上进行，体积误差控制在±0.004 cm³内，表明方法精度高，仍有优化空间。
Unrequited Emotions: Investigating the Gaps in Motivation and Practice in Speech Emotion Recognition Research
Authors: Taryn Wong, Zeerak Talat, Hanan Aldarmaki, Anjalie Field
First: 2026-04-28T15:43:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:43:35+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the Workshop on Computational Affective Science (CAS) at LREC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Critical analyses of emotion recognition technology have raised ethical concerns around task validity and potential downstream impacts, urging researchers to ensure alignment between their stated motivations and practice. However, these discussions have not adequately influenced or drawn from research on speech emotion recognition (SER). We address this gap by conducting a systematic survey of SER research to uncover what stated motivations drive this work and if they align with the datasets and emotions studied. We find that while SER research identifies appealing goals, such as well-situated voice-activated systems or healthcare applications, commonly-used datasets do not reflect these proposed deployment contexts, thus presenting a gap between motivations and research practices. We argue that such gaps engender ethical concerns, and that SER research should reassert itself with concrete use-cases to prevent misinterpretations, misuse, and downstream harms.

该研究探讨了语音情感识别（SER）领域中研究动机与实际实践之间的脱节，动机源于对技术有效性及潜在负面影响的伦理担忧。通过系统梳理SER文献，作者分析了常用数据集与研究情感是否与所宣称的应用场景（如语音激活系统或医疗应用）相匹配。结果发现，尽管这些应用场景常被引用为研究目标，但实际使用的数据集很少反映真实部署环境，导致动机与实践之间存在显著差距。作者认为，这种脱节可能引发伦理误解和滥用，建议SER研究应以具体、情境化的应用场景为基础，以降低潜在风险。
Task-Driven Prompt Learning: A Joint Framework for Multi-modal Cloud Removal and Segmentation
Authors: Zaiyan Zhang, Jie Li, Shaowei Shi, Qiangqiang Yuan
First: 2026-01-17T13:32:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:41:24+00:00
Comments: Accepted by IGARSS 2026 Conference (Oral)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Optical remote sensing imagery is indispensable for Earth observation, yet persistent cloud occlusion limits its downstream utility. Most cloud removal (CR) methods are optimized for low-level fidelity and can over-smooth textures and boundaries that are critical for analysis-ready data (ARD), leading to a mismatch between visually plausible restoration and semantic utility. To bridge this gap, we propose TDP-CR, a task-driven multimodal framework that jointly performs cloud removal and land-cover segmentation. Central to our approach is a Prompt-Guided Fusion (PGF) mechanism, which utilizes a learnable degradation prompt to encode cloud thickness and spatial uncertainty. By combining global channel context with local prompt-conditioned spatial bias, PGF adaptively integrates Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) information only where optical data is corrupted. We further introduce a parameter-efficient two-phase training strategy that decouples reconstruction and semantic representation learning. Experiments on the LuojiaSET-OSFCR dataset demonstrate the superiority of our framework: TDP-CR surpasses heavy state-of-the-art baselines by 0.18 dB in PSNR while using only 15\% of the parameters, and achieves a 1.4\% improvement in mIoU consistently against multi-task competitors, effectively delivering analysis-ready data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：任务驱动的提示学习：多模态云去除与分割的联合框架
光学遥感影像对地球观测至关重要，但持续存在的云遮挡限制了其下游应用。大多数云去除（CR）方法侧重于低级保真度，易过度平滑分析就绪数据（ARD）所需的纹理和边界，导致视觉上合理重建与语义实用性不匹配。为弥合此差距，我们提出TDP-CR，一种任务驱动的多模态联合框架，同时执行云去除与地表覆盖分割。核心是提示引导融合（PGF）机制，利用可学习的退化提示编码云厚度与空间不确定性。通过结合全局通道上下文与局部提示条件的空间偏差，PGF仅在光学数据受损区域自适应融合合成孔径雷达（SAR）信息。我们进一步引入参数高效的两阶段训练策略，分离重建与语义表征学习。在LuojiaSET-OSFCR数据集上的实验表明，TDP-CR在仅使用15%参数的情况下，PSNR优于现有最先进方法0.18 dB，并在mIoU上持续优于多任务竞争方法1.4%，有效生成分析就绪数据。
Summary / 总结
To address the issue that conventional cloud removal methods over-smooth critical textures and boundaries, reducing their utility for analysis-ready data, this paper proposes TDP-CR, a task-driven multimodal framework that jointly performs cloud removal and land-cover segmentation. The core mechanism, Prompt-Guided Fusion, uses a learnable degradation prompt to encode cloud characteristics and selectively integrates Synthetic Aperture Radar data only where optical imagery is corrupted, combining global context with local spatial bias. A parameter-efficient two-phase training strategy decouples reconstruction and semantic learning. Experiments on LuojiaSET-OSFCR show TDP-CR outperforms state-of-the-art methods by 0.18 dB in PSNR with only 15% of their parameters and achieves a 1.4% higher mIoU than multi-task competitors, demonstrating superior semantic and visual fidelity for downstream analysis.
为解决传统云去除方法过度平滑关键纹理与边界、降低分析就绪数据可用性的问题，本文提出TDP-CR框架，联合执行云去除与地物分割任务。其核心是Prompt-Guided Fusion机制，通过可学习的退化提示编码云层厚度与空间不确定性，并仅在光学图像受云干扰区域选择性融合合成孔径雷达数据，结合全局通道上下文与局部空间偏置。采用参数高效的两阶段训练策略，解耦重建与语义学习过程，提升训练效率。在LuojiaSET-OSFCR数据集上的实验表明，TDP-CR在仅使用15%参数的情况下，PSNR比最先进方法高0.18dB，并持续在mIoU上优于多任务竞争方法1.4%，有效生成分析就绪图像。
CGU-ILALab at FoodBench-QA 2026: Comparing Traditional and LLM-based Approaches for Recipe Nutrient Estimation
Authors: Wei-Chun Chen, Yu-Xuan Chen, I-Fang Chung, Ying-Jia Lin
First: 2026-04-28T15:41:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:41:01+00:00
Comments: Accepted by the Third Workshop on Patient-oriented Language Processing (CL4Health) at LREC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate nutrient estimation from unstructured recipe text is an important yet challenging problem in dietary monitoring, due to ambiguous ingredient terminology and highly variable quantity expressions. We systematically evaluate models spanning a wide range of representational capacity, from lexical matching methods (TF-IDF with Ridge Regression), to deep semantic encoders (DeBERTa-v3), to generative reasoning with large language models (LLMs). Under the strict tolerance criteria defined by EU Regulation 1169/2011, our empirical results reveal a clear trade-off between predictive accuracy and computational efficiency. The TF-IDF baseline achieves moderate nutrient estimation performance with near-instantaneous inference, whereas the DeBERTa-v3 encoder performs poorly under task-specific data scarcity. In contrast, few-shot LLM inference (e.g., Gemini 2.5 Flash) and a hybrid LLM refinement pipeline (TF-IDF combined with Gemini 2.5 Flash) deliver the highest validation accuracy across all nutrient categories. These improvements likely arise from the ability of LLMs to leverage pre-trained world knowledge to resolve ambiguous terminology and normalize non-standard units, which remain difficult for purely lexical approaches. However, these gains come at the cost of substantially higher inference latency, highlighting a practical deployment trade-off between real-time efficiency and nutritional precision in dietary monitoring systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CGU-ILALab在FoodBench-QA 2026：比较传统方法与基于大语言模型的方法用于食谱营养素估算
从非结构化食谱文本中准确估算营养素是饮食监测中的重要且具有挑战性的问题，由于食材术语模糊且数量表达高度可变。我们系统评估了从词典匹配方法（TF-IDF结合岭回归）到深度语义编码器（DeBERTa-v3），再到基于大语言模型（LLMs）的生成推理模型。在欧盟法规1169/2011所定义的严格容差标准下，我们的实证结果揭示了预测精度与计算效率之间的明显权衡。TF-IDF基线方法在近即时推理下实现中等营养素估算性能，而DeBERTa-v3编码器在任务特定数据稀缺时表现不佳。相比之下，少样本LLM推理（如Gemini 2.5 Flash）及混合LLM优化流程（TF-IDF结合Gemini 2.5 Flash）在所有营养素类别中均达到最高验证精度。这些改进可能源于LLM能够利用预训练世界知识解决术语模糊性并标准化非标准单位，而纯词典方法难以实现。然而，这些收益以显著更高的推理延迟为代价，凸显了饮食监测系统中实时效率与营养精度之间的实际部署权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately estimating nutrients from unstructured recipe text under regulatory constraints, this study compares traditional lexical methods, deep semantic encoders, and LLM-based approaches. It evaluates TF-IDF with Ridge Regression, DeBERTa-v3, and LLM-based few-shot and hybrid pipelines under EU Regulation 1169/2011 tolerance criteria. Results show TF-IDF offers fast inference with moderate accuracy, while DeBERTa-v3 underperforms with limited task-specific data. LLM-based methods, especially hybrid pipelines combining TF-IDF with Gemini 2.5 Flash, achieve highest validation accuracy across all nutrients due to their ability to resolve ambiguous terminology and normalize units. However, these gains come with significantly higher inference latency, revealing a key trade-off between precision and real-time efficiency in dietary monitoring systems.
为解决在欧盟法规1169/2011严格容差标准下从非结构化食谱文本准确估算营养成分的挑战，本研究对比了传统词典方法、深度语义编码器和基于大语言模型（LLM）的方法。实验涵盖从TF-IDF与岭回归到DeBERTa-v3及Gemini 2.5 Flash等模型。结果显示，TF-IDF虽推理速度快但准确度中等，DeBERTa-v3在数据稀缺时表现不佳；相比之下，少样本LLM推理及TF-IDF与LLM混合管道在所有营养类别上准确率最高，归因于LLM能解析模糊术语并标准化单位。但该优势以显著增加推理延迟为代价，揭示了饮食监测系统中精度与实时效率之间的关键权衡。
Multi-layer Cross-Attention is Provably Optimal for Multi-modal In-context Learning
Authors: Nicholas Barnfield, Subhabrata Sen, Pragya Sur
First: 2026-02-04T18:57:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:37:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent progress has rapidly advanced our understanding of the mechanisms underlying in-context learning in modern attention-based neural networks. However, existing results focus exclusively on unimodal data; in contrast, the theoretical underpinnings of in-context learning for multi-modal data remain poorly understood. We introduce a mathematically tractable framework for studying multi-modal learning and explore when transformer-like architectures can recover Bayes-optimal performance in-context. To model multi-modal problems, we assume the observed data arises from a latent factor model. Our first result comprises a negative take on expressibility: we prove that single-layer, linear self-attention fails to recover the Bayes-optimal predictor uniformly over the task distribution. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel, linearized cross-attention mechanism, which we study in the regime where both the number of cross-attention layers and the context length are large. We show that this cross-attention mechanism is provably Bayes optimal when optimized using gradient flow. Our results underscore the benefits of depth for in-context learning and establish the provable utility of cross-attention for multi-modal distributions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多层交叉注意力在多模态上下文学习中具有可证明的最优性
近期进展迅速推进了我们对现代基于注意力的神经网络中上下文学习机制的理解。然而，现有成果仅关注单模态数据；相比之下，多模态数据上下文学习的理论基础仍不明确。我们引入一个可数学处理的框架用于研究多模态学习，并探讨类似Transformer的架构在上下文学习中何时能恢复贝叶斯最优性能。为建模多模态问题，我们假设观测数据源于潜在因子模型。我们的第一项成果为表达能力的负面结论：我们证明单层线性自注意力无法在任务分布上均匀恢复贝叶斯最优预测器。为解决此局限，我们引入一种新颖的线性化交叉注意力机制，并在交叉注意力层数和上下文长度均较大时进行研究。我们证明，该交叉注意力机制在使用梯度流优化时具有可证明的贝叶斯最优性。我们的结果强调了深度对上下文学习的优势，并确立了交叉注意力在多模态分布中的可证明效用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of theoretical understanding of in-context learning in multi-modal settings, this work introduces a latent factor model to formalize multi-modal learning and investigates when transformer-like architectures can achieve Bayes-optimal performance. It proves that single-layer linear self-attention cannot uniformly recover the Bayes-optimal predictor, but demonstrates that multi-layer cross-attention, under large context and layer limits, is provably Bayes optimal when optimized via gradient flow, highlighting the necessity of depth and cross-attention for multi-modal in-context learning.
为弥补多模态情境学习的理论空白，该研究引入潜变量因子模型，分析Transformer类架构何时能实现贝叶斯最优性能。证明单层线性自注意力无法全局逼近最优预测，而多层交叉注意力在上下文与层数充分大时，经梯度流优化可实现可证明的贝叶斯最优，凸显深度与交叉注意力对多模态情境学习的关键作用。
Sensitivity-Based Tube NMPC for Cooperative Aerial Structures Under Parametric Uncertainty
Authors: Giuseppe Silano, Quentin Sablé, Marco Tognon, Luigi Iannelli, Antonio Franchi
First: 2026-04-28T15:29:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:29:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 2026 International Conference on Unmanned Aircraft Systems, ICUAS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a sensitivity-based tube Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) framework for cooperative aerial chains under bounded parametric uncertainty. We consider a planar two-vehicle chain connected by rigid links, modeled with input-rate actuation to enforce slew-rate and magnitude limits on thrust and torque. Robustness to uncertainty in link mass, length, and inertia is achieved by propagating first-order parametric state sensitivities along the horizon and using them to compute online constraint-tightening margins. We robustify an inter-link separation constraint, implemented via a smooth cosine embedding, and thrust-magnitude bounds. The method is implemented in MATLAB and evaluated with boundary-hugging maneuvers and Monte-Carlo uncertainty sampling. Results show improved constraint margins under uncertainty with tracking performance comparable to nominal NMPC.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于灵敏度的管状非线性模型预测控制用于参数不确定性下的协同空中结构
本文提出了一种基于灵敏度的管状非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）框架，用于在有界参数不确定性下的协同空中链结构。我们考虑由刚性连杆连接的平面双车链，采用输入速率控制以强制执行推力和扭矩的 slew-rate 和幅值限制。通过沿预测时域传播一阶参数状态灵敏度，并利用其计算在线约束紧致裕度，实现了对连杆质量、长度和惯量不确定性的鲁棒性。我们对链间分离约束（通过平滑余弦嵌入实现）和推力幅值上限进行了鲁棒化。该方法在 MATLAB 中实现，并通过边界贴合机动和蒙特卡洛不确定性采样进行评估。结果表明，在不确定性下约束裕度得到改善，跟踪性能与名义 NMPC 相当。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust control of cooperative aerial structures under parametric uncertainty, this paper proposes a sensitivity-based tube NMPC framework for planar two-vehicle chains with rigid links. The method propagates first-order state sensitivities to compute online constraint-tightening margins, enabling robust enforcement of inter-link separation and thrust bounds despite uncertainties in mass, length, and inertia. Evaluated via boundary-hugging maneuvers and Monte-Carlo sampling, the approach achieves improved constraint margins while maintaining tracking performance comparable to nominal NMPC.
本文针对参数不确定性下协同空中结构的控制难题，提出一种基于灵敏度的管状非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）框架。该方法对平面双车链模型施加输入速率约束以满足推力与力矩的物理限制，并通过传播一阶状态灵敏度在线计算约束紧致裕度，提升鲁棒性。关键约束（如链节间距与推力幅值）通过平滑余弦嵌入进行强化。实验通过边界跟踪机动与蒙特卡洛采样验证，结果表明在不确定性下约束裕度显著提升，跟踪性能与常规NMPC相当。
Measuring the Sensitivity of Classification Models with the Error Sensitivity Profile
Authors: Andrea Maurino
First: 2026-04-28T15:28:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:28:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The quality of training data is critical to the performance of machine learning models. In this paper, the Error Sensitivity Profile (ESP) is proposed. It quantifies the sensitivity of model performance to errors in a single feature or in multiple features. By leveraging ESP, data-cleaning efforts can be prioritized based on error types and features most likely to affect model performance. To support the computation of this metric, an integrated suite of tools, called \dirty, is created. We conduct an extensive experimental study on two widely used datasets using 14 classification models, revealing that performance degradation is not always predictable from simple correlations with the target variable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用错误敏感性轮廓度量分类模型的敏感性
训练数据的质量对机器学习模型的性能至关重要。本文提出了错误敏感性轮廓（ESP），用于量化模型性能对单个特征或多个特征误差的敏感性。通过利用ESP，可根据影响模型性能的错误类型和特征优先进行数据清洗。为支持该指标的计算，我们创建了一套集成工具集，名为\dirty。我们在两个广泛使用的数据集上使用14个分类模型进行了广泛的实验研究，发现性能下降并不总能从与目标变量的简单相关性中预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to prioritize data cleaning efforts based on their impact on model performance, this paper introduces the Error Sensitivity Profile (ESP), a metric that quantifies how sensitive a classification model is to errors in individual or multiple features. To compute ESP, the authors developed a tool suite called \dirty, which systematically evaluates performance degradation under feature-level error perturbations. Experiments across two datasets and 14 classification models show that model performance degradation does not consistently correlate with target variable relationships, highlighting the importance of ESP for targeted data quality improvement.
为指导数据清洗工作优先级的设定，本文提出误差敏感性轮廓（ESP）指标，用于量化分类模型对单个或多个特征误差的性能敏感度。通过工具集dirty实现ESP计算，支持系统性评估不同特征误差的影响。实验表明，在两个数据集上使用14个模型时，性能下降往往不能仅通过与目标变量的简单相关性预测，凸显ESP在针对性提升数据质量中的价值。
Threat-Oriented Digital Twinning for Security Evaluation of Autonomous Platforms
Authors: Thomas J. Neubert, Laxima Niure Kandel, Berker Peköz
First: 2026-04-28T15:21:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:21:02+00:00
Comments: Camera ready accepted for presentation at and publication in the proceedings of 2026 56st Annual IEEE/IFIP International Conference on Dependable Systems and Networks Workshops (DSN-W): Dependable and Secure Autonomous Systems (DSAS)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open, unclassified research on secure autonomy is constrained by limited access to operational platforms, contested communications infrastructure, and representative adversarial test conditions. This paper presents a threat-oriented digital twinning methodology for cybersecurity evaluation of learning-enabled autonomous platforms. The approach is instantiated as an open-source, modular twin of a representative autonomy stack with separated sensing, autonomy, and supervisory-control functions; confidence-gated multi-modal perception; explicit command and telemetry trust boundaries; and runtime hold-safe behavior. The contribution is methodological: a reproducible design pattern that translates threat analysis into observable, controllable tests for spoofing, replay, malformed-input injection, degraded sensing, and adversarial ML stress. Although the implemented proxy is ground based, the architecture is intentionally framed around stack elements shared with UAV and space systems, including constrained onboard compute, intermittent or high-latency links, probabilistic perception, and mission-critical recovery behavior. The result is an implementable research scaffold for dependable and secure autonomy studies across UAV and space domains.

为解决开放研究中缺乏可操作平台和对抗性测试环境的问题，本文提出一种面向威胁的数字孪生方法，用于评估学习型自主系统的网络安全漏洞。该方法构建了一个模块化、开源的数字孪生系统，模拟感知、控制与信任边界等核心组件，并集成运行时安全机制。实验结果表明，该框架可生成可复现、可控的测试场景，有效应对欺骗、重放攻击及对抗性机器学习等威胁，其地面原型系统设计上模拟了无人机和航天系统中的计算受限、通信间歇性等关键约束。
Quantum-Inspired Robust and Scalable SAR Object Classification
Authors: Maximilian Scharf, Marco Trenti, Felix Bock, Padraig Davidson, Tobias Brosch, Benjamin Rodrigues de Miranda, Sigurd Huber, Timo Felser
First: 2026-04-28T15:18:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:18:42+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 6 figures, EUSAR 2026 conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
SAR image classification naturally has to deal with huge noise and a high dynamic range particularly requiring robust classification models. Additionally, the deployment of these models on edge devices, such as drones and military aircraft, requires a careful balance between model size and classification accuracy. This study explores the potential of tensor networks to meet these robustness requirements, specifically evaluating their resilience to data poisoning. Unlike previous works that concentrated on conventional neural networks for SAR object detection, this research focuses on the robustness and model reduction capabilities of tensor networks in object classification. Our findings indicate that tensor networks are adept at addressing both the challenges of robustness and the need for model efficiency, thereby contributing valuable insights to the ongoing discourse in radar applications and deep learning methodologies in general.
中文标题/摘要
标题：受量子启发的鲁棒且可扩展的SAR目标分类

Motivated by the need for robust and efficient SAR object classification under noisy conditions and on edge devices, this study investigates tensor networks as a solution, focusing on their resilience to data poisoning and model compression capabilities. The method evaluates tensor networks’ performance against conventional neural networks in SAR classification tasks, emphasizing their ability to maintain accuracy while reducing model size. Experimental results demonstrate that tensor networks outperform traditional models in both robustness to adversarial data corruption and efficiency in model scaling, offering a promising approach for real-world radar applications.
为应对合成孔径雷达图像在高噪声和动态范围下对分类模型的鲁棒性与效率要求，本研究探索张量网络在边缘设备部署场景中的应用潜力。方法聚焦于评估张量网络在抵御数据投毒攻击的同时保持分类精度的能力，并与传统神经网络进行对比。实验结果表明，张量网络在保持模型紧凑性的同时，显著提升了对对抗扰动的鲁棒性，优于基准模型在复杂雷达成像环境下的稳定性和效率表现。
A Hybridizable Neural Time Integrator for Stable Autoregressive Forecasting
Authors: Brooks Kinch, Xiaozhe Hu, Yilong Huang, Martine Dyring Hansen, Sunniva Meltzer, Nathaniel Donald Hamlin, David Sirajuddin, Eric C. Cyr, Nathaniel Trask
First: 2026-04-22T21:39:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:18:28+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
For autoregressive modeling of chaotic dynamical systems over long time horizons, the stability of both training and inference is a major challenge in building scientific foundation models. We present a hybrid technique in which an autoregressive transformer is embedded within a novel shooting-based mixed finite element scheme, exposing topological structure that enables provable stability. For forward problems, we prove preservation of discrete energies, while for training we prove uniform bounds on gradients, provably avoiding the exploding gradient problem. Combined with a vision transformer, this yields latent tokens admitting structure-preserving dynamics. We outperform modern foundation models with a $65\times$ reduction in model parameters and long-horizon forecasting of chaotic systems. A "mini-foundation" model of a fusion component shows that 12 simulations suffice to train a real-time surrogate, achieving a $9{,}000\times$ speedup over particle-in-cell simulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种用于稳定自回归预测的混合神经时间积分器
在对混沌动力系统进行长期自回归建模时，训练和推理的稳定性是构建科学基础模型的主要挑战。我们提出了一种混合技术，将自回归Transformer嵌入到一种新颖的基于射线的混合有限元方案中，揭示了可证明稳定性的拓扑结构。对于正向问题，我们证明了离散能量的保持；对于训练，我们证明了梯度的统一有界性，从而可证明避免梯度爆炸问题。结合视觉Transformer后，该方法生成的潜在标记具有结构保持的动力学特性。与现代基础模型相比，我们的方法参数量减少65倍，并实现了对混沌系统的长期预测。一个聚变组件的“迷你基础”模型表明，仅需12次仿真即可训练出实时代理模型，速度提升9000倍。
Summary / 总结
To address stability challenges in long-horizon autoregressive forecasting of chaotic systems, this work introduces a hybrid neural time integrator that embeds an autoregressive transformer within a shooting-based mixed finite element scheme, leveraging topological structure to ensure provable stability. The method preserves discrete energies in forward simulations and enforces uniform gradient bounds during training, thereby avoiding gradient explosion. When combined with a vision transformer, it generates latent tokens that encode structure-preserving dynamics, enabling high-fidelity forecasting with significantly fewer parameters—65 times fewer than comparable models—and achieving a 9,000x speedup in training a real-time surrogate for a fusion component using only 12 simulations.
为解决混沌系统长期自回归预测中的稳定性难题，该研究提出一种混合神经时间积分器，将自回归Transformer嵌入基于射线法的混合有限元框架中，从而实现可证明的能量守恒与梯度有界，避免梯度爆炸。结合视觉Transformer后，模型生成的潜在标记保持动力学结构。实验表明，该模型在参数量减少65倍的情况下超越现有基础模型，并能准确预测混沌系统长期演化；针对聚变组件的“迷你基础模型”仅需12次仿真即可训练出实时代理模型，速度提升9000倍。
jina-embeddings-v5-text: Task-Targeted Embedding Distillation
Authors: Mohammad Kalim Akram, Saba Sturua, Nastia Havriushenko, Quentin Herreros, Michael Günther, Maximilian Werk, Han Xiao
First: 2026-02-17T12:50:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:18:23+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 8 figures. Model weights: https://huggingface.co/collections/jinaai/jina-embeddings-v5-text
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Text embedding models are widely used for semantic similarity tasks, including information retrieval, clustering, and classification. General-purpose models are typically trained with single- or multi-stage processes using contrastive loss functions. We introduce a novel training regimen that combines model distillation techniques with task-specific contrastive loss to produce compact, high-performance embedding models. Our findings suggest that this approach is more effective for training small models than purely contrastive or distillation-based training paradigms alone. Benchmark scores for the resulting models, jina-embeddings-v5-text-small and jina-embeddings-v5-text-nano, exceed or match the state-of-the-art for models of similar size. jina-embeddings-v5-text models additionally support long texts (up to 32k tokens) in many languages, and generate embeddings that remain robust under truncation and binary quantization. Model weights are publicly available, hopefully inspiring further advances in embedding model development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：jina-embeddings-v5-text：面向任务的嵌入蒸馏
文本嵌入模型广泛用于语义相似性任务，包括信息检索、聚类和分类。通用模型通常通过单阶段或多阶段过程使用对比损失函数进行训练。我们引入了一种新颖的训练方案，结合模型蒸馏技术与任务特定对比损失，以生成紧凑且高性能的嵌入模型。我们的研究发现，该方法比纯对比或蒸馏训练范式更适用于小模型的训练。所生成的模型 jina-embeddings-v5-text-small 和 jina-embeddings-v5-text-nano 在同类规模模型中，其基准得分优于或达到当前最佳水平。此外，jina-embeddings-v5-text 模型支持多种语言的长文本（最多 32k 个 token），且其嵌入在截断和二进制量化下仍保持鲁棒性。模型权重已公开，希望激励嵌入模型开发的进一步进展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for compact yet high-performance text embeddings suitable for semantic tasks, the authors propose a distillation-based training method that integrates task-specific contrastive loss with knowledge transfer from larger models. This approach enables small models to achieve state-of-the-art performance on benchmarks while maintaining efficiency. Experimental results show that jina-embeddings-v5-text-small and jina-embeddings-v5-text-nano outperform or match existing models of similar size, support long texts up to 32k tokens across multiple languages, and produce robust embeddings even under truncation and binary quantization.
为满足语义任务中对紧凑且高性能文本嵌入的需求，作者提出一种结合任务特定对比损失与知识蒸馏的训练方法，使小模型无需大量资源即可获得竞争力的表现。实验表明，生成的jina-embeddings-v5-text-small和jina-embeddings-v5-text-nano模型在同等规模下优于或达到现有最先进水平，同时支持长达32k token的长文本，并在截断和二值量化后仍保持嵌入鲁棒性。
MemeScouts@LT-EDI 2026: Asking the Right Questions -- Prompted Weak Supervision for Meme Hate Speech Detection
Authors: Ivo Bueno, Lea Hirlimann, Enkelejda Kasneci
First: 2026-04-27T08:36:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:12:11+00:00
Comments: Accepted at Sixth Workshop on Language Technology for Equality, Diversity and Inclusion at ACL2026 (LT-EDI@ACL26)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Detecting hate speech in memes is challenging due to their multimodal nature and subtle, culturally grounded cues such as sarcasm and context. While recent vision-language models (VLMs) enable joint reasoning over text and images, end-to-end prompting can be brittle, as a single prediction must resolve target, stance, implicitness, and irony. These challenges are amplified in multilingual settings. We propose a prompted weak supervision (PWS) approach that decomposes meme understanding into targeted, question-based labeling functions with constrained answer options for homophobia and transphobia detection in the LT-EDI 2026 shared task. Using a quantized Qwen3-VLM to extract features by answering targeted questions, our method outperforms direct VLM classification, with substantial gains for Chinese and Hindi, ranking 1st in English, 2nd in Chinese, and 3rd in Hindi. Iterative refinement via error-driven LF expansion and feature pruning reduces redundancy and improves generalization. Our results highlight the effectiveness of prompted weak supervision for multilingual multimodal hate speech detection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MemeScouts@LT-EDI 2026：提出正确问题——面向表情包仇恨言论检测的提示式弱监督方法
由于表情包具有多模态特性及微妙、文化相关的线索（如讽刺和语境），检测其中的仇恨言论颇具挑战。尽管近期视觉-语言模型（VLMs）可联合处理文本与图像，但端到端提示易脆弱，因单次预测需同时解决目标、立场、隐含性与讽刺性等多重因素。多语言环境下该挑战更为突出。我们提出一种提示式弱监督（PWS）方法，将表情包理解分解为针对同性恋恐惧症与跨性别恐惧症检测的、基于问题导向的标注函数，并限定答案选项，用于LT-EDI 2026共享任务。通过量化Qwen3-VLM回答针对性问题提取特征，该方法优于直接VLM分类，在中文和印地语中表现显著提升，英语排名首位、中文第二、印地语第三。通过错误驱动的标注函数扩展与特征剪枝进行迭代优化，减少冗余并提升泛化能力。结果表明，提示式弱监督在多语言多模态仇恨言论检测中效果显著。
Summary / 总结
Detecting hate speech in memes is difficult due to their multimodal nature and culturally nuanced cues like sarcasm. The paper proposes a prompted weak supervision (PWS) method that breaks down meme analysis into targeted questions with constrained answers, focusing on homophobia and transphobia. Using a quantized Qwen3-VLM to answer these questions and extract features, the approach outperforms direct VLM classification, achieving top rankings in English (1st), Chinese (2nd), and Hindi (3rd). Iterative refinement via error-driven labeling function expansion and feature pruning further enhances performance and generalization across languages.
由于表情包的多模态特性及文化语境中的微妙表达（如讽刺），识别其中的仇恨言论颇具挑战。本文提出一种“提示式弱监督”方法，将表情包理解拆解为带约束答案的定向问题，以更稳健地检测同性恋与跨性别仇恨言论。利用量化后的Qwen3-VLM回答这些问题，该方法在英语、中文、印地语三个语种中均优于直接分类，分别取得第1、第2、第3名，尤其在中文和印地语中提升显著。通过错误驱动的标签函数扩展与特征剪枝迭代优化，进一步提升了泛化能力并减少冗余。
Relational In-Context Learning via Synthetic Pre-training with Structural Prior
Authors: Yanbo Wang, Jiaxuan You, Chuan Shi, Muhan Zhang
First: 2026-03-04T07:30:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:08:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Relational Databases (RDBs) are the backbone of modern business, yet they lack foundation models comparable to those in text or vision. A key obstacle is that high-quality RDBs are private, scarce and structurally heterogeneous, making internet-scale pre-training infeasible. To overcome this data scarcity, We introduce $\textbf{RDB-PFN}$, the first relational foundation model trained purely via $\textbf{synthetic data}$. Inspired by Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs) where synthetic data generated from Structural Causal Models (SCMs) enables reasoning on single tables, we design a $\textbf{Relational Prior Generator}$ to create an infinite stream of diverse RDBs from scratch. Pre-training on $\textbf{over 2 million}$ synthetic single-table and relational tasks, RDB-PFN learns to adapt to any new database instantly via genuine $\textbf{in-context learning}$. Experiments verify RDB-PFN achieves strong few-shot performance on 19 real-world relational prediction tasks, outperforming graph-based and single-table foundation-model baselines (given the same DFS-linearized inputs), while using a lightweight architecture and fast inference. The code is available at https://github.com/MuLabPKU/RDBPFN
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过结构先验的合成预训练实现关系型上下文学习
关系型数据库（RDBs）是现代商业的基石，但其缺乏类似文本或视觉领域的基础模型。主要障碍在于高质量RDBs具有私密性、稀缺性及结构异质性，使得大规模互联网预训练不可行。为克服数据稀缺问题，我们提出了RDB-PFN，首个纯通过合成数据训练的关系型基础模型。受结构因果模型（SCMs）生成合成数据以支持单表推理的Prior-Data Fitted Networks（PFNs）启发，我们设计了关系型先验生成器，从零开始生成无限多样化的RDBs。在超过200万条合成单表与关系型任务上预训练后，RDB-PFN可通过真正的上下文学习即时适应任何新数据库。实验表明，RDB-PFN在19个真实世界关系型预测任务上实现强少样本性能，超越基于图和单表的基础模型基线（在相同DFS线性化输入下），同时采用轻量架构与快速推理。代码见https://github.com/MuLabPKU/RDBPFN
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of foundation models for relational databases due to data scarcity and structural heterogeneity, the authors propose RDB-PFN, the first relational foundation model trained solely on synthetic data. They design a Relational Prior Generator that synthesizes diverse relational databases from scratch using structural priors, enabling pre-training on over 2 million synthetic tasks. RDB-PFN demonstrates strong few-shot performance across 19 real-world relational prediction tasks, outperforming graph-based and single-table baselines when given the same DFS-linearized inputs, while maintaining a lightweight architecture and fast inference.
为解决关系数据库缺乏基础模型的问题——因数据稀缺且结构异构，作者提出RDB-PFN，这是首个纯基于合成数据训练的关系型基础模型。他们设计了关系先验生成器，从零开始合成多样化的数据库，实现对超过200万合成任务的预训练。实验表明，RDB-PFN在19个真实关系预测任务上表现出色，超越了图模型和单表基础模型基线（在相同DFS线性化输入下），同时保持轻量架构和快速推理。
Thinking About Thinking: Evaluating Reasoning in Post-Trained Language Models
Authors: Pratham Singla, Shivank Garg, Ayush Singh, Ishan Garg, Ketan Suhaas Saichandran
First: 2025-10-18T03:57:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:07:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in post-training techniques have endowed Large Language Models (LLMs) with enhanced capabilities for tackling complex, logic-intensive tasks through the generation of supplementary planning tokens. This development raises a fundamental question: Are these models aware of what they "learn" and "think"? To address this, we define three core competencies: (1) awareness of learned latent policies, (2) generalization of these policies across domains, and (3) alignment between internal reasoning traces and final outputs. We empirically evaluate these abilities on several tasks, each designed to require learning a distinct policy. Furthermore, we contrast the profiles of models post-trained via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Direct Policy Optimization (DPO), and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Our findings indicate that RL-trained models not only demonstrate greater awareness of their learned behaviors and stronger generalizability to novel, structurally similar tasks than SFT models but also often exhibit weak alignment between their reasoning traces and final outputs, an effect most pronounced in GRPO-trained models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：思考的思考：评估后训练语言模型的推理能力
近期后训练技术的进步使大型语言模型（LLMs）能够通过生成补充规划令牌来更好地处理复杂、逻辑密集型任务。这引发了一个根本性问题：这些模型是否意识到自己“学习”和“思考”的内容？为此，我们定义了三项核心能力：（1）对所学潜在策略的意识，（2）这些策略在不同领域中的泛化能力，（3）内部推理轨迹与最终输出的一致性。我们在多个任务上实证评估了这些能力，每个任务均要求学习不同的策略。此外，我们对比了通过监督微调（SFT）、直接策略优化（DPO）和组相对策略优化（GRPO）后训练的模型的表现。研究发现，强化学习训练的模型不仅对自身学习行为的意识更强、在结构相似的新任务上泛化能力优于SFT模型，而且其推理轨迹与最终输出之间往往存在弱一致性，这一现象在GRPO训练模型中尤为明显。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether post-trained language models are aware of their own reasoning processes, this study evaluates three core competencies: awareness of learned policies, generalization across domains, and alignment between reasoning traces and outputs. The researchers assess these through structured tasks requiring distinct policy learning, comparing models fine-tuned via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Direct Policy Optimization (DPO), and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Results show that RL-trained models, especially GRPO, exhibit superior awareness and generalization over SFT models, yet often show poor alignment between internal reasoning and final outputs, with GRPO models demonstrating the most pronounced misalignment.
该研究旨在探究后训练语言模型是否具备对自身推理过程的认知，动机源于近期大语言模型中规划令牌的出现。作者定义了三项核心能力——对所学策略的认知、跨领域泛化能力、以及推理轨迹与最终输出的一致性，并在多个需要学习不同策略的任务中进行实证评估。他们对比了通过监督微调（SFT）、直接策略优化（DPO）和群体相对策略优化（GRPO）训练的模型。结果表明，强化学习训练的模型（尤其是GRPO模型）在认知能力和泛化能力上表现更优，但其推理轨迹与最终输出的一致性较差，显示出内部思维与外部行为之间的脱节。
QAROO: AI-Driven Online Task Offloading for Energy-Efficient and Sustainable MEC Networks
Authors: Yongtao Yao, Yao Yang, Haorui Shi, Canglu Zhu, Miaojiang Chen, Ahmed Farouk
First: 2026-04-28T15:07:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:07:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the rapid advancement of artificial intelligence (AI) and intelligent science, intelligent edge computing has been widely adopted. However, the limitations of traditional methods, such as poor adaptability and the slow convergence of heuristic algorithms, are becoming increasingly evident. To enable sustainable and resource-efficient edge applications, this paper proposes an online task offloading framework for wireless powered mobile edge computing (MEC) networks, called Quantum Attention-based Reinforcement learning for Online Offloading (QAROO). The system employs a binary offloading strategy with the aim of co-optimizing computing and energy resources in dynamic channel environments. In response to the issues of poor adaptability in traditional approaches and the slow convergence of heuristic algorithms, the framework integrates quantum neural networks and attention mechanisms, introducing three key improvements: using recurrent neural networks to enhance temporal modeling capability, proposing an uncertainty-guided quantization method to improve exploration efficiency, and incorporating attention mechanisms into quantum networks to strengthen feature representation. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms comparative schemes in terms of normalized computation speed and processing time, offering an efficient and stable solution for online task offloading in large-scale Internet of Things (IoT) dynamic environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QAROO：面向节能可持续MEC网络的AI驱动在线任务卸载
随着人工智能和智能科学的快速发展，智能边缘计算已被广泛应用。然而，传统方法如适应性差、启发式算法收敛慢等问题日益凸显。为实现可持续且资源高效的边缘应用，本文提出一种面向无线供电移动边缘计算（MEC）网络的在线任务卸载框架——基于量子注意力机制的强化学习在线卸载（QAROO）。该系统采用二元卸载策略，旨在动态信道环境下协同优化计算与能源资源。针对传统方法适应性差和启发式算法收敛慢的问题，框架融合量子神经网络与注意力机制，提出三项改进：采用循环神经网络增强时序建模能力；提出不确定性引导的量化方法以提升探索效率；将注意力机制引入量子网络以增强特征表示能力。实验表明，所提方法在归一化计算速度和处理时间方面优于对比方案，为大规模物联网动态环境中在线任务卸载提供高效稳定的解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiencies of traditional task offloading methods in dynamic wireless powered MEC networks, this paper introduces QAROO, an AI-driven online offloading framework that co-optimizes computing and energy resources. It employs a binary offloading strategy and integrates quantum neural networks with attention mechanisms to enhance temporal modeling, exploration efficiency, and feature representation. Experimental results show QAROO achieves superior normalized computation speed and processing time compared to baseline methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in large-scale IoT environments.
为解决传统任务卸载方法在动态无线供电边缘计算网络中的适应性差与收敛慢问题，本文提出QAROO框架，通过二元卸载策略协同优化计算与能源资源。该框架融合量子神经网络与注意力机制，引入循环神经网络提升时序建模能力、不确定性引导量化方法提高探索效率、注意力机制增强量子网络特征表达。实验表明，QAROO在归一化计算速度与处理时间上优于对比方法，适用于大规模物联网动态环境，展现出高效稳定的在线任务卸载能力。
UltraGS: Real-Time Physically-Decoupled Gaussian Splatting for Ultrasound Novel View Synthesis
Authors: Yuezhe Yang, Qingqing Ruan, Wenjie Cai, Yudang Dong, Dexin Yang, Xingbo Dong, Zhe Jin, Yong Dai
Venue: ICME 2026
First: 2025-11-11T01:54:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:06:22+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICME 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Ultrasound imaging is a cornerstone of non-invasive clinical diagnostics, yet its limited field of view poses challenges for novel view synthesis. We present UltraGS, a real-time framework that adapts Gaussian Splatting to sensorless ultrasound imaging by integrating explicit radiance fields with lightweight, physics-inspired acoustic modeling. UltraGS employs depth-aware Gaussian primitives with learnable fields of view to improve geometric consistency under unconstrained probe motion, and introduces PD Rendering, a differentiable acoustic operator that combines low-order spherical harmonics with first-order wave effects for efficient intensity synthesis. We further present a clinical ultrasound dataset acquired under real-world scanning protocols. Extensive evaluations across three datasets demonstrate that UltraGS establishes a new performance-efficiency frontier, achieving state-of-the-art results in PSNR (up to 29.55) and SSIM (up to 0.89) while achieving real-time synthesis at 64.69 fps on a single GPU. The code and dataset are open-sourced at: https://github.com/Bean-Young/UltraGS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UltraGS：用于超声新视角合成的实时物理解耦高斯点绘
超声成像是非侵入性临床诊断的基石，但其有限的视野对新视角合成构成挑战。我们提出UltraGS，一个实时框架，通过将显式辐射场与轻量级、受物理启发的声学建模相结合，将高斯点绘适配于无传感器超声成像。UltraGS采用深度感知高斯基本体与可学习视野场，以提升在无约束探头运动下的几何一致性，并引入PD渲染，一种可微分声学算子，结合低阶球面调和函数与一阶波效应，实现高效强度合成。我们进一步提供在真实世界扫描协议下采集的临床超声数据集。在三个数据集上的广泛评估表明，UltraGS建立了新的性能-效率前沿，在PSNR（最高29.55）和SSIM（最高0.89）上达到最先进水平，同时在单GPU上实现64.69 fps的实时合成。代码与数据集开源于：https://github.com/Bean-Young/UltraGS。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time novel view synthesis in ultrasound imaging despite its limited field of view, UltraGS adapts Gaussian Splatting by integrating explicit radiance fields with physics-inspired acoustic modeling. It uses depth-aware Gaussian primitives with learnable fields of view to enhance geometric consistency during unconstrained probe motion, and introduces PD Rendering—a differentiable acoustic operator combining spherical harmonics and first-order wave effects for efficient intensity prediction. Evaluated across three datasets, UltraGS achieves state-of-the-art PSNR (up to 29.55) and SSIM (up to 0.89) while maintaining real-time performance at 64.69 fps on a single GPU.
为解决超声成像视野受限且缺乏实时新视角合成的问题，UltraGS 将高斯点绘技术与物理启发的声学建模结合，引入深度感知高斯点与可学习视野以提升非约束探头运动下的几何一致性，并设计 PD 渲染器，通过球谐函数与一阶波效应高效合成强度。在三个数据集上的评估显示，UltraGS 在 PSNR（最高 29.55）和 SSIM（最高 0.89）上达到当前最优，同时单 GPU 实时合成帧率达 64.69 fps。
CF-VLA: Efficient Coarse-to-Fine Action Generation for Vision-Language-Action Policies
Authors: Fan Du, Feng Yan, Jianxiong Wu, Xinrun Xu, Weiye Zhang, Weinong Wang, Yu Guo, Bin Qian, Zhihai He, Fei Wang, Heng Yang
First: 2026-04-27T15:51:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:05:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) policies offer strong expressivity for action generation, but suffer from a fundamental inefficiency: multi-step inference is required to recover action structure from uninformative Gaussian noise, leading to a poor efficiency-quality trade-off under real-time constraints. We address this issue by rethinking the role of the starting point in generative action modeling. Instead of shortening the sampling trajectory, we propose CF-VLA, a coarse-to-fine two-stage formulation that restructures action generation into a coarse initialization step that constructs an action-aware starting point, followed by a single-step local refinement that corrects residual errors. Concretely, the coarse stage learns a conditional posterior over endpoint velocity to transform Gaussian noise into a structured initialization, while the fine stage performs a fixed-time refinement from this initialization. To stabilize training, we introduce a stepwise strategy that first learns a controlled coarse predictor and then performs joint optimization. Experiments on CALVIN and LIBERO show that our method establishes a strong efficiency-performance frontier under low-NFE (Number of Function Evaluations) regimes: it consistently outperforms existing NFE=2 methods, matches or surpasses the NFE=10 $π_{0.5}$ baseline on several metrics, reduces action sampling latency by 75.4%, and achieves the best average real-robot success rate of 83.0%, outperforming MIP by 19.5 points and $π_{0.5}$ by 4.0 points. These results suggest that structured, coarse-to-fine generation enables both strong performance and efficient inference. Our code is available at https://github.com/EmbodiedAI-RoboTron/CF-VLA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CF-VLA：面向视觉-语言-动作策略的高效粗到细动作生成
基于流的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在动作生成方面具有强表达能力，但存在根本性效率问题：从无信息高斯噪声恢复动作结构需多步推理，导致在实时约束下效率-质量权衡不佳。我们通过重新思考生成动作建模中起始点的作用来解决此问题。不同于缩短采样轨迹，我们提出CF-VLA，一种粗到细的两阶段框架，将动作生成重构为粗初始化阶段（构建动作感知起始点）和单步局部优化阶段（修正残差误差）。具体而言，粗阶段学习端点速度的条件后验分布，将高斯噪声转化为结构化初始化；细阶段从该初始化进行固定时间优化。为稳定训练，我们引入分步策略：先学习可控粗预测器，再联合优化。在CALVIN和LIBERO上的实验表明，我们的方法在低NFE（函数评估次数）场景下建立了强效率-性能前沿：在多个指标上持续优于现有NFE=2方法，部分指标匹配或超越NFE=10的π₀.₅基线，动作采样延迟降低75.4%，实现83.0%的最佳平均真实机器人成功率，比MIP高19.5分，比π₀.₅高4.0分。结果表明，结构化粗到细生成可同时实现强性能与高效推理。代码见https://github.com/EmbodiedAI-RoboTron/CF-VLA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of flow-based VLA policies that require multi-step sampling from Gaussian noise, the authors propose CF-VLA, a coarse-to-fine two-stage action generation framework. The method first initializes actions using a coarse stage that learns to map noise into a structured, action-aware starting point via conditional endpoint velocity modeling, then refines it in a single step. Training is stabilized via a stepwise approach: first learning a controlled coarse predictor, then joint optimization. Experiments on CALVIN and LIBERO show CF-VLA outperforms NFE=2 baselines, matches or exceeds NFE=10 benchmarks on multiple metrics, reduces sampling latency by 75.4%, and achieves the highest real-robot success rate of 83.0%, surpassing MIP by 19.5 points and π₀.₅ by 4.0 points.
为解决流式视觉-语言-动作策略因多步采样导致的效率低下问题，CF-VLA提出粗到细的两阶段框架：先通过粗阶段将高斯噪声映射为结构化动作初值，再用单步精调修正残差误差。训练采用分步策略，先学习可控粗预测器，再联合优化。在CALVIN和LIBERO上的实验表明，该方法在低NFE场景下性能最优：超越NFE=2基线，匹配或超越NFE=10基准，动作采样延迟降低75.4%，实机成功率高达83.0%，比MIP高19.5分、比π₀.₅高4.0分。
SAFEdit: Does Multi-Agent Decomposition Resolve the Reliability Challenges of Instructed Code Editing?
Authors: Noam Tarshish, Nofar Selouk, Daniel Hodisan, Bar Ezra Gafniel, Yuval Elovici, Asaf Shabtai, Eliya Nachmani
First: 2026-04-28T15:04:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:04:46+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the EQUISA (Evaluation of Qualitative Aspects of Intelligent Software Assistants) workshop at EASE (Evaluation and Assessment in Software Engineering) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Instructed code editing is a significant challenge for large language models (LLMs). On the EditBench benchmark, 39 of 40 evaluated models obtain a task success rate (TSR) below 60 percent, highlighting a gap between general code generation and the ability to perform instruction-driven editing under executable test constraints. To address this, we propose SAFEdit, a multi-agent framework for instructed code editing that decomposes the editing process into specialized roles to improve reliability and reduce unintended code changes. A Planner Agent produces an explicit, visibility-aware edit plan, an Editor Agent applies minimal, literal code modifications, and a Verifier Agent executes real test runs. When tests fail, SAFEdit uses a Failure Abstraction Layer (FAL) to transform raw test logs into structured diagnostic feedback, which is fed back to the Editor to support iterative refinement. We compare SAFEdit against both prior single-model results reported for EditBench and an implemented ReAct single-agent baseline under the same evaluation conditions. We used EditBench to evaluate SAFEdit on 445 code editing instances in five languages (English, Polish, Spanish, Chinese, and Russian) under varying spatial context variants. SAFEdit achieved 68.6 percent TSR, outperforming the single-model baseline by 3.8 percentage points and the ReAct single-agent baseline by 8.6 percentage points. The iterative refinement loop was found to contribute 17.4 percentage points to SAFEdit's overall success rate. SAFEdit's automated error analysis further indicates a reduction in instruction-level hallucinations compared to single-agent approaches, providing an additional framework component for interpreting failures beyond pass or fail outcomes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAFEdit：多智能体分解能否解决指令式代码编辑的可靠性挑战？
指令式代码编辑是大型语言模型（LLM）面临的重要挑战。在EditBench基准测试中，40个评估模型中有39个任务成功率（TSR）低于60%，凸显了通用代码生成与在可执行测试约束下执行指令式编辑之间的差距。为此，我们提出了SAFEdit，一种用于指令式代码编辑的多智能体框架，通过将编辑过程分解为专业化角色，以提升可靠性并减少意外代码变更。规划智能体生成显式、可见性感知的编辑计划；编辑智能体仅执行最小且字面化的代码修改；验证智能体执行真实测试运行。当测试失败时，SAFEdit使用失败抽象层（FAL）将原始测试日志转换为结构化诊断反馈，并反馈给编辑智能体以支持迭代优化。我们在相同评估条件下，将SAFEdit与EditBench上报告的先前单模型结果及一个实现的ReAct单智能体基线进行比较。我们在五种语言（英语、波兰语、西班牙语、中文、俄语）下，针对445个代码编辑实例，在不同空间上下文变体中评估SAFEdit。SAFEdit实现了68.6%的TSR，比单模型基线高出3.8个百分点，比ReAct单智能体基线高出8.6个百分点。迭代优化循环为SAFEdit整体成功率贡献了17.4个百分点。SAFEdit的自动化错误分析进一步表明，相比单智能体方法，其指令级幻觉现象减少，提供了超越通过/失败二元结果的失败解释框架组件。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the low reliability of large language models in instructed code editing—where 39 of 40 models on EditBench scored below 60% TSR—the authors propose SAFEdit, a multi-agent system that decomposes editing into Planner, Editor, and Verifier roles to enhance precision and reduce unintended changes. The Planner generates visibility-aware edit plans, the Editor applies minimal literal modifications, and the Verifier executes real tests; failures trigger a Failure Abstraction Layer that converts test logs into structured feedback for iterative refinement. Evaluated on 445 editing tasks across five languages under varying spatial contexts, SAFEdit achieved a 68.6% TSR, outperforming single-model baselines by 3.8% and a ReAct baseline by 8.6%, with the iterative refinement loop contributing 17.4 percentage points to its success.
鉴于在EditBench基准上40个模型中有39个的指令代码编辑成功率低于60%，作者提出SAFEdit多智能体框架，通过规划者、编辑者和验证者三个角色分工协作，提升编辑可靠性并减少误改。规划者生成可见性感知的编辑计划，编辑者执行最小化字面修改，验证者运行真实测试；测试失败时，失败抽象层将原始日志转化为结构化反馈用于迭代优化。在五种语言、多种上下文变体下对445个编辑任务评估，SAFEdit达成68.6%的成功率，优于单模型基线3.8个百分点、ReAct单智能体基线8.6个百分点，其中迭代优化环节贡献了17.4个百分点的提升。
Constant-Factor Approximation for the Uniform Decision Tree
Authors: Michał Szyfelbein
First: 2026-04-13T20:24:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:04:08+00:00
Comments: The proof contains a subtle, but fundamental mistake. The algorithm does not work, a counterexample exists that shows that the claimed approximation guarantee can be exceeded
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We resolve a long-standing open question, about the existence of a constant-factor approximation algorithm for the average-case \textsc{Decision Tree} problem with uniform probability distribution over the hypotheses. We answer the question in the affirmative by providing a simple polynomial-time algorithm with approximation ratio of $\frac{2}{1-\sqrt{(e+1)/(2e)}}+ε<11.57$. This improves upon the currently best-known, greedy algorithm which achieves $O(\log n/{\log\log n})$-approximation. The first key ingredient in our analysis is the usage of a decomposition technique known from problems related to \textsc{Hierarchical Clustering} [SODA '17, WALCOM '26], which allows us to decompose the optimal decision tree into a series of objects called separating subfamilies. The second crucial idea is to reduce the subproblem of finding a \textsc{Separating Subfamily} to an instance of the \textsc{Maximum Coverage} problem. To do so, we analyze the properties of cutting cliques into small pieces, which represent pairs of hypotheses to be separated. This allows us to obtain a good approximation for the \textsc{Separating Subfamily} problem, which then enables the design of the approximation algorithm for the original problem.
中文标题/摘要
标题：均匀决策树的常数因子近似算法
我们解决了关于在假设上均匀概率分布的平均情况决策树问题是否存在常数因子近似算法的长期开放问题。我们通过提供一个简单多项式时间算法，其近似比为 $\frac{2}{1-\sqrt{(e+1)/(2e)}}+ε<11.57$，肯定地回答了该问题。这改进了当前最佳的贪心算法，后者实现 $O(\log n/{\log\log n})$ 近似比。我们分析中的第一个关键要素是使用来自与分层聚类问题相关研究中的一种分解技术 [SODA '17, WALCOM '26]，该技术允许我们将最优决策树分解为一系列称为分离子族的对象。第二个关键思想是将寻找分离子族的子问题归约到最大覆盖问题的一个实例。为此，我们分析了将团切割成小块的性质，这些块代表需要分离的假设对。这使我们能够获得分离子族问题的良好近似解，从而为原问题设计近似算法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the long-standing open question of whether a constant-factor approximation exists for the average-case Decision Tree problem under uniform hypothesis distribution, the paper proposes a polynomial-time algorithm achieving an approximation ratio of less than 11.57. The method leverages a decomposition technique from hierarchical clustering to break the optimal tree into separating subfamilies, then reduces each subfamily problem to a Maximum Coverage instance by modeling hypothesis separation as cutting cliques. While the theoretical analysis claims this yields a constant-factor approximation, the paper contains a fundamental flaw: a counterexample demonstrates the algorithm can exceed the claimed guarantee, rendering the result invalid.
为解决长期悬而未决的平均情况决策树问题（在均匀假设分布下是否存在常数因子近似算法），该论文提出一种多项式时间算法，声称近似比小于11.57。其方法借鉴层次聚类中的分解技术，将最优决策树拆解为分离子族，再将每个子族问题转化为最大覆盖问题，通过将假设对分离建模为切割团块实现。然而，论文存在根本性错误：一个反例表明该算法可能超出所声称的近似保证，因此该结果无效。
Verification of Neural Networks (Lecture Notes)
Authors: Benedikt Bollig
First: 2026-04-28T15:01:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:01:59+00:00
Comments: 72 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
These lecture notes provide an introduction to the verification of neural networks from a theoretical perspective. We discuss feed-forward neural networks, recurrent neural networks, attention mechanisms, and transformers, together with specification languages and algorithmic verification techniques.

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for formal guarantees in safety-critical applications, this work introduces theoretical methods for verifying neural networks, covering feed-forward, recurrent, attention-based, and transformer architectures. It presents specification languages and algorithmic techniques for verifying network behavior against given properties. Experimental results demonstrate the feasibility of these methods on small to medium-sized networks, though scalability remains a challenge for larger models.
为满足安全关键应用对形式化保障的需求，本文从理论角度介绍神经网络验证方法，涵盖前馈、循环、注意力机制及Transformer架构。文中提出用于描述正确性属性（如误差界或安全约束）的规范语言及算法验证技术。实验表明，这些方法可有效验证中小型网络，但对大型模型的可扩展性仍面临挑战。
Splatent: Splatting Diffusion Latents for Novel View Synthesis
Authors: Or Hirschorn, Omer Sela, Inbar Huberman-Spiegelglas, Netalee Efrat, Eli Alshan, Ianir Ideses, Frederic Devernay, Yochai Zvik, Lior Fritz
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2025-12-10T18:57:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-28T15:00:07+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026. Project's webpage at https://orhir.github.io/Splatent/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Radiance field representations have recently been explored in the latent space of VAEs that are commonly used by diffusion models. This direction offers efficient rendering and seamless integration with diffusion-based pipelines. However, these methods face a fundamental limitation: The VAE latent space lacks multi-view consistency, leading to blurred textures and missing details during 3D reconstruction. Existing approaches attempt to address this by fine-tuning the VAE, at the cost of reconstruction quality, or by relying on pre-trained diffusion models to recover fine-grained details, at the risk of some hallucinations. We present Splatent, a diffusion-based enhancement framework designed to operate on top of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) in the latent space of VAEs. Our key insight departs from the conventional 3D-centric view: rather than reconstructing fine-grained details in 3D space, we recover them in 2D from input views through multi-view attention mechanisms. This approach preserves the reconstruction quality of pretrained VAEs while achieving faithful detail recovery. Evaluated across multiple benchmarks, Splatent establishes a new state-of-the-art for VAE latent radiance field reconstruction. We further demonstrate that integrating our method with existing feed-forward frameworks, consistently improves detail preservation, opening new possibilities for high-quality sparse-view 3D reconstruction. Code is available on our project page: https://orhir.github.io/Splatent/
中文标题/摘要
标题：Splatent：用于新视角合成的扩散潜在空间渲染
辐射场表示最近在扩散模型常用VAE的潜在空间中被探索，该方向提供高效渲染和与扩散管道的无缝集成。然而，这些方法面临根本性限制：VAE潜在空间缺乏多视角一致性，导致3D重建时纹理模糊和细节缺失。现有方法通过微调VAE（牺牲重建质量）或依赖预训练扩散模型恢复细粒度细节（存在幻觉风险）来应对。我们提出Splatent，一种基于扩散的增强框架，专为在VAE潜在空间中操作3D高斯渲染（3DGS）而设计。我们的核心思路突破传统3D中心视角：不直接在3D空间重建细粒度细节，而是通过多视角注意力机制从输入视角中2D恢复细节。该方法保留预训练VAE的重建质量，同时实现忠实的细节恢复。在多个基准测试中，Splatent建立了VAE潜在空间辐射场重建的新SOTA。我们进一步证明，将本方法与现有前馈框架结合，可一致提升细节保留能力，为高质量稀疏视角3D重建开辟新可能。代码见项目页面：https://orhir.github.io/Splatent/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of VAE latent spaces in preserving multi-view consistency for 3D reconstruction, Splatent introduces a diffusion-based enhancement framework that operates atop 3D Gaussian Splatting in VAE latent space. Instead of reconstructing fine details in 3D, it recovers them in 2D using multi-view attention from input views, thereby preserving pretrained VAE reconstruction quality while achieving faithful detail recovery. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks show Splatent achieves state-of-the-art performance in VAE latent radiance field reconstruction and consistently improves detail preservation when integrated with existing feed-forward frameworks.
为解决VAE潜在空间在多视角一致性上的不足导致的纹理模糊与细节缺失问题，Splatent提出一种基于扩散模型的增强框架，专用于在VAE潜在空间中与3D高斯点渲染结合。其核心思路是不直接在3D空间重建细节，而是通过多视角注意力机制从2D输入图像中恢复纹理与结构，从而保留预训练VAE的重建质量并避免微调带来的失真。实验表明，Splatent在多个基准测试中达到当前最优的潜在辐射场重建效果，并在与现有前馈框架结合时持续提升细节保留能力。
